Their numbers were eight thousand five hundred and eighty.


###### Onkelos Numbers 4:49

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/4:49](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/4:49)


עַל מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ מְנָא יָתְהוֹן בִּידָא דְמשֶׁה גְּבַר גְּבַר עַל פָּלְחָנֵיהּ וְעַל מַטּוּלֵיהּ וּמִנְיָנוֹהִי דִּי פַקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה:


According to the word of Adonoy he counted them, by the hand of Moshe, each man according to his service and according to what he carries; and they were counted, as Adonoy commanded Moshe.



## Chapter 5

###### Onkelos Numbers 5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:1)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying;


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:2)


פַּקֵּיד יָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וִישַׁלְּחוּן מִן מַשְׁרִיתָא כָּל דִּסְגִיר וְכָל דְּדָאֵב וְכֹל דִּמְסָאָב **לִטְמֵי** נַפְשָׁא **דֶאֱנָשָׁא**:


Command Bnei Yisroel that they must expel from the camp. anyone with *tzora’as,* and anyone from whom flows a [seminal] discharge and anyone who became defiled by a (departed) [**the bones of the**] soul [**of a person**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:3)


מִדְּכַר עַד נוּקְבָא תְּשַׁלְּחוּן לְמִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא תְּשַׁלְּחֻנּוּן וְלָא יְסַאֲבוּן יָת מַשְׁרִיתְהוֹן דִּי **שְׁכִנְתִּי** שָׁרְיָא בֵינֵיהוֹן:


Whether male or female you shall send them out. Beyond the camp you shall send them, and they shall not defile their camps in which I dwell [**My Shechina dwells**] among them.


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:4)


וַעֲבָדוּ כֵן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְשַׁלְּחוּ יָתְהוֹן לְמִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא כְּמָא דִּי מַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה כֵּן עֲבָדוּ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


Bnei Yisroel did so, and they sent them beyond the camp; just as Adonoy spoke to Moshe, so did Bnei Yisroel do.


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:5)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying;


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:6)


מַלֵּל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל גְּבַר אוֹ אִתְּתָא אֲרֵי יַעְבְּדוּן מִכָּל חוֹבֵי אֱנָשָׁא **לְשַׁקָּרָא שְׁקָר קֳדָם** יְיָ וְיֵחוֹב אֱנָשָׁא הַהוּא:


Speak to Bnei Yisroel: When a man or a woman commits any of the sins against man, acting treacherously [**untruthfully before**] against Adonoy, and that person is guilty.


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:7)


וִיוַדּוּן יָת חוֹבֵיהוֹן דִּי עֲבָדוּ וְיָתֵב יָת חוֹבְתֵיהּ בְּרֵישֵׁיהּ וְחֻמְשֵׁיהּ יוֹסֵף עֲלוֹהִי וְיִתֵּן לִדְחָב לֵיהּ:


They shall confess the sins which they committed and return the principal amount [of the object] of his guilt, and add one fifth to that amount. He shall then give it to whom he is indebted.


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:8)


וְאִם לֵית לִגְבַר פָּרִיק לַאֲתָבָא חוֹבְתָא לֵיהּ חוֹבְתָא דִּמְתֵב קֳדָם יְיָ לְכַהֲנָא בַּר מִדְּכַר כִּפּוּרַיָּא דִּי יְכַפֵּר בֵּיהּ עֲלוֹהִי:


But if the man has no redeemer (relative) to whom the debt may be returned, the debt being returned then belongs to Adonoy, [then] to the *kohein.* This is besides the ram of forgiveness with which he [his sin] will be atoned.


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:9)


וְכָל אַפְרָשׁוּתָא לְכָל קוּדְשַׁיָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל דִּי יְקָרְבוּן לְכַהֲנָא דִּלֵּיהּ יְהֵא:


Every *terumah*-gift of all the sacred things of Bnei Yisroel that they will bring to the *kohein,* shall be his.


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:10)


וּגְבַר יָת **מַעֲשַׂר** קוּדְשֵׁיהּ דִּלֵּיהּ יְהוֹן גְּבַר דִּיִתֵּן לְכַהֲנָא דִּלֵּיהּ יְהֵא:


A [**tenth of**] man’s sacred objects shall be his; whatever a man gives to the *kohein,* shall be his.


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:11)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying;


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:12)


מַלֵּל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן גְּבַר גְּבַר אֲרֵי תִסְטֵי אִתְּתֵיהּ **וּתְשַׁקֵּר בֵּיהּ שְׁקָר**:


Speak to Bnei Yisroel and say to them: If any man’s wife goes astray and acts treacherously [**untruthfully**] toward him.


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:13)


וְיִשְׁכֻּב גְּבַר יָתַהּ שָׁכְבַת זַרְעָא וִיהֵי **מְכַסָּא** מֵעֵינֵי בַעְלָהּ וּמִטַּמְּרָא וְהִיא מְסָאָבָא וְסָהִיד לֵית בַּהּ וְהִיא לָא אִתַּחָדָת:


And a man has lain with her conjugally but it was hidden [**covered**] from her husband’s eyes and she was concealed and has become defiled and there is no witness against her, and she was not seized forcibly.


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:14)


וְיֶעְבַּר עֲלוֹהִי רוּחַ קִנְאָה וִיקַנֵּי יָת אִתְּתֵיהּ וְהִיא מְסָאָבָא אוֹ עֲבַר עֲלוֹהִי רוּחַ קִנְאָה וִיקַנֵּי יָת אִתְּתֵיהּ וְהִיא לָא מְסָאָבָא:


[When] a spirit of jealousy comes upon him and he is jealous of his wife and she was defiled or, a spirit of jealousy comes upon him and he is jealous of his wife and she was not defiled,


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:15)


וְיַיְתִי גַבְרָא יָת אִתְּתֵיהּ לְוָת כַּהֲנָא וְיַיְתִי יָת קֻרְבָּנַהּ עֲלַהּ חַד מִן עַסְרָא **בִּתְלַת סְאִין** קִמְחָא שְׂעָרִין לָא יְרִיק עֲלוֹהִי מִשְׁחָא וְלָא יִתֵּן עֲלוֹהִי לְבֻנְתָּא אֲרֵי מִנְחַת קִנְאָתָא הוּא מִנְחַת דּוּכְרָנָא מַדְכָּרַת חוֹבָא:


that man shall bring his wife to the *kohein* and he shall bring her offering for her; [which is] one tenth of an *epha* [**three se’in**] of barley flour; he shall not pour oil over it and he shall not put frankincense on it, for it is a meal-offering of jealousy, a meal-offering of remembrance, recalling an iniquity.


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:16)


וִיקָרֵב יָתַהּ כַּהֲנָא וִיקִימִנַּהּ קֳדָם יְיָ:


The *kohein* shall bring her near and station her before Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:17)


וְיִסַּב כַּהֲנָא **מֵי כִיּוֹר** בְּמַן דַּחֲסָף וּמִן עַפְרָא דִּי יְהֵי **בִּיסוֹדֵי** מַשְׁכְּנָא יִסַּב כַּהֲנָא וְיִתֵּן לְמַיָּא:


The *kohein* shall take sanctified waters [**water from the copper wash basin**] in a clay utensil, and of the earth that will be on the floor [**base**] of the *Mishkon*, the *kohein* shall take and put into the water.


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:18)


וִיקִים כַּהֲנָא יָת אִתְּתָא קֳדָם יְיָ וְיִפְרַע יָת רֵישָׁא דְאִתְּתָא וִיהֵב עַל **יְדָהָא** יָת מִנְחַת דּוּכְרָנָא מִנְחַת קִנְאָתָא הִיא וּבִידָא דְכַהֲנָא יְהוֹן מַיָּא מְרִירַיָּא מְלַטְטַיָּא:


The *kohein* shall station the woman before Adonoy, and expose the woman’s hair, and place on her palms [**hands**] the meal-offering of remembrance; it is a meal-offering of jealousy and there will be in the *kohein’s* hand, the bitter, lethal waters.


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:19)


וְיוֹמֵי יָתַהּ כַּהֲנָא וְיֵימַר לְאִתְּתָא אִם לָא שְׁכִיב גְּבַר יָתִיךְ וְאִם לָא סְטִית **לְאִסְתָּאָבָא בַּר** מִבַּעֲלִיךְ הֱוֵי זַכָּאָה מִמַּיָּא מְרִירַיָּא מְלַטְטַיָּא הָאִלֵּין:


The *kohein* shall place her under oath and say to the woman: If a man has not lain with you [conjugally] and if you have not gone astray to be defiled while you were married to [**by someone other than**] your husband, you shall be absolved of these bitter, lethal waters.


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:20)


וְאַתְּ אֲרֵי סְטִית **בַּר** מִבַּעֲלִיךְ וַאֲרֵי אִסְתָּאָבְתְּ וִיהַב גְּבַר בִּיךְ יָת שְׁכֻבְתֵּיהּ בַּר מִבַּעֲלִיךְ:


[But] you, if you have gone astray while you were married to your husband, and if you have been defiled, and if a man has lain conjugally with you, [a man] other than your husband.


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:21)


וְיוֹמֵי כַהֲנָא יָת אִתְּתָא בְּמוֹמָתָא דִלְוָטָא וְיֵימַר כַּהֲנָא לְאִתְּתָא יְהֵב יְיָ יָתִיךְ לִלְוַט וּלְמוֹמֵי בְּגוֹ עַמִּיךְ בְּדִיהֵב יְיָ יָת יַרְכִּיךְ **מַסְיָא** וְיָת מְעַיְכִי **נְפִיחִין**:


The *kohein* shall place the woman under oath with the oath of accursedness, and the *kohein* shall say to the woman: “Adonoy shall make you a curse and an oath among your people, when Adonoy causes your thigh to collapse [**fall away**] and your abdomen to swell.


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:22)


וְיֵעֲלוּן מַיָּא מְלַטְטַיָּא הָאִלֵּין בִּמְעַיְכִי **לַאֲפָחָא** מְעִין **וּלְאַמְסָאָה** יַרְכָּא וְתֵימַר אִתְּתָא אָמֵן אָמֵן:


This bitter, lethal water will enter your abdomen to swell [**inflate**] the abdomen, and collapse [**fall away**] the thigh and the woman shall say “Amein, Amein”.


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:23)


וְיִכְתּוֹב יָת לְוָטַיָּא הָאִלֵּין כַּהֲנָא בְּסִפְרָא וְיִמְחוֹק לְמַיָּא מְרִירַיָּא:


The *kohein* shall write all these curses, on the scroll and dissolve them (the writing) in the bitter waters.


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:24)


וְיַשְׁקֵי יָת אִתְּתָא יָת מַיָּא מְרִירַיָּא מְלַטְטַיָּא וְיֵעֲלוּן בַּהּ מַיָּא מְלַטְטַיָּא לִמְרִירוּ:


He shall have the woman drink the bitter, lethal waters and the lethal waters, shall enter her to become bitter.


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:25)


וְיִסַּב כַּהֲנָא מִידָא דְאִתְּתָא יָת מִנְחַת קִנְאָתָא וִירִים יָת מִנְחָתָא קֳדָם יְיָ וִיקָרֵב יָתַהּ לְמַדְבְּחָא:


The *kohein* shall take from the woman’s hand the meal-offering of jealousy and he shall wave the meal-offering before Adonoy, and bring it close to the Altar.


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:26)


וְיִקְמוֹץ כַּהֲנָא מִן מִנְחָתָא יָת אַדְכַּרְתַּהּ **וְיַסֵּק** לְמַדְבְּחָא וּבָתַר כֵּן יַשְׁקֵי יָת אִתְּתָא יָת מַיָּא:


The *kohein* shall scoop out from the meal-offering its commemoration and burn it on [**bring it up to**] the Altar, and after that he shall cause the woman to drink the water.


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:27)


וְיַשְׁקִנַּהּ יָת מַיָּא וּתְהֵי אִם אִסְתָּאָבַת **וְשַׁקָּרַת שְׁקָר** בְּבַעֲלַהּ וְיֵעֲלוּן בַּהּ מַיָּא מְלַטְטַיָּא לִמְרִירוּ **וְיִפְּחוּן** מְעָהָא **וְתִתְמְסֵי** יַרְכַּהּ וּתְהֵי אִתְּתָא לִלְוַט בְּגוֹ עַמַּהּ:


He shall make her drink the water, and it shall be that if she had been defiled and had deceived [**been untruthful to**] her husband, the lethal waters shall enter her to become bitter, and her abdomen will swell and her thigh will collapse [**fall away**], and the woman will be a curse among her people.


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:28)


וְאִם לָא אִסְתָּאָבַת אִתְּתָא וְדַכְיָאָה הִיא **וְתִפּוֹק זַכָּאָה וְתַעְדִּי עִדּוּי**:


But if the woman was not defiled and [she] is pure, she will be cleansed [**go out exonerated**] and shall bear seed [**will conceive**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:29)


דָּא אוֹרַיְתָא דְּקִנְאָתָא דִּי תִסְטֵי אִתְּתָא בַּר מִבַּעֲלַהּ וְתִסְתָּאָב:


This is the law of jealousies, when a woman goes astray during her marriage to her husband and is defiled.


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:30)


אוֹ גְבַר דִּי תֶעְבַּר עֲלוֹהִי רוּחַ קִנְאָה וִיקַנֵּי יָת אִתְּתֵהּ וִיקֵם יָת אִתְּתָא קֳדָם יְיָ וְיֶעְבַּד לַהּ כַּהֲנָא יָת כָּל אוֹרַיְתָא הָדָא:


If a man is overcome with a spirit of jealousy and he is jealous of his wife, then he shall have the woman stand before Adonoy, and the *kohein* shall do unto her all of these laws.


###### Onkelos Numbers 5:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/5:31)


וִיהֵי זַכָּאָה גַּבְרָא מֵחוֹבָא וְאִתְּתָא הַהִיא תְּקַבֵּל יָת חוֹבָהּ:


The man is cleansed [**innocent**] of sin and that woman shall bear [the burden of] her iniquity.



## Chapter 6

###### Onkelos Numbers 6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:1)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying;


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:2)


מַלֵּל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן גְּבַר אוֹ אִתְּתָא אֲרֵי יְפָרֵשׁ לְמִדַּר נְדַר נְזִירוּ לְמֵיזַר **קֳדָם** יְיָ:


Speak to Bnei Yisroel and say to them: If a man or a woman sets apart a vow, a nazirite vow, to set [himself] apart for [**from before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:3)


מֵחֲמַר חֲדַת וְעַתִּיק יִזַּר חַל דַּחֲמַר חֲדַת וְחַל דַּחֲמַר עַתִּיק לָא יִשְׁתֵּי וְכָל מַתְרוּת עִנְבִין לָא יִשְׁתֵּי וְעִנְבִין רַטִּיבִין וִיבֵשִׁין לָא יֵיכוּל:


From new or old [intoxicating] wine, he must abstain. Vinegar made from new wine and vinegar made from old wine he shall not drink; anything steeped in grapes he shall not drink; moist grapes or dried grapes (raisins) he shall not eat.


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:4)


כֹּל יוֹמֵי נִזְרֵיהּ מִכֹּל דְּיִתְעֲבֵד מִגֻּפְנָא דְחַמְרָא מִפּוּרְצְנִין וְעַד עִצּוּרִין לָא יֵיכוּל:


As long as he is a *Nazir* from anything made of the grape-vine, from seeds to skin, he shall not eat.


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:5)


**כָּל יוֹמֵי** נְדַר נִזְרֵיהּ מַסְפַּר לָא יֶעְבַּר עַל רֵישֵׁיהּ עַד מִשְׁלַם יוֹמַיָּא דְּיַנְזֵר קֳדָם יְיָ קַדִּישׁ יְהֵי מַרְבֵּי פֵרוּעַ שְׂעַר רֵישֵׁיהּ:


As long as [**all the days**] he is under his nazirite oath a razor shall not pass over [to touch] his head. Until he completes his days as a *Nazir* to Adonoy he shall be sacred, he shall let the hair of his head grow long.


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:6)


כָּל יוֹמִין דְיַנְזֵר קֳדָם יְיָ עַל נַפְשָׁא דְמֵתָא לָא יֵיעוּל:


All the days that he is a *Nazir* to Adonoy, he shall have no contact with the dead.


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:7)


לְאָבוּהִי וּלְאִמֵּיהּ לְאַחוּהִי וּלְאַחָתֵיהּ לָא יִסְתָּאַב לְהוֹן בְּמוֹתְהוֹן אֲרֵי כְּלִילָא דֶאֱלָהֵיהּ עַל רֵישֵׁיהּ:


For his father, mother, brother and sister he shall not [ritually] defile himself for them when they die. For, the nazirite crown of his God is upon his head.


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:8)


כֹּל יוֹמֵי נִזְרֵיהּ קַדִּישׁ הוּא קֳדָם יְיָ:


All the days of his separation, he is sacred to Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:9)


וַאֲרֵי יְמוּת מֵתָא עֲלוֹהִי בִּתְכֵּף שָׁלוּ וִיסָאֵב רֵישׁ נִזְרֵיהּ וִיגַלַּח רֵישֵׁיהּ בְּיוֹמָא דִדְכוּתֵיהּ בְּיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה יְגַלְּחִנֵּה:


If someone died near him suddenly, or unexpectedly, and caused his nazirite crowned head to be defiled, he shall shave his head on the day of his purification, he shall shave it on the seventh day.


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:10)


וּבְיוֹמָא תְמִּינָאָה יַיְתֵי תַּרְתֵּין שַׁפְנִינִין אוֹ תְרֵין בְּנֵי יוֹנָה לְוָת כַּהֲנָא לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא:


On the eighth day he shall take two turtledoves or two young pigeons to the *kohein,* to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting.


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:11)


וְיַעְבֵּד כַּהֲנָא חַד לְחַטָּאתָא וְחַד לַעֲלָתָא וִיכַפֵּר עֲלוֹהִי מִדְּחָב עַל מֵתָא וִיקַדַּשׁ יָת רֵישֵׁיהּ בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא:


The *kohein* shall prepare one for a sin-offering and one for a burnt-offering and atone on his behalf from his sin [of contact] with a [departed] soul, and he shall sanctify his head on that day.


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:12)


וְיַזֵּר קֳדָם יְיָ יָת יוֹמֵי נִזְרֵיהּ וְיַיְתִי אִמַּר בַּר שַׁתֵּיהּ לַאֲשָׁמָא וְיוֹמַיָּא קַדְמָאֵי יִבְטְלוּן אֲרֵי אִסְתָּאַב נִזְרֵיהּ:


He shall set apart, for Adonoy, his nazirite days, and bring a sheep in its first year as a guilt-offering. The first days shall elapse because his nazirite crown was defiled.


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:13)


וְדָא אוֹרַיְתָא דִנְזִירָא בְּיוֹם מִשְׁלַם יוֹמֵי נִזְרֵיהּ יַיְתִי יָתֵיהּ לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא:


This is the law of the *Nazir*: when the days of his nazirite status are fulfilled, he shall bring himself to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting.


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:14)


וִיקָרֵב יָת קֻרְבָּנֵיהּ קֳדָם יְיָ אִמַּר בַּר שַׁתֵּהּ שְׁלִים חַד לַעֲלָתָא וְאִמַּרְתָּא חֲדָא בַּת שַׁתֵּהּ שְׁלֶמְתָּא לְחַטָּאתָא וּדְכַר חַד שְׁלִים **לְנִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָּא**:


He shall bring his offering to Adonoy; one unblemished sheep, in its first year, as a burnt-offering, and one unblemished ewe, in its first year as a sin-offering, and one unblemished ram as a peace-offering [**sacred sacrifice**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:15)


וְסַל פַּטִּיר סֻלְתָּא גְרִיצַן דְּפִילָן בִּמְשַׁח וְאִסְפּוֹגִין פַּטִּירִין דִמְשִׁיחִין בִּמְשָׁח וּמִנְחַתְהוֹן וְנִסְכֵּיהוֹן:


A basket of *matzos* of fine flour, loaves mixed with oil and *matzoh*-wafers spread with oil and their meal-offerings and libations.


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:16)


וִיקָרֵב כַּהֲנָא קֳדָם יְיָ וְיַעְבֵּד יָת חַטָּאתֵיהּ וְיָת עֲלָתֵיהּ:


The *kohein* shall bring it before Adonoy and do the service of his sin-offering and of his burnt-offering.


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:17)


וְיָת דִּכְרָא יַעְבֵּד **נִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָּא** קֳדָם יְיָ עַל סַלָּא דְפַטִּירַיָּא וְיַעְבֵּד כַּהֲנָא יָת מִנְחָתֵיהּ וְיָת נִסְכֵּיהּ:


He shall make the ram a peace-offering [**sacred sacrifice**] for Adonoy along with the basket of *matzos,* and the *kohein* shall do the service of his meal-offering and libation.


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:18)


וִיגַלַּח נְזִירָא בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא יָת רֵישׁ נִזְרֵיהּ וְיִסַּב יָת שְׂעַר רֵישׁ נִזְרֵיהּ וְיִתֵּן עַל אֶשָּׁתָא דִּי תְחוֹת **דּוּדָא** דְּנִכְסַת **קוּדְשַׁיָּא**:


The *Nazir* shall shave—at the entrance to the Tent of Meeting—the nazirite crown of hair on his head, and he shall take the hair from the nazirite crown on his head and put it in the fire which is under the [**cauldron of the**] peace-offering [**sacred**] sacrifice.


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:19)


וְיִסַּב כַּהֲנָא יָת דְּרָעָא בְּשֵׁלָא מִן דִּכְרָא וּגְרִצְתָּא פַּטִּירְתָּא חֲדָא מִן סַלָּא וְאִסְפּוֹג פַּטִּיר חַד וְיִתֵּן עַל יְדֵי נְזִירָא בָּתַר דְּגַלַּח יָת נִזְרֵיהּ:


The *kohein* shall take the cooked foreleg from the ram and one *matzoh*-loaf from the basket, and one *matzoh*-wafer, and put it on the palms [**hands**] of the *Nazir* after he has shaved his nazirite crown.


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:20)


וִירִים יָתְהוֹן כַּהֲנָא אֲרָמָא קֳדָם יְיָ קוּדְשָׁא הוּא לְכַהֲנָא עַל חַדְיָא דַאֲרָמוּתָא וְעַל שָׁקָא דְאַפְרָשׁוּתָא וּבָתַר כֵּן יִשְׁתֵּי נְזִירָא חַמְרָא:


The *kohein* shall wave them as a wave-offering, before Adonoy. It is consecrated to the *kohein* besides the breast of the wave-offering and the separated thigh portion. After this, the *Nazir* may drink wine.


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:21)


דָּא אוֹרַיְתָא דִנְזִירָא דִּי יִדַּר קֻרְבָּנֵיהּ קֳדָם יְיָ עַל נִזְרֵיהּ בַּר **מִדְּתַדְבֵּק יְדֵיהּ** כְּפוּם נִדְרֵיהּ דִּי יִדַּר כֵּן יַעְבֵּד עַל אוֹרַיְתָא דְנִזְרֵיהּ:


This is the law of the *Nazir* who has vowed—[this is] his offering to Adonoy for his nazirite vow, aside from what he can afford [lit. **may attach to his hands**] to bring according to his vow that he vows. So shall he fulfill in addition to the laws of his nazirite [vows.]


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:22)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying;


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:23)


מַלֵּל עִם אַהֲרֹן וְעִם בְּנוֹהִי לְמֵימַר כְּדֵין תְּבָרְכוּן יָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל תֵּימְרוּן לְהוֹן:


Speak to Aharon and to his sons, saying; This is how you shall bless Bnei Yisroel, saying to them:


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:24)


יְבָרְכִנָּךְ יְיָ וְיִטְּרִנָּךְ:


May Adonoy bless you and protect you.


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:25)


יַנְהַר יְיָ **שְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ** לְוָתָךְ וִירַחֵם יָתָךְ:


May Adonoy cause His countenance [**Shechinah**] to shine upon you and favor you.


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:26)


יִסַּב יְיָ אַפֵּיהּ לְוָתָךְ וִישַׁוֵּי לָךְ שְׁלָם:


May Adonoy lift His face to you, and grant you peace.


###### Onkelos Numbers 6:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/6:27)


וִישַׁוּוּן יָת **בִּרְכַּת** שְׁמִי עַל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וַאֲנָא אֲבָרֵכִנּוּן:


They shall bestow [**the blessing of**] My Name upon Bnei Yisroel, and I will bless them.



## Chapter 7

###### Onkelos Numbers 7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:1)


וַהֲוָה בְּיוֹמָא דְשֵׁיצֵי משֶׁה לַאֲקָמָא יָת מַשְׁכְּנָא וְרַבִּי יָתֵיהּ וְקַדִּישׁ יָתֵיהּ וְיָת כָּל מָנוֹהִי וְיָת מַדְבְּחָא וְיָת כָּל מָנוֹהִי וְרַבִּנּוּן וְקַדִּישׁ יָתְהוֹן:


It came to pass, on the day Moshe finished erecting the *Mishkon,* and he anointed it and consecrated it and all of its utensils, and the Altar and all its utensils, and he anointed them and consecrated them.


###### Onkelos Numbers 7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:2)


וּקְרִיבוּ רַבְרְבֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל רֵישֵׁי בֵּית אֲבָהַתְהוֹן אִנּוּן רַבְרְבֵי שִׁבְטַיָּא אִנּוּן דְּקַיְמִין עַל מִנְיָנַיָּא:


The leaders of Israel brought, [those who were] the heads of their fathers’ houses; they were the leaders of the tribes, they stood by during the counting.


###### Onkelos Numbers 7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:3)


וְאַיְתִיוּ יָת קֻרְבָּנֵיהוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ שִׁית עֶגְלָן כַּד מְחַפְיַן וּתְרֵי עֲשַׂר תּוֹרִין עֶגְלְתָא עַל תְּרֵין רַבְרְבַיָּא וְתוֹר לְחַד וְקָרִיבוּ יָתְהוֹן לָקֳדָם מַשְׁכְּנָא:


They brought their offerings before Adonoy; six covered wagons and twelve oxen; a wagon for each two leaders and an ox for each individual, and they placed them before the *Mishkon.*


###### Onkelos Numbers 7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:4)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy said to Moshe, saying:


###### Onkelos Numbers 7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:5)


קַבֵּל מִנְּהוֹן וִיהוֹן לְמִפְלַח יָת פָּלְחַן מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְתִתֵּן יָתְהוֹן לְלֵוָאֵי גְּבַר **כְּמִסַּת** פָּלְחָנֵיהּ:


Take [the offering] from them, and let them be used for the service of the Tent of Meeting. Give them to the Levites, each man according to [**the needs**] his work.


###### Onkelos Numbers 7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:6)


וּנְסֵיב משֶׁה יָת עֶגְלָתָא וְיָת תּוֹרֵי וִיהַב יָתְהוֹן לְלֵוָאֵי:


Moshe took the wagons and the oxen and gave them to the Levites.


###### Onkelos Numbers 7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:7)


יָת תַּרְתֵּין עֶגְלָתָא וְיָת אַרְבְּעָא תוֹרֵי יְהַב לִבְנֵי גֵרְשׁוֹן **כְּמִסַּת** פָּלְחַנְהוֹן:


Two [of the] wagons and four [of the] oxen he gave to the sons of Gershon, according to [**the needs**] their work.


###### Onkelos Numbers 7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:8)


וְיָת אַרְבַּע עֶגְלַן וְיָת תְּמַנְיָא תוֹרֵי יְהַב לִבְנֵי מְרָרִי כְּמִסַּת פָּלְחַנְהוֹן בִּידָא דְּאִיתָמָר בַּר אַהֲרֹן כַּהֲנָא:


Four [of the] wagons and eight [of the] oxen he gave to the sons of Merori. According to their work they were in the charge of Isamar the son of Aharon the *kohein.*


###### Onkelos Numbers 7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:9)


וְלִבְנֵי קְהָת לָא יְהָב אֲרֵי פָלְחַן קוּדְשָׁא עֲלֵיהוֹן בְּכַתְפָּא נָטְלִין:


He did not give any to the sons of Kehos because the sacred work was [incumbent] upon them, which they had to carry on their shoulders.


###### Onkelos Numbers 7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:10)


וְקָרִיבוּ רַבְרְבַיָּא יָת חֲנֻכַּת מַדְבְּחָא בְּיוֹמָא דְרַבִּיּוּ יָתֵיהּ וְקָרִיבוּ רַבְרְבַיָּא יָת קֻרְבַּנְהוֹן לְקֳדָם מַדְבְּחָא:


The leaders brought forward the [offerings for] the consecration of the altar on the day it was anointed. The leaders placed their offering before the altar.


###### Onkelos Numbers 7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:11)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה רַבָּא חַד לְיוֹמָא רַבָּא חַד לְיוֹמָא יְקָרְבוּן יָת קֻרְבַּנְהוֹן לַחֲנֻכַּת מַדְבְּחָא:


Adonoy said to Moshe: One leader each day, one leader each day, they shall bring forth their offering, for the dedication of the altar.


###### Onkelos Numbers 7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:12)


וַהֲוָה דִּמְקָרַב בְּיוֹמָא קַדְמָאָה יָת קֻרְבָּנֵיהּ נַחְשׁוֹן בַּר עַמִּינָדָב לְשִׁבְטָא דִיהוּדָה:


The one that brought his offering on the first day [was] Nachshon the son of Aminadav, of the tribe of Yehudah.


###### Onkelos Numbers 7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:13)


וְקֻרְבָּנֵיהּ מְגִסְתָּא דִכְסַף חֲדָא מְאָה וּתְלָתִין סִלְעִין הֲוָה מַתְקְלַהּ מִזְרְקָא חַד דִּכְסַף מַתְקְלֵיהּ שַׁבְעִין סִלְעִין בְּסִלְעֵי קוּדְשָׁא תַּרְוֵיהוֹן מְלַן סֻלְתָּא דְּפִילָא בִמְשַׁח לְמִנְחָתָא:


His offering was one silver tray, its weight was one hundred thirty *shekolim;* one silver bowl [that weighed] seventy *shekolim* according to sanctuary weights. Both were filled with fine flour kneaded with [olive] oil, for a meal-offering.


###### Onkelos Numbers 7:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:14)

One spoon of ten gold [*shekolim*], filled with [**fragrant**] incense.

One young bull, one ram, and one yearling sheep for a burnt-offering.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:74](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:74)

One spoon of ten gold [*shekolim*], filled with incense.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:75](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:75)


תּוֹר חַד בַּר תּוֹרֵי דְּכַר חַד אִמַּר חַד בַּר שַׁתֵּיהּ לַעֲלָתָא:


One young bull, one ram, and one yearling sheep for a burnt-offering.


###### Onkelos Numbers 7:76

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:76](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:76)


צְפִיר בַּר עִזִּין חַד לְחַטָּאתָא:


[And] one he-goat for a sin-offering.


###### Onkelos Numbers 7:77

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:77](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:77)


וּלְנִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָּא תּוֹרִין תְּרֵין דִּכְרִין חַמְשָׁא גְּדָיֵי חַמְשָׁא אִמְּרִין בְּנֵי שְׁנָא חַמְשָׁא דֵּין קֻרְבַּן פַּגְעִיאֵל בַּר עָכְרָן:


For the peace-offering sacrifice, two oxen, five rams, five kids and five yearling sheep. This was the offering of Pagi’el the son of Ochran.


###### Onkelos Numbers 7:78

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:78](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:78)


בְּיוֹמָא תְּרֵי עֲשַׂר יוֹמָא רַבָּא לְבְנֵי נַפְתָּלִי אֲחִירַע בַּר עֵינָן:


On the day of the twelfth day, the leader of the sons of Naftali, Achirah the son of Einon.


###### Onkelos Numbers 7:79

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:79](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:79)


קֻרְבָּנֵיהּ מְגִסְתָּא דִכְסַף חֲדָא מְאָה וּתְלָתִין סִלְעִין הֲוָה מַתְקְלַהּ מִזְרְקָא חַד דִּכְסַף מַתְקְלֵיהּ שַׁבְעִין סִלְעִין בְּסִלְעֵי קוּדְשָׁא תַּרְוֵיהוֹן מְלַן סֻלְתָּא דְּפִילָא בִמְשַׁח לְמִנְחָתָא:


His offering was one silver tray its weight was one hundred thirty *shekolim*; one silver bowl [that weighed] seventy *shekolim* according to sanctuary weights. Both were filled with fine flour kneaded with [olive] oil, for a meal-offering.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:80](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:80)


בָּזִיכָא חֲדָא מַתְקַל עֲשַׂר סִלְעִין הִיא דִדְהַב מַלְיָא קְטֹרֶת בּוּסְמַיָּא:


One spoon of ten gold [*shekolim*], filled with incense.

תּוֹר חַד בַּר תּוֹרֵי דְּכַר חַד אִמַּר חַד בַּר שַׁתֵּיהּ לַעֲלָתָא:


One young bull, one ram, and one yearling sheep for a burnt-offering.

###### Onkelos Numbers 7:83

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:83](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:83)


וּלְנִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָּא תּוֹרִין תְּרֵין דִּכְרִין חַמְשָׁא גְּדָיֵי חַמְשָׁא אִמְּרִין בְּנֵי שְׁנָא חַמְשָׁא דֵּין קֻרְבַּן אֲחִירַע בַּר עֵינָן:
