דָּא חֲנֻכַּת מַדְבְּחָא בְּיוֹמָא דְּרַבִּיּוּ יָתֵיהּ מִן רַבְרְבֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מְגִיסֵי כַסְפָּא תַּרְתָּא עַשְׂרֵי מַזְרְקֵי כַסְפָּא תְּרֵין עֲשַׂר בָּזִיכֵי דְדַהֲבָא תַּרְתָּא עַשְׂרֵי:


This was the dedication of the altar on the day that it was anointed, given by the leaders of Israel; twelve silver trays twelve silver bowls and twelve golden spoons.


###### Onkelos Numbers 7:85

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:85](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:85)


מְאָה וּתְלָתִין **סִלְעִין הֲוָה מַתְקְלָא** דִמְגִיסְתָּא חֲדָא דְּכַסְפָּא וְשַׁבְעִין דְּמִזְרְקָא חַד כֹּל כְּסַף מָנַיָּא תְּרֵין אַלְפִין וְאַרְבַּע מְאָה בִּסִלְעֵי קוּדְשָׁא:


One hundred thirty [**shekolim was the weight of**] each silver tray and each bowl weighed seventy [*shekolim*]. All the silver vessels weighed two thousand four hundred [*shekolim*] according to sanctuary weights.


###### Onkelos Numbers 7:86

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:86](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:86)


בָּזִיכֵי דְדַהֲבָא תַּרְתֵּי עַשְׂרֵי מַלְיַן קְטֹרֶת **בּוּסְמַיָּא** מַתְקַל עֲשַׂר סִלְעִין הֲוָה מַתְקְלָא דְבָזִיכָא בְּסִלְעֵי קוּדְשָׁא כָּל דְּהַב בָּזִיכַיָּא מְאָה וְעַשְׂרִין:


Twelve golden spoons filled with [**fragrant**] incense; each spoon weighed ten [*shekolim*] according to sanctuary weights. The total of the golden spoons is one hundred twenty [*shekolim*].


###### Onkelos Numbers 7:87

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:87](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:87)


כָּל תּוֹרֵי לַעֲלָתָא תְּרֵי עֲשַׂר תּוֹרִין דִּכְרִין תְּרֵי עֲשַׂר אִמְּרִין בְּנֵי שְׁנָא תְּרֵי עֲשַׂר וּמִנְחַתְהוֹן וּצְפִירֵי בַּר עִזִּין תְּרֵי עֲשַׂר לְחַטָּאתָא:


The total of the oxen for the burnt-offering is twelve oxen, twelve rams, twelve yearling sheep and their meal-offerings, and twelve he-goats for a sin-offering.


###### Onkelos Numbers 7:88

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:88](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:88)


וְכֹל תּוֹרֵי לְנִכְסַת **קוּדְשַׁיָּא** עַשְׂרִין וְאַרְבְּעָא תּוֹרִין דִּכְרִין שִׁתִּין גַּדְיַן שִׁתִּין אִמְּרִין בְּנֵי שְׁנָא שִׁתִּין דָּא חֲנֻכַּת מַדְבְּחָא בָּתַר דְּרַבִּיּוּ יָתֵיהּ:


The total of the oxen for the peace [**sacred**]-offering sacrifice is twenty-four oxen, sixty rams, sixty kids and sixty yearling sheep. This was the dedication of the altar after it was anointed.


###### Onkelos Numbers 7:89

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:89](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/7:89)


וְכַד עָלִיל משֶׁה לְמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לְמַלָּלָא עִמֵּיהּ וּשְׁמַע יָת קָלָא דְמִתְמַלֵּל עִמֵּיהּ מֵעִלָּוֵי כַפֻּרְתָּא דִּי עַל אֲרוֹנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא מִבֵּין תְּרֵין כְּרוּבַיָּא וּמִתְמַלֵּל עִמֵּיהּ:


When Moshe would enter the Tent of Meeting to speak with Him (God), he would hear the Voice being spoken to him from above the Ark-cover which is atop the Ark of the Testimony from between the two cherubim; and He spoke to him.



## Chapter 8

###### Onkelos Numbers 8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:1)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


And Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying;


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:2)


מַלֵּל עִם אַהֲרֹן וְתֵימַר לֵיהּ בְּאַדְלָקוּתָךְ יָת בּוֹצִינַיָּא לָקֳבֵל אַפֵּי מְנַרְתָּא יְהוֹן מְנַהֲרִין שִׁבְעָא בוֹצִינַיָּא:


Speak to Aharon, and say to him; When you light the lamps towards the face of the Menorah shall the seven lamps cast [their] light.


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:3)


וַעֲבַד כֵּן אַהֲרֹן לָקֳבֵל אַפֵּי מְנַרְתָּא אַדְלֵק בּוֹצִינָהָא כְּמָא דִּי פַקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה:


Aharon did so; towards the face of the Menorah he lit its lamps, just as Adonoy commanded Moshe.


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:4)


וְדֵין עוֹבַד מְנַרְתָּא נְגִידָא דְהַב עַד שִׁידַהּ עַד שׁוֹשַׁנַּהּ נְגִידָא הִיא כְּחֶזְוָא דִּי אַחֲזֵי יְיָ יָת משֶׁה כֵּן עֲבַד יָת מְנַרְתָּא:


This is how the Menorah was made; beaten from [a block of] gold, from its base until its flowers it is beaten out [of a solid block], according to the vision which Adonoy showed to Moshe, so he made the Menorah.


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:5)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying;


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:6)


**קָרֵב** יָת לֵוָאֵי מִגּוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וּתְדַּכֵּי יָתְהוֹן:


Take [**bring near**] the Levites from among Bnei Yisroel and purify them.


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:7)


וּכְדֵין תַּעְבֵּד לְהוֹן לְדַכּוֹאֵיהוֹן אַדֵּי עֲלֵיהוֹן מַיָּא דְחַטָּאתָא וְיַעְבְּרוּן מַסְפַּר עַל כָּל בִּשְׂרְהוֹן וִיחַוְּרוּן לְבוּשֵׁיהוֹן וְיִדְּכּוּן:


This is what you shall do to them, to purify them; sprinkle them with the sin-offering waters, and cause a razor to pass over (shave) their entire bodies, and they shall wash their garments and purify themselves.


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:8)


וִיסְבוּן תּוֹר בַּר תּוֹרֵי וּמִנְחָתֵיהּ סֻלְתָּא דְּפִילָא בִמְשָׁח וְתוֹר תִּנְיַן בַּר תּוֹרֵי תִּסַּב לְחַטָּאתָא:


They should take a young bullock and its meal-offering of flour mixed with oil, and you shall take a second young bullock, for a sin-offering.


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:9)


וּתְקָרֵב יָת לֵוָאֵי קֳדָם מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְתִכְנֵשׁ יָת כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא דִּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


You shall bring the Levites near, before the Tent of Meeting, and you shall gather the entire congregation of Bnei Yisroel.


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:10)


וּתְקָרֵב יָת לֵוָאֵי קֳדָם יְיָ וְיִסְמְכוּן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל יָת יְדֵיהוֹן עַל לֵוָאֵי:


You shall bring the Levites near, before Adonoy, and Bnei Yisroel shall lay their hands on the Levites.


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:11)


וִירִים אַהֲרֹן יָת לֵוָאֵי אֲרָמָא קֳדָם יְיָ מִן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וִיהוֹן לְמִפְלַח יָת פָּלְחָנָא דַיְיָ:


Aharon shall wave the Levites as a wave-offering before Adonoy from Bnei Yisroel and they will be [entrusted] to do the worship of Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:12)


וְלֵוָאֵי יִסְמְכוּן יָת יְדֵיהוֹן עַל רֵישׁ תּוֹרַיָּא וְיַעֲבֵּד יָת חַד חַטָּאתָא וְיָת חַד עֲלָתָא קֳדָם יְיָ לְכַפָּרָא עַל לֵוָאֵי:


The Levites shall lay their hands on the head of the bulls and make the one for a sin-offering and the [other] one for a burnt-offering to Adonoy, to atone for the Levites.


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:13)


וּתְקֵם יָת לֵוָאֵי קֳדָם אַהֲרֹן וְקָדָם בְּנוֹהִי וּתְרֵם יָתְהוֹן אֲרָמָא קֳדָם יְיָ:


You shall set the Levites before Aharon and before his sons, and lift them up as a wave-offering to Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:14)


וְתַפְרֵשׁ יָת לֵוָאֵי מִגּוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וִיהוֹן **מְשַׁמְּשִׁין קֳדָמַי** לֵוָאֵי:


You shall separate the Levites from among Bnei Yisroel and the Levites shall be Mine [**serve before Me**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:15)


וּבָתַר כֵּן יַעֲלוּן לֵוָאֵי לְמִפְלַח יָת מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וּתְדַכֵּי יָתְהוֹן וּתְרֵם יָתְהוֹן אֲרָמָא:


After this is done, the Levites shall come to work in the Tent of Meeting, and you shall purify them, and you shall lift them up as a wave-offering,


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:16)


אֲרֵי **אַפְרָשָׁא מַפְרְשִׁין** אִנּוּן לִי מִגּוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל חֲלַף פְּתַח כָּל וַלְדָּא בּוּכְרָא כֹלָּא מִבְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל קָרֵבִית יָתְהוֹן קֳדָמָי:


For they are given [**have been separated**]; they are given [**have been separated**] to Me from among Bnei Yisroel in place of those who open the womb,—every firstborn of Bnei Yisroel—I have taken them to be Mine.


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:17)


אֲרֵי דִילִי כָל בּוּכְרָא בִּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל בַּאֲנָשָׁא וּבִבְעִירָא בְּיוֹמָא דִּקְטָלִית כָּל בּוּכְרָא בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם אַקְדֵּשִׁית יָתְהוֹן קֳדָמָי:


For every firstborn of Bnei Yisroel is Mine, human and animal; on the day in which I slew every firstborn in the land of Egypt, I have sanctified them to be Mine.


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:18)


וְקָרֵבִית יָת לֵוָאֵי חֲלַף כָּל בּוּכְרָא בִּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


I have taken the Levites instead of all firstborn of Bnei Yisroel,


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:19)


וִיהָבִית יָת לֵוָאֵי מְסִירִין לְאַהֲרֹן וְלִבְנוֹהִי מִגּוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמִפְלַח יָת פָּלְחַן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וּלְכַפָּרָא עַל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְלָא יְהֵי בִּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מוֹתָא בְּמִקְרַב בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְקוּדְשָׁא:


and I have given the Levites to be given as a gift to Aharon and to his sons from among Bnei Yisroel to do the duties of Bnei Yisroel in the Tent of Meeting, and to atone for Bnei Yisroel, so that there will not be a plague among Bnei Yisroel when Bnei Yisroel approach the sanctuary.


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:20)


וַעֲבַד משֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן וְכָל כְּנִשְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְלֵוָאֵי כְּכֹל דִּי פַקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה לְלֵוָאֵי כֵּן עֲבָדוּ לְהוֹן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


Moshe, Aharon, and the entire congregation of Bnei Yisroel did to the Levites just as Adonoy commanded Moshe regarding the Levites, so did Bnei Yisroel do to them.


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:21)


וְאִדַּכִּיּוּ לֵוָאֵי וְחַוָּרוּ לְבוּשֵׁיהוֹן וַאֲרֵם אַהֲרֹן יָתְהוֹן אֲרָמָא קֳדָם יְיָ וְכַפַּר עֲלֵיהוֹן אַהֲרֹן לְדַכּוֹאֵיהוֹן:


The Levites underwent purification and they washed their garments, and Aharon lifted them as a wave-offering before Adonoy; and Aharon achieved atonement on their behalf, to purify them.


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:22)


וּבָתַר כֵּן עֲלּוּ לֵוָאֵי לְמִפְלַח יָת פָּלְחַנְהוֹן בְּמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא קֳדָם אַהֲרֹן וְקָדָם בְּנוֹהִי כְּמָא דִּי פַקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה עַל לֵוָאֵי כֵּן עֲבָדוּ לְהוֹן:


Afterwards, the Levites came to perform their duties in the Tent of Meeting in the presence of Aharon and in the presence of his sons; just as Adonoy commanded Moshe regarding the Levites, so they did unto them.


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:23)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying;


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:24)


דָּא דִּי לְלֵוָאֵי מִבַּר חָמֵשׁ וְעַשְׂרִין שְׁנִין וּלְעֵלָּא יֵיתֵי לַחֲיָלָא חֵילָא בְּפָלְחַן מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא:


This concerns the Levites; from the age of twenty-five years and above he shall enter the division for the duties of the Tent of Meeting.


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:25)


וּמִבַּר חַמְשִׁין שְׁנִין יְתוּב מֵחֵיל פָּלְחָנָא וְלָא יִפְלַח עוֹד:


From the age of fifty he shall retire from the work force, and shall no longer work.


###### Onkelos Numbers 8:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/8:26)


וִישַׁמַּשׁ עִם אֲחוֹהִי בְּמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לְמִטַּר מַטָּרָא וּפָלְחָנָא לָא יִפְלָח כְּדֵין תַּעְבֵּד לְלֵוָאֵי בְּמַטְּרַתְהוֹן:


But shall serve with his brethren in the Tent of Meeting to keep the watch, and he shall not do any service; so you shall have the Levites do in their guard duties.



## Chapter 9

###### Onkelos Numbers 9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:1)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְסִינַי בְּשַׁתָּא תִנְיֵתָא לְמִפַּקְהוֹן מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם בְּיַרְחָא קַדְמָאָה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe in the desert of Sinai in the second year of their exodus from the land of Egypt, in the first month, saying;


###### Onkelos Numbers 9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:2)


וְיַעְבְּדוּן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל יָת פִּסְחָא בְּזִמְנֵיהּ:


Bnei Yisroel shall perform the *Pesach*-offering in its proper time.


###### Onkelos Numbers 9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:3)


בְּאַרְבְּעַת עַשְׂרָא יוֹמָא בְּיַרְחָא הָדֵין בֵּין שִׁמְשַׁיָּא תַּעְבְּדוּן יָתֵיהּ בְּזִמְנֵיהּ כְּכָל גְּזֵרָתֵיהּ וּכְכָל **דְּחָזֵי לֵיהּ** תַּעְבְּדוּן יָתֵיהּ:


On the fourteenth day of this month, in the afternoon, you shall perform it in its proper time; according to all its statutes and according to all its laws [**that are appropriate for it**] you shall perform it.


###### Onkelos Numbers 9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:4)


וּמַלִּיל משֶׁה עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמֶעְבַּד פִּסְחָא:


Moshe spoke to Bnei Yisroel [telling them] to perform the Pesach-offering.


###### Onkelos Numbers 9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:5)


וַעֲבָדוּ יָת פִּסְחָא **בְּנִיסָן** בְּאַרְבְּעַת עַשְׂרָא יוֹמָא לְיַרְחָא בֵּין שִׁמְשַׁיָּא בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְסִינָי כְּכֹל דִּי פַקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה כֵּן עֲבָדוּ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


They performed the Pesach-offering in the first [month] [**in Nissan**] on the fourteenth day of the month in the afternoon in the Sinai desert; according to all that Adonoy commanded Moshe, so did Bnei Yisroel do.


###### Onkelos Numbers 9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:6)


וַהֲווֹ גֻבְרַיָּא דִּי הֲווֹ מְסָאֲבִין לִטְמֵי נַפְשָׁא דֶאֱנָשָׁא וְלָא יְכִילוּ לְמֶעְבַּד פִּסְחָא בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא וּקְרִיבוּ קֳדָם משֶׁה וְקָדָם אַהֲרֹן בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא:


There were men who were impure, having had [contact with] a corpse, and they were not able to perform the Pesach-offering on that day, and they drew near before Moshe and before Aharon, on that day.


###### Onkelos Numbers 9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:7)


וַאֲמָרוּ גֻּבְרַיָּא הָאִנּוּן לֵיהּ אֲנַחְנָא מְסָאֲבִין לִטְמֵי נַפְשָׁא דֶאֱנָשָׁא לְמָא נִתְמְנַע בְּדִיל דְּלָא לְקָרָבָא יָת קֻרְבָּנָא דַיְיָ בְּזִמְנֵיהּ בְּגוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


Those men said to him: “We are impure, having had contact with a corpse; why should we be excluded and not be able to bring the offering of Adonoy in its proper time, among Bnei Yisroel?”


###### Onkelos Numbers 9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:8)


וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן משֶׁה **אוֹרִיכוּ** עַד דְּאִשְׁמַע מָה **דְאִתְפַּקַּד** קֳדָם יְיָ **עַל דִּי** לְכוֹן:


Moshe said to them: “Stand [**wait**] and I will listen to what Adonoy will instruct [**command**] for [**regarding**] you.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:9)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


###### Onkelos Numbers 9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:10)


מַלֵּל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמֵימָר גְּבַר גְּבַר אֲרֵי יְהֵי מְסָאָב לִטְמֵי נַפְשָׁא דֶאֱנָשָׁא אוֹ בְאָרְחָא רְחִיקָא לְכוֹן אוֹ לְדָרֵיכוֹן וְיַעְבֵּד פִּסְחָא קֳדָם יְיָ:


Speak to Bnei Yisroel, saying; any person, if he will be impure from a corpse, or [is] on a distant road, [whether among] yourselves or for your descendants, he shall perform the Pesach-offering for Adonoy


###### Onkelos Numbers 9:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:11)


בְּיַרְחָא תִנְיָנָא בְּאַרְבְּעַת עַשְׂרָא יוֹמָא בֵּין שִׁמְשַׁיָּא יַעְבְּדוּן יָתֵיהּ עַל פַּטִּיר וּמְרָרִין יֵיכְלֻנֵּיהּ:


in the second month, on the fourteenth day in the afternoon he shall perform it. Together with *matzohs* and bitter herbs they shall eat it.


###### Onkelos Numbers 9:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:12)


לָא יַשְׁאֲרוּן מִנֵּיהּ עַד צַפְרָא וְגַרְמָא לָא יִתְבְּרוּן בֵּיהּ כְּכָל גְּזֵרַת פִּסְחָא יַעְבְּדוּן יָתֵיהּ:


They shall not leave any of it over until the morning and they shall not break any of its bones; according to all the statutes of the Pesach-offering they shall perform it.


###### Onkelos Numbers 9:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:13)


וְגַבְרָא דְהוּא דְכֵי וּבְאֹרַח לָא הֲוָה וְיִתְמְנַע לְמֶעְבַּד פִּסְחָא וְיִשְׁתֵּצֵי אֲנָשָׁא הַהִיא מֵעַמַּהּ אֲרֵי קֻרְבָּנָא דַיְיָ לָא קָרִיב בְּזִמְנֵיהּ חוֹבֵיהּ יְקַבֵּל גַּבְרָא הַהוּא:


The person who is pure and was not on the road, and refrained from performing the Pesach-offering, that soul shall be cut off from its people, for the offering of Adonoy he did not bring in its proper time; that person shall bear his sin.


###### Onkelos Numbers 9:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:14)


וַאֲרֵי יִתְגַּיַּר עִמְּכוֹן גִּיּוֹרָא וְיַעְבֵּד פִּסְחָא קֳדָם יְיָ כִּגְזֵרַת פִּסְחָא וְכִדְחָזֵי לֵיהּ כֵּן יַעְבֵּד קְיָמָא חַד יְהֵי לְכוֹן וּלְגִיּוֹרָא וּלְיַצִּיבָא דְאַרְעָא:


If a proselyte dwells among you and brings the Pesach-offering to Adonoy; according to the statues of the Pesach-offering and according to its laws so shall he perform it; there shall be the same statute for yourselves; [both] for the proselyte and for the native born citizen.


###### Onkelos Numbers 9:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:15)


וּבְיוֹמָא דְּאִתָּקַם יָת מַשְׁכְּנָא חֲפָא עֲנָנָא יָת מַשְׁכְּנָא לְמַשְׁכְּנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא וּבְרַמְשָׁא הֲוָה עַל מַשְׁכְּנָא כְּחֵזוּ אֶשָּׁתָא עַד צַפְרָא:


On the day that the *Mishkon* was erected, the Cloud covered the *Mishkon* [which served] as the Tent of Testimony; and in the evening, there was over the *Mishkon* an appearance of fire, until morning.


###### Onkelos Numbers 9:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:16)


כֵּן הֲוָה תְדִירָא עֲנָנָא חָפֵי לֵיהּ וְחֵזוּ אֶשָּׁתָא בְּלֵילְיָא:


So it always was, the Cloud covered it, and an appearance of fire, at night.


###### Onkelos Numbers 9:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:17)


וּלְפוּם אִסְתַּלָּקוּת עֲנָנָא מֵעִלָּוֵי מַשְׁכְּנָא וּבָתַר כֵּן נָטְלִין בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וּבְאַתְרָא דְּשָׁרֵי תַמָּן עֲנָנָא תַּמָּן שָׁרָן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


Whenever the Cloud was lifted from the Tent, afterwards, did Bnei Yisroel travel; in the place where the Cloud came to rest, it is there that Bnei Yisroel did camp.


###### Onkelos Numbers 9:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:18)


עַל מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ נָטְלִין בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְעַל מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ שָׁרָן כָּל יוֹמֵי דִּי שָׁרֵי עֲנָנָא עַל מַשְׁכְּנָא שָׁרָן:


On the word of Adonoy, did Bnei Yisroel travel and on the word of Adonoy, did they camp; all those days on which the Cloud will rested above the *Mishkon*, did they camp.


###### Onkelos Numbers 9:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:19)


וּבְאוֹרָכוּת עֲנָנָא עַל מַשְׁכְּנָא יוֹמִין סַגִּיאִין וְיִטְּרוּן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל יָת מַטְּרַת **מֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ וְלָא נָטְלִין:


When the Cloud tarried above the *Mishkon*, for many days, Bnei Yisroel would keep the watch of [**the word of**] Adonoy and they would not travel.


###### Onkelos Numbers 9:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:20)


וְאִית דִּי הֲוָה עֲנָנָא יוֹמֵי דְמִנְיַן עַל מַשְׁכְּנָא עַל מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ שָׁרָן וְעַל מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ נָטְלִין:


It sometimes happened that the Cloud was for a number of days above the *Mishkon*; on the order of Adonoy did they camp and on the order of Adonoy did they travel.


###### Onkelos Numbers 9:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:21)


וְאִית דִּי הֲוָה עֲנָנָא מֵרַמְשָׁא עַד צַפְרָא וּמִסְתַּלַּק עֲנָנָא בְּצַפְרָא וְנָטְלִין אוֹ יֵמָם וְלֵילֵי וּמִסְתַּלַּק עֲנָנָא וְנָטְלִין:


It sometimes happened that the Cloud was [there] [but] from evening until morning, and then the Cloud rose in the morning and they traveled; or, [the Cloud was there] for a day and a night and the Cloud was lifted, and they traveled.


###### Onkelos Numbers 9:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:22)


אוֹ תְרֵין יוֹמִין אוֹ יַרְחָא אוֹ **עִדָּן בְּעִדָּן** בְּאוֹרָכוּת עֲנָנָא עַל מַשְׁכְּנָא לְמִשְׁרֵי עֲלוֹהִי שָׁרָן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְלָא נָטְלִין וּבְאִסְתַּלָּקוּתֵיהּ נָטְלִין:


Or two days or a month or a year [**period of time**] that the Cloud prolonged its stay above the *Mishkon* to rest upon it; Bnei Yisroel remained encamped and they did not travel; when it rose they traveled.


###### Onkelos Numbers 9:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/9:23)


עַל מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ שָׁרָן וְעַל מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ נָטְלִין יָת מַטְּרַת **מֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ נָטְרִין עַל מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ בִּידָא דְמשֶׁה:


On the order of Adonoy they camped and on the order of [**the word of**] Adonoy they traveled; they kept the watch of Adonoy on the order of Adonoy, through Moshe.



## Chapter 10

###### Onkelos Numbers 10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:1)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:2)


עִבֵיד לָךְ תַּרְתֵּין חֲצוֹצְרַן דִּכְסַף נְגִיד תַּעְבֵּד יָתְהוֹן וִיהֶוְיָן לָךְ לְעַרְעָא כְנִשְׁתָּא וּלְאַטָּלָא יָת מַשְׁרִיתָא:


Make for yourself two silver trumpets; you shall make them, beaten [from one block], and they shall be used by you to summon the congregation and to initiate the departure of the camps.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:3)


וְיִתְקְעוּן בְּהֵן וְיִזְדַּמְּנוּן לְוָתָךְ כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא:


When they will blow them, the entire congregation shall present itself to you at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:4)


וְאִם בַּחֲדָא יִתְקְעוּן וְיִזְדַּמְּנוּן לְוָתָךְ רַבְרְבַיָּא רֵישֵׁי אַלְפַיָּא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


But if they blow one of them, the leaders shall present themselves to you, the heads of Israel’s thousands.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:5)


וְתִתְקְעוּן **יַבֶּבְתָּא** וְיִטְּלוּן מַשִּׁרְיָתָא דִּשְׁרַן קִדּוּמָא:


When you blow a *teruah* [**a wailing sound**]; the camps that are camped eastward shall travel.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:6)


וְתִתְקְעוּן יַבֶּבְתָּא תִּנְיָנוּת וְיִטְּלוּן מַשִּׁרְיָתָא דִּשְׁרַן דָּרוֹמָא יַבָּבָא יִתְקְעוּן לְמַטְּלָנֵיהוֹן:


If you blow a second *teruah,* then shall travel the camps that are camped on the south side. They shall blow a *teruah* for their journeys.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:7)


וּבְמִכְנַשׁ יָת קְהָלָא תִּתְקְעוּן וְלָא תְיַבְּבוּן:


But when gathering the congregation, you shall blow [a blast], but not a *teruah.*


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:8)


וּבְנֵי אַהֲרֹן כָּהֲנַיָּא יִתְקְעוּן בַּחֲצוֹצְרָתָא וִיהֶוְיָן לְכוֹן לִקְיַם עֲלָם לְדָרֵיכוֹן:


The sons of Aharon, the *kohanim,* shall sound the trumpets. This shall be for you an everlasting statute, for all your descendants.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:9)


וַאֲרֵי תֵעֲלוּן לְאַגָּחָא קְרָבָא בְּאַרְעֲכוֹן עַל מְעִיקֵי דִּמְעִיקִין לְכוֹן **וּתְיַבְּבוּן** בַּחֲצוֹצְרָתָא **וְיֵיעוּל דּוּכְרָנֵיכוֹן לְטָבָא** קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן וְתִתְפָּרְקוּן מִסַּנְאֵיכוֹן:


If war shall come into your land against the oppresor who oppresses you and you shall blow *teruahs* [**wail**] with the trumpets and you will be remembered [**your remembrance will come beneficially**] before Adonoy, your God, and you will be delivered from you enemies.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:10)


וּבְיוֹם חֶדְוַתְכוֹן וּבְמוֹעֲדֵיכוֹן וּבְרֵישֵׁי יַרְחֵיכוֹן וְתִתְקְעוּן בַּחֲצוֹצְרָתָא עַל עֲלָוָתְכוֹן וְעַל נִכְסַת קוּדְשֵׁיכוֹן וִיהֶוְיָן לְכוֹן לְדוּכְרָנָא קֳדָם אֱלָהָכוֹן אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן:


On your joyous days and on your festivals and on the beginnings of your months you shall blow with the trumpets for your burnt-offerings and for your peace-offerings; and it shall be a remembrance for you before your God, I am Adonoy your God.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:11)


וַהֲוָה בְּשַׁתָּא תִנְיֵתָא בְּיַרְחָא תִנְיָנָא בְּעַשְׂרִין לְיַרְחָא אִסְתַּלַּק עֲנָנָא מֵעִלָּוֵי מַשְׁכְּנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא:


It was in the second year, in the second month on the twentieth of the month that the Cloud rose up from above the *Mishkon* of the Testimony.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:12)


וּנְטָלוּ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמַטְּלָנֵיהוֹן מִמַּדְבְּרָא דְסִינָי וּשְׁרָא עֲנָנָא בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְפָארָן:


Bnei Yisroel traveled along on their journeys, from the Sinai desert, and the Cloud came to rest in the desert of Paran.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:13)


וּנְטָלוּ בְּקַדְמֵיתָא עַל מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ בִּידָא דְמשֶׁה:


This was the first time that they traveled, on the order of Adonoy, through Moshe.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:14)


וּנְטַל טֵקַס מַשְׁרִית בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה בְּקַדְמֵיתָא לְחֵילֵיהוֹן וְעַל חֵילֵיהּ נַחְשׁוֹן בַּר עַמִּינָדָב:


Then traveled the banner of the sons of Yehudah’s camp in the forefront according to their divisions; in charge of its divisions [was] Nachshon the son of Aminadav.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:15)


וְעַל חֵילָא דְּשִׁבְטָא דִּבְנֵי יִשָּׂשכָר נְתַנְאֵל בַּר צוּעָר:


In charge of the division of the tribe of the sons of Yissachar, [was] Nesanel the son of Tzu’ar.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:16)


וְעַל חֵילָא דְּשִׁבְטָא דִּבְנֵי זְבוּלֻן אֱלִיאָב בַּר חֵלוֹן:


In charge of the division of the tribe of the sons of Zevulun, [was] Eliav the son of Cheilon.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:17)


וּמִתְפָּרַק מַשְׁכְּנָא וְנָטְלִין בְּנֵי גֵרְשׁוֹן וּבְנֵי מְרָרִי נָטְלֵי מַשְׁכְּנָא:


[Then] the *Mishkon* was dismantled and the sons of Gershon traveled, with the sons of Merori, those who carried the *Mishkon*.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:18)


וּנְטַל טֵקַס מַשְׁרִית רְאוּבֵן לְחֵילֵיהוֹן וְעַל חֵילֵיהּ אֱלִיצוּר בַּר שְׁדֵיאוּר:


[Then] the banner of Reuven’s camp traveled, according to their divisions. In charge of its division [was] Elitzur the son of Shdeiur.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:19)


וְעַל חֵילָא דְּשִׁבְטָא דִּבְנֵי שִׁמְעוֹן שְׁלֻמִיאֵל בַּר צוּרִישַׁדָּי:


In charge of the division of the tribe of the sons of Shimon, [was] Shlumiel the son of Tzurishadai.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:20)


וְעַל חֵילָא דְּשִׁבְטָא דִּבְנֵי גָד אֶלְיָסָף בַּר דְּעוּאֵל:


In charge of the division of the tribe of the sons of Gad, [was] Elyasaf the son of De’uel.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:21)


וְנָטְלִין בְּנֵי קְהָת נָטְלֵי מַקְדְּשָׁא וּמְקִימִין יָת מַשְׁכְּנָא עַד מֵיתֵיהוֹן:


[Then] the Kehos family traveled, the carriers of the sacred, and they erected the *Mishkon* by the time of their arrival.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:22)


וּנְטַל טֵקַס מַשְׁרִית בְּנֵי אֶפְרַיִם לְחֵילֵיהוֹן וְעַל חֵילֵיהּ אֱלִישָׁמָע בַּר עַמִּיהוּד:


[Then] the banner of the sons of Ephraim’s camp traveled according to their divisions. In charge of its division, [was] Elishamah the son of Amihud.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:23)


וְעַל חֵילָא דְּשִׁבְטָא דִּבְנֵי מְנַשֶּׁה גַּמְלִיאֵל בַּר פְּדָהצוּר:


In charge of the division of the tribe of the sons of Menashe, [was] Gamliel the son of Pedahtzur.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:24)


וְעַל חֵילָא דְּשִׁבְטָא דִּבְנֵי בִנְיָמִן אֲבִידָן בַּר גִּדְעוֹנִי:


In charge of the division of the tribe of the sons of Binyamin, [was] Avidan the son of Gid’oni.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:25)


וְנָטֵל טֵקַס מַשְׁרִית בְּנֵי דָן מַכְנֵישׁ לְכָל מַשִּׁרְיָתָא לְחֵילֵיהוֹן וְעַל חֵילֵיהּ אֲחִיעֶזֶר בַּר עַמִּישַׁדָּי:


[Then] the banner of the sons of Don’s camp traveled; the gatherer for all the camps, according to their divisions. In charge of its division, [was] Achiezer the son of Amishadai.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:26)


וְעַל חֵילָא דְּשִׁבְטָא דִּבְנֵי אָשֵׁר פַּגְעִיאֵל בַּר עָכְרָן:


In charge of the division of the tribe of the sons of Asher, [was] Pagiel the son of Ochran.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:27)


וְעַל חֵילָא דְּשִׁבְטָא דִּבְנֵי נַפְתָּלִי אֲחִירַע בַּר עֵינָן:


In charge of the division of the tribe of the sons of Naftali, [was] Achirah the son of Einan.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:28)


אִלֵּין מַטְּלָנֵי בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְחֵילֵיהוֹן וּנְטָלוּ:


These are the journeys of Bnei Yisroel according to their divisions; and they journeyed.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:29)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְחֹבָב בַּר רְעוּאֵל מִדְיָנָאָה חֲמוּהִי דְמשֶׁה נָטְלִין אֲנַחְנָא לְאַתְרָא דִּי אֲמַר יְיָ יָתֵיהּ אֶתֵּן לְכוֹן אִיתָא עִמָּנָא וְנוֹטֵיב לָךְ אֲרֵי יְיָ מַלִּיל לְאַיְתָאָה טָבָא עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל:


Moshe said to Chovav the son of Reuel, the Midianite, Moshe’s father-in-law: “We are journeying to the place about which Adonoy said; ‘I will give it to you’, come along with us and we will treat you well, for Adonoy spoke of bringing good [fortune] on Israel.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:30)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ לָא אֵזֵל אֶלָּהֵן לְאַרְעִי וּלְיַלָּדוּתִי אֵזֵל:


He said to him “I will not go, but rather to my land and to my birthplace will I go.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:31)


וַאֲמַר לָא כְעַן תִּשְׁבּוֹק יָתָנָא אֲרֵי עַל כֵּן יְדַעַתְּ כַּד הֲוֵינָא שָׁרַן בְּמַדְבְּרָא **וּגְבוּרַן דְּאִתְעֲבִידַן לָנָא חֲזֵיתָא בְעֵינָיִךְ**:


He said to him “please do not forsake us, for, because you know of our encampment in the desert, and you will be our eyes [**the great things that were done for us you saw with your eyes**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:32)


וִיהֵי אֲרֵי תֵזֵל עִמָּנָא וִיהֵי טָבָא הַהוּא דִּי יוֹטִיב יְיָ עִמָּנָא וְנוֹטֵיב לָךְ:


It will be, that when you go with us, it shall be that the very good which Adonoy will bestow on us we will bestow on (share with) you.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:33)


וּנְטָלוּ מִטּוּרָא **דְּאִתְגְּלֵי עֲלוֹהִי יְקָרָא דַיְיָ** מַהֲלַךְ תְּלָתָא יוֹמִין וַאֲרוֹן קְיָמָא דַיְיָ נָטֵל קֳדָמֵיהוֹן מַהֲלַךְ תְּלָתָא יוֹמִין לְאַתְקְנָאָה לְהוֹן אֲתַר בֵּית מֵישְׁרֵי:


They traveled from the mountain of Adonoy [**upon which the glory of Hashem was revealed**] a journey of three days, and the Ark of the Covenant of Adonoy traveled in front of them a journey of three days, to prepare for them a place to settle.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:34)


וַעֲנַן **יְקָרָא** דַיְיָ מַטֵּיל עֲלֵיהוֹן בִּימָמָא בְּמִטַּלְהוֹן מִן מַשְׁרִיתָא:


The Cloud [**of the glory**] of Adonoy was above them by day when they traveled from the camp.


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:35)


וַהֲוָה בְּמִטַּל אֲרוֹנָא וַאֲמַר משֶׁה **אִתְגְּלֵי** יְיָ וְיִתְבַּדְּרוּן סַנְאָיךְ וִיעָרְקוּן בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבָךְ מִן קֳדָמָךְ:


Whenever the Ark departed Moshe woud say: “Rise, [**reveal Yourself**] Adonoy, and may Your enemies disperse, and those who hate You flee before You.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 10:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/10:36)


וּבְמִשְׁרוֹהִי אֲמַר תּוּב יְיָ **שְׁרֵי בִיקָרָךְ** בְּגוֹ רִבְוַת אַלְפַיָּא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


When it rested, he would say: “Come to rest, Adonoy, [**dwell in Your glory**] among the myriads and thousands of Israel.”



## Chapter 11

###### Onkelos Numbers 11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:1)


וַהֲוָה עַמָּא כַּד מִסְתַּקְפִין בִּישׁ **קֳדָם** יְיָ וּשְׁמִיעַ**קֳדָם יְיָ** וּתְקֵף רָגְזֵיהּ וּדְלֵקַת בְּהוֹן אֶשָּׁתָא מִן **קֳדָם** יְיָ וְשֵׁצִיאַת בִּסְיָפֵי מַשְׁרִיתָא:


The people were like complainers; [it was] wicked in the ears of [**before**] Adonoy. Adonoy heard [**before Hashem it was revealed**] and His Anger flared and the fire of [**from before**] Adonoy burned among them and consumed [some] of the outcasts of the camp.


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:2)


וּצְוַח עַמָּא עַל משֶׁה וְצַלִּי משֶׁה קֳדָם יְיָ וְאִשְׁתַּקְּעַת אֶשָּׁתָא:


The people cried out to Moshe, and Moshe prayed to Adonoy, and the fire subsided.


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:3)


וּקְרָא שְׁמָא דְאַתְרָא הַהוּא דְּלֶקְתָּא אֲרֵי דְלֵקַת בְּהוֹן אֶשָּׁתָא מִן **קֳדָם** יְיָ:


He called the name of that place Taveirah, for it was there that burned among them the fire of [**from before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:4)


וַעֲרַבְרְבִין דִּי בֵינֵיהוֹן **שְׁאִילוּ שְׁאֶלְתָּא** וְתָבוּ וּבְכוֹ אַף בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וַאֲמָרוּ מַאן יֵיכְלִנָּנָא בִּסְרָא:


The collection [of nationalities] among them began to have strong cravings [**make demands**], and Bnei Yisroel turned and began to weep; and they said “Who will feed us meat?


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:5)


דְּכִירִין אֲנַחְנָא יָת נוּנַיָּא דַּהֲוֵינָא אָכְלִין בְּמִצְרַיִם מַגָּן יָת בּוֹצִינַיָּא וְיָת אֲבַטִּיחַיָּא וּכְרָתֵי וּבוּצְלֵי וְתוּמֵי:


We remember the fish which we ate in Egypt freely; the cucumbers, watermelons, leeks, onions and garlic.


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:6)


וּכְעַן נַפְשָׁנָא תָאִיבָא לֵית כָּל מִדָּעַם אֶלָּהֵן לְמַנָּא עֵינָנָא:


Now our bodies are withered, there is nothing at all, but the manna before our eyes.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:7)


וּמַנָּא כְּבַר זְרַע גַּדָּא הוּא וְחֶזְוֵיהּ כְּחֵזוּ בְדֹלְחָא:


The manna was like coriander seed and it is similiar to the appearance of crystal.


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:8)


שַׁיְטִין עַמָּא וְלָקְטִין **דְּצָבֵי** טָחִין בְּרֵיחַיָּא אוֹ **דְצָבֵי** דָאִיךְ בַּמְּדֻכְתָּא וּמְבַשְּׁלִין לֵיהּ בְּקִדְרָא וְעָבְדִין יָתֵיהּ גְּרִיצָן וַהֲוָה טַעֲמֵיהּ כִּטְעֵם דְּלִישׁ בְּמִשְׁחָא:


The people [merely] strolled about and they gathered it and [**if they so desired,**] they ground it with a millstone or [**if they so desired,**] crushed it in mortars and cooked it in pots and made cakes out of it. Its taste was like the taste of cake kneaded with oil.


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:9)


וְכַד נָחֵית טַלָּא עַל מַשְׁרִיתָא לֵילְיָא נָחֵית מַנָּא עֲלוֹהִי:


When the dew would descend on the camp, at night, the manna would descend on it.


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:10)


וּשְׁמַע משֶׁה יָת עַמָּא בָּכַן לְזַרְעֲיַתְהוֹן גְּבַר בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכְּנֵיהּ וּתְקֵף רָגְזָא דַיְיָ לַחֲדָא וּבְעֵינֵי משֶׁה בִּישׁ:


Moshe heard the people weep according to their families, at the door of each one’s tent. The Anger of Adonoy was greatly aroused and in Moshe’s eyes, it was evil.


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:11)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה קֳדָם יְיָ לְמָא אַבְאֶשְׁתָּא לְעַבְדָּךְ וּלְמָא לָא אַשְׁכָּחִית רַחֲמִין קֳדָמָךְ לְשַׁוָּאָה יָת מְטוּל כָּל עַמָּא הָדֵין עָלָי:


Moshe said to Adonoy: “Why have you dealt poorly with Your servant, and why have I not found favor in Your eyes that You place the burden of the entire people upon me?


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:12)


**הֲאַב אֲנָא** לְכָל עַמָּא הָדֵין אִם **בְּנַי אִנּוּן** דְּאָמְרֵת לִי סוֹבַרְהִי **בְּתָקְפָּךְ** כְּמָא דִמְסוֹבַר תֻּרְבְּיָנָא יָת יַנְקָא עַל אַרְעָא דִּי קַיֶּמְתָּא לַאֲבָהָתוֹהִי:


Have I conceived [**am I a father to**] this entire people? have I given birth to it [**are they my children**] that You say to me, carry it [**with might**] in your lap as a nurse carries an infant, to the land which You swore to their forefathers?


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:13)


מְנַן לִי בִּסְרָא לְמֵיהַב לְכָל עַמָּא הָדֵין אֲרֵי בָּכַן עָלַי לְמֵימַר הַב לָנָא בִסְרָא וְנֵיכוּל:


From where do I have meat to give to this entire people on account of which they cry to me, saying: “Give us meat and we shall eat.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:14)


לֵית אֲנָא יָכִיל בִּלְחוֹדִי לְסוֹבָרָא יָת כָּל עַמָּא הָדֵין אֲרֵי יַקִּיר מִנִּי:


I alone can not carry this entire people, for they are too burdensome for me.


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:15)


וְאִם כְּדֵין אַתְּ עָבֵד לִי קְטָלְנִי כְעַן קְטוֹל אִם אַשְׁכָּחִית רַחֲמִין **קֳדָמָךְ** וְלָא אֶחֱזֵי בְּבִישְׁתִּי:


If this is the way You treat me, please kill me, if I have found favor in Your eyes [**before You**]; so that I will not see my evil.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:16)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה כְּנוֹשׁ **קֳדָמַי** שַׁבְעִין גַּבְרָא מִסָּבֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל דִּי יְדַעַתְּ אֲרֵי אִנּוּן סָבֵי עַמָּא וְסַרְכוֹהִי וּתְדַבַּר יָתְהוֹן לְמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְיִתְעַתְּדוּן תַּמָּן עִמָּךְ:


Adonoy said to Moshe: “Gather seventy men for [**before**] Me, from among the elders of Israel, [men] whom you know to be the people’s elders, and its officers, and you shall take them to the Tent of Meeting, and they shall stand there with you.


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:17)


**וְאֶתְגְּלֵי** וֶאֱמַלֵּל עִמָּךְ תַּמָּן וַאֲרַבֵּי מִן רוּחָא דַּעֲלָךְ וֶאֱשַׁוֵּי עֲלֵיהוֹן וִיסוֹבְרוּן עִמָּךְ בְּמַטּוּל עַמָּא וְלָא תְסוֹבַר אַתְּ בִּלְחוֹדָךְ:


I will descend [**appear**] and speak there with you, and I will make greater the spirit which is upon you, and place it on them, and they will bear, along with you, the burden of the people so that you need not bear it alone.


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:18)


וּלְעַמָּא תֵימַר אִזְדַּמְּנוּ לִמְחַר וְתֵיכְלוּן בִּסְרָא אֲרֵי בְּכֵיתוּן **קֳדָם** יְיָ לְמֵימַר מָן יֵכְלִנָּנָא בִּסְרָא אֲרֵי טַב לָנָא בְּמִצְרָיִם וְיִתֵּן יְיָ לְכוֹן בִּסְרָא וְתֵיכְלוּן:


You shall say to the people, prepare yourselves for the morrow and you shall eat meat, for you have cried in the ears of [**before**] Adonoy, saying: “Who will feed us meat; we had it good in Egypt;” [now] Adonoy gives you meat and you shall eat.


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:19)


לָא יוֹמָא חַד תֵּיכְלוּן וְלָא תְרֵין יוֹמִין וְלָא חַמְשָׁא יוֹמִין וְלָא עַשְׂרָא יוֹמִין וְלָא עַשְׂרִין יוֹמִין:


Not for one day will you be eating and not for two days; not for five days, not for ten days, and not for twenty days.


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:20)


עַד יְרַח יוֹמִין עַד דִּי **תְקוֹצוּן** בֵּיהּ וִיהֵי לְכוֹן לְתַקְלָא חֲלַף דְּקַצְתּוּן יָת **מֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ **דִּשְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ שַׁרְיַת** בֵּינֵיכוֹן וּבְכֵיתוּן קֳדָמוֹהִי לְמֵימַר לְמָא דְנַן נְפַקְנָא מִמִּצְרָיִם:


But rather for a month of days, until it comes out of your nostrils [**will disgust you**] and it will be repulsive to you; [this is] because you have rejected [**the word of**] Adonoy Who[**’s Shechinah dwells**] is among you, and you have cried before Him, saying: “Why have we come out from Egypt?”


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:21)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה שִׁית מְאָה אַלְפִין גַּבְרָא רִגְלָאָה עַמָּא דִּי אֲנָא בֵינֵיהוֹן וְאַתְּ אֲמַרְתְּ בִּסְרָא אֶתֵּן לְהוֹן וְיֵיכְלוּן יְרַח יוֹמִין:


Moshe said: “The people are six hundred thousand on foot that I am among and You say that I will give them meat, to eat it for a month of days?


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:22)


הֲעָן וְתוֹרִין יִתְנַכְּסוּן לְהוֹן הַיְסַפְּקוּן לְהוֹן אִם יָת כָּל נוּנֵי יַמָּא יִתְכַּנְּשׁוּן לְהוֹן הַיְסַפְּקוּן לְהוֹן:


Will sheep and cattle be slaughtered for them would that be sufficient for them? If all the fish of the sea would be gathered for them, would that be sufficient for them?”


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:23)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה **הֲמֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ **יִתְעַכָּב** כְּעַן תֶּחֱזֵי הַיְעַרְעִנָּךְ פִּתְגָּמִי אִם לָא:


Adonoy said to Moshe: “Has [**can**] the Hand [**word**] of Adonoy become short [**be restrained**]? Now you will see if My words will occur unto you or not.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:24)


וּנְפַק משֶׁה וּמַלִּיל לְעַמָּא יָת פִּתְגָּמַיָּא דַיְיָ וּכְנֵשׁ שַׁבְעִין גַּבְרָא מִסָּבֵי עַמָּא וַאֲקֵם יָתְהוֹן סְחוֹר סְחוֹר לְמַשְׁכְּנָא:


Moshe went out and spoke the words of Adonoy to the people, and he gathered seventy men from the elders of the people and he had them stand around the Tent.


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:25)


**וְאִתְגְּלִי** יְיָ בַּעֲנָנָא וּמַלִּיל עִמֵּיהּ וְרַבִּי מִן רוּחָא דִּי עֲלוֹהִי וִיהַב עַל שַׁבְעִין גַּבְרָא סָבַיָּא וַהֲוָה כַּד שְׁרַת עֲלֵיהוֹן רוּחָא **דִנְבוּאָה** וּמִתְנַבְּאִין וְלָא פַסְקִין:


Adonoy descended [**appeared**] in a cloud and spoke to him, and He magnified the spirit which was upon him and He gave it unto the seventy men, [who were] the elders. When the spirit [**of prophecy**] came to rest upon them they prophesied, and they did not cease.


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:26)


וְאִשְׁתָּאָרוּ תְּרֵין גֻּבְרִין בְּמַשְׁרִיתָא שׁוּם חָד אֶלְדָּד וְשׁוּם תִּנְיָנָא מֵידָד וּשְׁרַת עֲלֵיהוֹן רוּחָא **דִנְבוּאָה** וְאִנּוּן בִּכְתִיבַיָּא וְלָא נְפָקוּ לְמַשְׁכְּנָא וְאִתְנַבִּיאוּ בְּמַשְׁרִיתָא:


Two people remained in the camp; one’s name was Eldad and the second one’s name was Meidad. The spirit [**of prophecy**] rested upon them; they were among those inscribed, and they did not go out to the Tent, and they prophesied in the camp.


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:27)


וּרְהַט עוּלֵמָא וְחַוִּי לְמשֶׁה וַאֲמַר אֶלְדָּד וּמֵידָד מִתְנַבְּאִין בְּמַשְׁרִיתָא:


The youth ran and told Moshe, and said: “Eldad and Meidad are prophesying in the camp.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:28)


וַאֲתֵב יְהוֹשֻׁעַ בַּר נוּן מְשֻׁמְּשָׁנֵיהּ דְמשֶׁה מֵעוּלֵמוּתֵיהּ וַאֲמַר רִבּוֹנִי משֶׁה **אֲסָרִנּוּן**:


Yehoshua the son of Nun answered, [he being] the attendant of Moshe, from his youth, and he said: “My master Moshe, destroy [**bind**] them!”


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:29)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ משֶׁה הֲקִנְאָתִי אַתְּ מְקַנֵּי לִי רְעֵינָא פוֹן דִּיהוֹן כָּל עַמָּא דַיְיָ נְבִיאָין אֲרֵי יִתֵּן יְיָ יָת רוּחַ נְבוּאֲתֵיהּ עֲלֵיהוֹן:


Moshe said to him, “Are you vengeful for me? Would (that) it be that all Adonoy’s people were prophets, that Adonoy would give His Spirit upon them.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:30)


וְאִתְכְּנֵשׁ משֶׁה לְמַשְׁרִיתָא הוּא וְסָבֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


Moshe entered into the camp, he and the elders of Israel.


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:31)


וְרוּחָא נְטַל מִן **קֳדָם** יְיָ וְאַפְרַח שְׂלָיו מִן יַמָּא וּרְמָא עַל מַשְׁרִיתָא כְּמַהֲלַךְ יוֹמָא לְכָא וּכְמַהֲלַךְ יוֹמָא לְכָא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר לְמַשְׁרִיתָא וּכְרוּם תַּרְתֵּין אַמִּין עַל אַפֵּי אַרְעָא:


From [**before**] Adonoy there gusted a wind and He caused quail to fly from the sea and spread them over the camp, about a day’s journey from this side and about a day’s journey from that side, around the camp, and about two *amo¯s* above the ground’s surface.


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:32)


וְקָם עַמָּא כָּל יוֹמָא הַהוּא וְכָל לֵילְיָא וְכֹל יוֹמָא דְבַתְרוֹהִי וּכְנָשׁוּ יָת שְׂלָיו דְּאַזְעַר כְּנַשׁ עַשְׂרָא דְגוֹרִין וּשְׁטָחוּ לְהוֹן מַשְׁטִיחִין סְחוֹר סְחוֹר לְמַשְׁרִיתָא:


The people arose all of that day, the entire night and the entire next day, and they gathered the quail. He who gathered the least amount, gathered ten heaps and they spread them for themselves around the camp.


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:33)


בִּסְרָא עַד כְּעַן בֵּין שִׁנֵּיהוֹן עַד לָא פְסַק וְרָגְזָא דַיְיָ תְּקֵיף בְּעַמָּא וּקְטַל יְיָ בְּעַמָּא קְטוֹל סַגִּי לַחֲדָא:


The meat was still between their teeth before it was [even] finished, when the wrath of Adonoy was incited upon the people, and Adonoy smote the people an enormous blow.


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:34)


וּקְרָא יָת שְׁמָא דְאַתְרָא הַהוּא **קִבְרֵי דִמְשַׁאֲלֵי** אֲרֵי תַמָּן קְבָרוּ יָת עַמָּא דְּשָׁאִילוּ:


He called the name of that place Kivros Hataavoh [**Graves of the Demanders**], for it was there that they buried the people who had these cravings.


###### Onkelos Numbers 11:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/11:35)


מִקִּבְרֵי דִמְשַׁאֲלֵי נְטָלוּ עַמָּא לַחֲצֵרוֹת וַהֲווֹ בַּחֲצֵרוֹת:


From Kivros Hataavoh the people journeyed to Chatzeiros, and they stayed in Chatzeiros.



## Chapter 12

###### Onkelos Numbers 12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:1)


וּמַלֵּלַת מִרְיָם וְאַהֲרֹן בְּמשֶׁה עַל עֵסַק אִתְּתָא **שַׁפִּרְתָּא** דִּי נְסִיב אֲרֵי אִתְּתָא **שַׁפִּרְתָּא** דִנְסִיב **רָחִיק**:


Miriam and Aharon spoke about Moshe concerning the Cushite [**fair**] woman that he married, for, he married [**sent away**] a Cushite [**fair**] woman.


###### Onkelos Numbers 12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:2)


וַאֲמָרוּ הַלְחוֹד בְּרַם בְמשֶׁה מַלִּיל יְיָ הֲלָא אַף עִמָּנָא מַלִּיל **וּשְׁמִיעַ קֳדָם** יְיָ:


They said: “Is it only to Moshe that Adonoy has spoken? Did He not also speak with us?” and [**it was heard before**] Adonoy heard.


###### Onkelos Numbers 12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:3)


וְגַבְרָא משֶׁה עִנְוְתָן לַחֲדָא מִכֹּל אֱנָשָׁא דִּי עַל אַפֵּי אַרְעָא:


The man, Moshe [was] most humble, more so than any other person on the face of the earth.


###### Onkelos Numbers 12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:4)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ בִּתְכֵּף לְמשֶׁה וּלְאַהֲרֹן וּלְמִרְיָם פּוּקוּ תְלָתֵיכוֹן לְמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וּנְפָקוּ תְּלָתֵיהוֹן:


Adonoy said, suddenly, to Moshe, Aharon and Miriam: “Come out, all three of you, to the Tent of Meeting,” and the three of them went out.


###### Onkelos Numbers 12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:5)


**וְאִתְגְּלִי** יְיָ בְּעַמּוּדָא דַעֲנָנָא וְקָם בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכְּנָא וּקְרָא אַהֲרֹן וּמִרְיָם וּנְפָקוּ תַּרְוֵיהוֹן:


Adonoy descended [**appeared**] in a pillar of cloud and He stood at the entrance of the Tent, and He called [to] Aharon and Miriam, and both of them went out.


###### Onkelos Numbers 12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:6)


וַאֲמַר שְׁמָעוּ כְעַן פִּתְגָּמָי אִם יְהוֹן לְכוֹן נְבִיאִין אֲנָא יְיָ בְּחֶזְיָן אֲנָא **מִתְגְּלִי** לְהוֹן בְּחֶלְמִין אֲנָא מְמַלֵּל עִמְּהוֹן:


He said: “Please listen to My words. If there will be a prophet among you, [I,] Adonoy, will make [**reveal**] Myself known to him in a vision; in a dream I will speak to him.


###### Onkelos Numbers 12:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:7)


לָא כֵן עַבְדִּי משֶׁה בְּכָל בֵּיתִי מְהֵימָן הוּא:


That is not the case with My servant, Moshe; in My entire house he is trusted.


###### Onkelos Numbers 12:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:8)


**מַמְלַל עִם מַמְלַל** מַלֶּלְנָא עִמֵּיהּ בְּחֵזוּ וְלָא בְחִדְוָן וּדְמוּת **יְקָרָא** דַיְיָ מִסְתַּכָּל וּמָא דֵין לָא דְחֶלְתּוּן לְמַלָּלָא בְּעַבְדִּי בְמשֶׁה:


Mouth [**speaker**] to mouth [**speaker**] I speak to [**with**] him, in a vision and not in riddles; he gazes at the [**glorious**] likeness of Adonoy; so why are you not afraid to speak about My servant, about Moshe?”


###### Onkelos Numbers 12:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:9)


וּתְקִיף רָגְזָא דַיְיָ בְּהוֹן וְאִסְתַּלָּק:


Adonoy’s anger flared against them and He left.


###### Onkelos Numbers 12:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:10)


וַעֲנָנָא אִסְתַּלַּק מֵעִלָּוֵי מַשְׁכְּנָא וְהָא מִרְיָם חַוְּרָא כְּתַלְגָּא וְאִתְפְּנִי אַהֲרֹן לְוָת מִרְיָם וְהָא סְגִירָת:


The cloud departed from atop the Tent and behold! Miriam became afflicted with *tzora’as* white as snow; and Aharon turned towards Miriam and behold, she was afflicted with *tzora’as.*


###### Onkelos Numbers 12:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:11)


וַאֲמַר אַהֲרֹן לְמשֶׁה בְּבָעוּ רִבּוֹנִי לָא כְעַן תְּשַׁוִּי עֲלָנָא חוֹבָא דְּאִטְפַּשְׁנָא וְדִי סְרָחְנָא:


Aharon said to Moshe: “Oh, my master please do not put iniquity upon us for what we have foolishly done and for what we have transgressed.


###### Onkelos Numbers 12:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:12)


לָא כְעַן **תִּתְרַחַק דָּא מִבֵּינָנָא אֲרֵי אֲחָתָנָא הִיא צַלִּי כְעַן עַל בִּסְרָא מִיתָא הָדֵין דִּי בַהּ וְיִתַּסִּי**:


Please let her not be like the dead [**separated from among us for she is our sister**]; That, since he came from his mother’s womb, it would be as half of his own flesh was consumed [**pray now for this dead flesh which is in her, so she will be healed**].”


###### Onkelos Numbers 12:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:13)


וְצַלִּי משֶׁה קֳדָם יְיָ לְמֵימָר אֱלָהָא בְּבָעוּ אַסֵּי כְעַן יָתָהּ:


Moshe cried out [in prayer] to Adonoy, saying: “Please, God, please heal her.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 12:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:14)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה וְאִלּוּ אָבוּהַהּ **מִנְזַף נְזִיף** בַּהּ הֲלָא תִתְכְּלֵם שַׁבְעָא יוֹמִין תִּסְתְּגַר שַׁבְעָא יוֹמִין מִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא וּבָתַר כֵּן תִּתְכְּנֵשׁ:


Adonoy said to Moshe: “If her father were to spit in her face [**strongly chastise her**] would she not be in shame for seven days? She shall be closed off for seven days, beyond the encampment, and after that, she may join again.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 12:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:15)


וְאִסְתְּגָרַת מִרְיָם מִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא שַׁבְעָא יוֹמִין וְעַמָּא לָא נְטַל עַד דְּאִתְכְּנֵשַׁת מִרְיָם:


Miriam was closed off beyond the encampment for seven days, and the people did not depart until Miriam was brought in to join them.


###### Onkelos Numbers 12:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/12:16)


וּבָתַר כֵּן נְטָלוּ עַמָּא מֵחֲצֵרוֹת וּשְׁרוֹ בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְפָארָן:


After that, the people traveled from Chatzeiros and they camped in the desert of Paran.



## Chapter 13

###### Onkelos Numbers 13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:1)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


And Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying;


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:2)


שְׁלַח לָךְ גֻּבְרִין וִיאַלְּלוּן יָת אַרְעָא דִכְנַעַן דִּי אֲנָא יָהֵב לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל גַּבְרָא חַד גַּבְרָא חַד לְשִׁבְטָא דַאֲבָהָתוֹהִי תְּשַׁלְּחוּן כֹּל רַבָּא דִבְהוֹן:


“Send, for yourself, men, and have them scout the Land of Cana’an, which I am giving to Bnei Yisroel; one man, one man each, for his father’s tribe you shall send them, each leader among them.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:3)


וּשְׁלַח יָתְהוֹן משֶׁה מִמַּדְבְּרָא דְפָארָן עַל מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ כֻּלְּהוֹן גֻּבְרִין רֵישֵׁי בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אִנּוּן:


Moshe sent them from the desert of Paran by the word of Adonoy; all of them [were] men of distinction, they were the heads of Bnei Yisroel.


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:4)


וְאִלֵּין שְׁמָהַתְהוֹן לְשִׁבְטָא דִרְאוּבֵן שַׁמּוּעַ בַּר זַכּוּר:


These are their names; for the tribe of Reuven, Shamua the son of Zaccur.


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:5)


לְשִׁבְטָא דְשִׁמְעוֹן שָׁפָט בַּר חוֹרִי:


For the tribe of Shimon, Shafat the son of Chori.


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:6)


לְשִׁבְטָא דִיהוּדָה כָּלֵב בַּר יְפֻנֶּה:


For the tribe of Yehudah, Caleiv the son of Yefuneh.


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:7)


לְשִׁבְטָא דְּיִשָּׂשכָר יִגְאָל בַּר יוֹסֵף:


For the tribe of Yissachar, Yigal the son of Yoseif.


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:8)


לְשִׁבְטָא דְאֶפְרַיִם הוֹשֵׁעַ בַּר נוּן:


For the tribe of Ephraim, Hosheiah the son of Nun.


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:9)


לְשִׁבְטָא דְבִנְיָמִן פַּלְטִי בַּר רָפוּא:


For the tribe of Binyamin, Palti the son of Raphu.


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:10)


לְשִׁבְטָא דִזְבוּלֻן גַּדִּיאֵל בַּר סוֹדִי:


For the tribe of Zevulun, Gadiel the son of Sodi.


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:11)


לְשִׁבְטָא דְיוֹסֵף לְשִׁבְטָא דִמְנַשֶּׁה גַּדִּי בַּר סוּסִי:


For the tribe of Yoseif, for the tribe of Menashe, Gadi the son of Susi.


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:12)


לְשִׁבְטָא דְדָן עַמִּיאֵל בַּר גְּמַלִּי:


For the tribe of Don, Amiel the son of Gemali.


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:13)


לְשִׁבְטָא דְאָשֵׁר סְתוּר בַּר מִיכָאֵל:


For the tribe of Asher, Sesur the son of Michael.


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:14)


לְשִׁבְטָא דְנַפְתָּלִי נַחְבִּי בַּר וָפְסִי:


For the tribe of Naftali, Nachbi the son of Vofsi.


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:15)


לְשִׁבְטָא דְגָד גְּאוּאֵל בַּר מָכִי:


For the tribe of Gad, Geu’el the son of Machi.


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:16)


אִלֵּין שְׁמָהַת גֻּבְרַיָּא דִּי שְׁלַח משֶׁה לְאַלָּלָא יָת אַרְעָא וּקְרָא משֶׁה לְהוֹשֵׁעַ בַּר נוּן יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


These are the names of the men whom Moshe sent to scout the land, and Moshe called Hosheiah the son of Nun, Yehoshua.


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:17)


וּשְׁלַח יָתְהוֹן משֶׁה לְאַלָּלָא יָת אַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן סָקוּ דָא בִּדְרוֹמָא וְתִסְקוּן לְטוּרָא:


Moshe sent them to scout the land of Cana’an and he said to them: “Go up this [way], to the south and go up to the mountain.


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:18)


וְתֶחֱזוּן יָת אַרְעָא מָא הִיא וְיָת עַמָּא דְּיָתֵב עֲלַהּ הֲתַקִּיף הוּא אִם חַלָּשׁ הַזְעֵר הוּא אִם סַגִּי:


See what the land is; and the people living on it; are they strong or weak, if they are few or many;


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:19)


וּמָא אַרְעָא דִּי הוּא יָתֵב בַּהּ הֲטָבָא הִיא אִם בִּישָׁא וּמָא קִרְוַיָּא דִּי הוּא יָתֵב בְּהוֹן הַבְּפַצְחִין אִם **בִּכְרַכִּין**:


and how is the land in which they live, is it good, or bad? And how are the cities in which they reside; are they open, or are they fortified [**walled-in**]?


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:20)


וּמָא אַרְעָא הֲעַתִּירָא הִיא אִם מִסְכֵּנָא הֲאִית בַּהּ אִילָנִין אִם לָא וְתִתְקְפוּן וְתִסְּבוּן מֵאִבָּא דְאַרְעָא וְיוֹמַיָּא יוֹמֵי בִּכּוּרֵי עִנְּבִין:


How is the land (soil) is it fat (rich) or lean (poor), does it have trees, or not? You shall strengthen yourselves, and you shall take some fruit of the land.” Those days were the days of the first ripening of the grapes.


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:21)


וּסְלִיקוּ וְאַלִּילוּ יָת אַרְעָא מִמַּדבְּרָא דְצִין עַד רְחוֹב לִמְטֵי חֲמָת:


They went up and they scouted the land from the desert of Tzin until Rechov, at the entrance of Chamas.


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:22)


וּסְלִיקוּ בִדְרוֹמָא וַאֲתָא עַד חֶבְרוֹן וְתַמָּן אֲחִימָן שֵׁשַׁי וְתַלְמַי בְּנֵי גִּבָּרַ**יָּא** וְחֶבְרוֹן שְׁבַע שְׁנִין אִתְבְּנִיאַת קֳדָם **טָנַס** דְּמִצְרָיִם:


They went up, into the southern part [of the land] and [he] came to Chevron; and there, were Achiman, Sheshai and Talmai, the offspring of the giant[**s**]. [The city of] Chevron was built seven years before the city of Tzo’an [**Tonas**] in Egypt.


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:23)


וַאֲתוֹ עַד נַחֲלָא דְאַתְכָּלָא וּקְצוֹ מִתַּמָּן עוֹבַרְתָּא וְאַתְכַּל עִנְּבִין חַד וּנְטָלוֹהִי בַאֲרִיחָא בִּתְרֵין וּמִן רִמּוֹנַיָּא וּמִן תְּאֵנַיָּא:


They came until the Valley of Eshkol, and from there they cut off a branch and one cluster of grapes, and they carried it between two on a pole, [and they took] some pomegranates and some figs.


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:24)


לְאַתְרָא הַהוּא קְרָא נַחְלָא דְאַתְכָּלָא עַל עֵסַק אַתְכָּלָא דִּקְצוֹ מִתַּמָּן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


That place they called the Valley of Eshkol, because of the event of the cluster that Bnei Yisroel cut from there.


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:25)


וְתָבוּ מִלְּאַלָּלָא יָת אַרְעָא מִסּוֹף אַרְבְּעִין יוֹמִין:


They returned from spying the land, at the end of forty days.


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:26)


וַאֲזָלוּ וַאֲתוֹ לְוַת משֶׁה וּלְוָת אַהֲרֹן וּלְוַת כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמַדְבְּרָא דְפָארָן **לִרְקָם** וַאֲתִיבוּ יָתְהוֹן פִּתְגָּמָא וְיָת כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא וְאַחֲזִיאֻנּוּן יָת אִבָּא דְאַרְעָא:


They went and came to Moshe and Aharon and to the entire congregation of Bnei Yisroel [that came] to the desert of Paran, in Kadeish [**to Rekom**]. They brought word back to them, and to the entire congregation, and they showed them the fruit of the land.


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:27)


וְאִשְׁתָּעִיאוּ לֵיהּ וַאֲמָרוּ אֲתֵינָא לְאַרְעָא דִּי שְׁלַחְתָּנָא וְאַף עָבְדָא חֲלַב וּדְבַשׁ הִיא וְדֵין אִבַּהּ:


They recounted to him, and they said: “We came into the land into which you sent us, and it indeed flows with milk and honey, and this is its fruit.


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:28)


לְחוֹד אֲרֵי תַקִּיף עַמָּא דְּיָתֵב בְּאַרְעָא וְקִרְוַיָּא כְּרִיכַן רַבְרְבַן לַחֲדָא וְאַף בְּנֵי גִבָּרַ**יָּא** חֲזֵינָא תַמָּן:


However, the nation is mighty, those who inhabit the land, and the cities are greatly fortified to the utmost, and we also saw the offspring of the giant[**s**] over there.


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:29)


עֲמַלְקָאָה יָתֵב בַּאֲרַע דָּרוֹמָא וְחִתָּאָה וִיבוּסָאָה וֶאֱמוֹרָאָה יָתֵב בְּטוּרָא וּכְנַעֲנָאָה יָתֵב עַל יַמָּא וְעַל כֵּיף יַרְדְּנָא:


Amalek dwells in the southern part of the land, the Chiti, Yevusi and Emori dwell in the mountain and the Cana’ani dwell by the sea and next to the Yardein.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:30)


וְאַצֵּית כָּלֵב יָת עַמָּא לְמשֶׁה וַאֲמַר מֵיסַק נֵיסַק וְנִירַת יָתַהּ אֲרֵי מֵיכַל נֵיכוּל לַהּ:


Caleiv silenced the people to [hear about] Moshe, and he said: “We can surely go up [to the land] and we shall possess it for we are surely able to overcome it.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 13:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/13:31)

But the men who went up with him said: “We are not able to go up against the nation, for they are more powerful than we.”

There we saw the giants, the sons of the giant [**Anok**], of the Nephilim [**giants**], and we were like grasshoppers in our [**own**] eyes, and so we appeared in their eyes.”

###### Onkelos Numbers 14:1

The entire community arose and raised their voices; and the people wept that night.

They railed against Moshe and Aharon—all of Bnei Yisroel—and the entire congregation said to them: “Would we had died in the land of Egypt or in this desert, would that we had died.

They said to each other: “Let us appoint (lit. place) a head and we will return to Egypt.”

Adonoy, slow to [**distances**] anger and abounding in [**performing**] kindness, forgiving iniquity, [**rebellion**] and transgression; Who does not completely acquit [**pardoning those who return to His Torah, but to those who do not return, He does not acquit**], Who will visit the inquities of the fathers upon their [**rebellious**] children, to the third [generation] and to the fourth [generation.]

###### Onkelos Numbers 14:26

וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה וְעִם אַהֲרֹן לְמֵימַר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe and to Aharon, saying:


###### Onkelos Numbers 14:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:27)


עַד אֵימָתַי לִכְנִשְׁתָּא בִישְׁתָא הָדָא דִּי אִנּוּן מִתְרַעֲמִין עֲלָי יָת תֻּרְעֲמַת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל דִּי אִנּוּן מִתְרַעֲמִין עֲלַי שְׁמִיעַ **קֳדָמָי**:


“Until when will this wicked congregation [exist], those who provoke Me? The complaints of Bnei Yisroel, which they complain against Me, I have heard [**before Me**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 14:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:28)


אֱמַר לְהוֹן קַיָּם אֲנָא אֲמַר יְיָ אִם לָא כְּמָא דִי מַלֶּלְתּוּן **קֳדָמָי** כֵּן אֶעְבֵּד לְכוֹן:


Say to them: “As I live, says Adonoy, if I do not [do to you] as you have spoken in My ears [**before Me**]; so I shall do to you.


###### Onkelos Numbers 14:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:29)


בְּמַדְבְּרָא הָדֵין יִפְּלוּן פִּגְרֵיכוֹן וְכָל מִנְיָנֵיכוֹן לְכָל חֻשְׁבַּנְכוֹן מִבַּר עַשְׂרִין שְׁנִין וּלְעֵלָּא דִּי אִתְרַעַמְתּוּן עֲלָי:


In this desert your corpses shall fall, all of you who were counted according to your numbers, from twenty years and above, because you have complained against Me,


###### Onkelos Numbers 14:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:30)


אִם אַתּוּן תֵּעֲלוּן לְאַרְעָא דִּי **קַיֵּמִית בְּמֵימְרִי** לְאַשְׁרָאָה יָתְכוֹן בַּהּ אֶלָּהֵן כָּלֵב בַּר יְפֻנֶּה וִיהוֹשֻׁעַ בַּר נוּן:


Surely you shall [not] come into the land to which I raised My Hand [to swear] [**sworn by My word**] that I would cause you to live in it; except for Caleiv the son of Yefuneh and Yehoshua the son of Nun.


###### Onkelos Numbers 14:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:31)


וְטַפְלְכוֹן דִּי אֲמַרְתּוּן לְבִזָּא יְהֵי וְאָעֵל יָתְהוֹן וְיִדְּעוּן יָת אַרְעָא דִּי קַצְתּוּן בַּהּ:


Your infants, about whom you said that they will be as spoils, I shall bring them [there] and they will know the land which you despised.


###### Onkelos Numbers 14:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:32)


וּפִגְרֵיכוֹן דִּי לְכוֹן יִפְּלוּן בְּמַדְבְּרָא הָדֵין:


Your corpses—yours, shall fall in this desert.


###### Onkelos Numbers 14:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:33)


וּבְנֵיכוֹן יְהוֹן **מְאַחֲרִין** בְּמַדְבְּרָא אַרְבְּעִין שְׁנִין וִיקַבְּלוּן יָת חוֹבֵיכוֹן עַד דִּיסוּפוּן פִּגְרֵיכוֹן בְּמַדְבְּרָא:


Your children, they shall be wanderers [**delayed**] in the desert for forty years, and they will bear [the burden of] your dissoluteness, until the last of your corpses are in the desert.


###### Onkelos Numbers 14:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:34)


בְּמִנְיַן יוֹמַיָּא דִּי אַלֶּלְתּוּן יָת אַרְעָא אַרְבְּעִין יוֹמִין יוֹמָא לְשַׁתָּא יוֹמָא לְשַׁתָּא תְּקַבְּלוּן יָת חוֹבֵיכוֹן אַרְבְּעִין שְׁנִין וְתִדְּעוּן יָת **דְּאִתְרַעַמְתּוּן עֲלָי**:


According to the number of days which you scouted the land; forty days, a day for a year, a day for a year you will bear the burden of your iniquity forty years, and you will know My displeasure [**that you have murmured against Me**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 14:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:35)


אֲנָא יְיָ **גְּזָרִית בְּמֵימְרִי** אִם לָא דָּא אֶעְבֵּד לְכָל כְּנִשְׁתָּא בִישְׁתָא הָדָא דְּאִזְדַּמְּנוּן עֲלָי בְּמַדְבְּרָא הָדֵין יְסוּפוּן וְתַמָּן יְמוּתוּן:


I am Adonoy; I have spoken—[**decreed by My word**]—if I do not do this to this entire evil congregation that are gathered against Me; in this desert they shall be finished and there they shall die.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 14:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:36)


וְגֻבְרַיָּא דִּי שְׁלַח משֶׁה לְאַלָּלָא יָת אַרְעָא וְתָבוּ וְאַרְעִימוּ עֲלוֹהִי יָת כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא לְאַפָּקָא שׁוּם בִּישׁ עַל אַרְעָא:


Those men whom Moshe sent to scout the land, who returned and provoked the complaints of the entire congregation against him, by spreading slander about the land;


###### Onkelos Numbers 14:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:37)


וּמִיתוּ גֻּבְרַיָּא דְּאַפִּיקוּ שׁוּם בִּישׁ עַל אַרְעָא בְּמוֹתָנָא קֳדָם יְיָ:


Those men died—who spread evil slander against the land—in a plague, before Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Numbers 14:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:38)


וִיהוֹשֻׁעַ בַּר נוּן וְכָלֵב בַּר יְפֻנֶּה אִתְקַיְּמוּ מִן גֻּבְרַיָּא הָאִנּוּן דַּאֲזָלוּ לְאַלָּלָא יָת אַרְעָא:


Yehoshua the son of Nun and Caleiv the son of Yefuneh, lived, from among those men who went to scout the land.


###### Onkelos Numbers 14:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:39)


וּמַלִּיל משֶׁה יָת פִּתְגָּמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין לְכָל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְאִתְאַבָּלוּ עַמָּא לַחֲדָא:


Moshe spoke these words to all of Bnei Yisroel, and the people mourned greatly.


###### Onkelos Numbers 14:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:40)


וְאַקְדִּימוּ בְצַפְרָא וּסְלִיקוּ לְרֵישׁ טוּרָא לְמֵימַר הָא אֲנַחְנָא סָלְקִין לְאַתְרָא דִּי אֲמַר יְיָ אֲרֵי חַבְנָא:


They awoke early in the morning and went up to the peak of the mountain, saying: “We are ready to go up to the place of which Adonoy spoke (promised), for we have sinned.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 14:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:41)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְמָא דְנַן אַתּוּן עָבְרִין עַל **גְּזֵרַת** מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ וְהִיא לָא תַצְלַח:


Moshe said: “Why are you now transgressing the word [**decree**] of Adonoy; it will not succeed.


###### Onkelos Numbers 14:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:42)


לָא תִסְּקוּן אֲרֵי לֵית שְׁכִנְתָּא דַיְיָ בֵּינֵיכוֹן וְלָא תִתַּבְּרוּן קֳדָם בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבֵיכוֹן:


Do not go up, for Adonoy is not among you, and do not die before your enemies,


###### Onkelos Numbers 14:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:43)
