אֲרֵי עֲמַלְקָאָה וּכְנַעֲנָאָה תַמָּן קֳדָמֵיכוֹן וְתִפְּלוּן בְּחַרְבָּא אֲרֵי עַל כֵּן תַּבְתּוּן מִבָּתַר **פָּלְחָנָא** דַיְיָ וְלָא יְהֵי **מֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ **בְּסַעְדְּכוֹן**:


For the Amaleki and the Cana’ani are there before you, and you will fall by the sword, for, you turned from following [**the service of**] Adonoy, and [**the word of**] Adonoy will not be with you [**to your aid**].”


###### Onkelos Numbers 14:44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:44)


וְאַרְשָׁעוּ לְמִסַּק לְרֵישׁ טוּרָא וַאֲרוֹן קְיָמָא דַיְיָ וּמשֶׁה לָא עֲדוֹ מִגּוֹ מַשְׁרִיתָא:


They defiantly went up the mountain peak, but the Ark of the Covenant of Adonoy, and Moshe, did not move from amidst the encampment.


###### Onkelos Numbers 14:45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/14:45)


וּנְחַת עֲמַלְקָאָה וּכְנַעֲנָאָה דְּיָתֵב בְּטוּרָא הַהוּא וּמְחֻנּוּן **וּטְרָדֻנּוּן** עַד חָרְמָה:


The Amaleki and the Cana’ani swooped down, they who were dwelling in that mountain, and they smote and crushed [**persued**] them until Chormah.



## Chapter 15

###### Onkelos Numbers 15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:1)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימַר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying;


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:2)


מַלֵּל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן אֲרֵי תֵעֲלוּן לַאֲרַע מוֹתְבָנֵיכוֹן דִּי אֲנָא יָהֵב לְכוֹן:


“Speak to Bnei Yisroel, and say to them, when you arrive into the land in which you will dwell, which I am giving to you.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:3)


וְתַעְבְּדוּן **קֻרְבָּנָא** קֳדָם יְיָ עֲלָתָא אוֹ נִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָּא לְאַפְרָשָׁא נִדְרָא אוֹ בִנְדַבְתָּא אוֹ בְּמוֹעֲדֵיכוֹן לְמֶעְבַּד **לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְרַעֲוָא קֳדָם** יְיָ מִן תּוֹרֵי אוֹ מִן עָנָא:


When you make a fire-offering [**sacrifice**] to Adonoy, a burnt-offering or a sacrifice, by verbalizing as a pledge or as a donation or on your festivals to provide a pleasing fragrance to [**to be accepted with pleasure before**] Adonoy, from cattle or from sheep.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:4)


וִיקָרֵב דִּמְקָרֵב קֻרְבָּנֵיהּ קֳדָם יְיָ מִנְחָתָא סֻלְתָּא עַשְׂרוֹנָא דְּפִילָא בְּרַבְעוּת הִינָא מִשְׁחָא:


The one bringing his offering to Adonoy shall present one tenth of flour mixed with one fourth of a *hin* of oil,


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:5)


וְחַמְרָא לְנִסְכָּא רַבְעוּת הִינָא תַּעְבֵּד עַל עֲלָתָא אוֹ לְנִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָּא לְאִמְּרָא חָד:


Along with wine, for a wine-offering, one fourth of a *hin,* so you shall do for a burnt-offering or a sacrifice, for the one sheep.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:6)


אוֹ לְדִכְרָא תַּעְבֵּד מִנְחָתָא סֻלְתָּא תְּרֵין עַשְׂרוֹנִין דְּפִילָא בִמְשַׁח תַּלְתּוּת הִינָא:


If for a ram you shall make a meal-offering of two tenths, mixed with one third of a *hin* of oil.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:7)


וְחַמְרָא לְנִסְכָּא תַּלְתּוּת הִינָא תְּקָרֵב **לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְרַעֲוָא קֳדָם** יְיָ:


Wine, for a wine-offering shall be one third of a *hin.* This is how you shall offer a pleasing fragrance to [**to be accepted with pleasure before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:8)


וַאֲרֵי תַעְבֵּד בַּר תּוֹרֵי עֲלָתָא אוֹ נִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָּא לְפָרָשָׁא נִדְרָא אוֹ נִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָּא קֳדָם יְיָ:


If you are making a calf as a burnt-offering or sacrifice by verbalizing as a pledge or for a peace-offering, to Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:9)


וִיקָרֵב עַל בַּר תּוֹרֵי מִנְחָתָא סֻלְתָּא תְּלָתָא עַשְׂרוֹנִין דְּפִילָא בִמְשַׁח פַּלְגוּת הִינָא:


Along with the calf he shall bring a meal-offering of three tenths mixed with one half of a *hin* of oil.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:10)


וְחַמְרָא תְּקָרֵב לְנִסְכָּא פַּלְגוּת הִינָא קֻרְבַּן **דְּמִתְקַבֵּל בְּרַעֲוָא קֳדָם** יְיָ:


You shall bring wine, for a wine-offering, one half of a *hin,* a fire-offering of pleasing fragrance to [**which will be accepted with pleasure before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:11)


כְּדֵין יִתְעֲבֵד לְתוֹרָא חַד אוֹ לְדִכְרָא חָד אוֹ לְאִמַּר בְּאִמְּרַיָּא אוֹ בְעִזַּיָּא:


So you shall do for each ox or ram or for the sheep or goats.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:12)


כְּמִנְיָן דִּי תַעְבְּדוּן כְּדֵין תַּעְבְּדוּן לְחַד כְּמִנְיַנְהוֹן:


According to the number, which you offer, so you shall make for each one according to the[ir] number[s].


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:13)


כָּל יַצִּיבָא יַעְבֵּד כְּדֵין יָת אִלֵּין לְקָרָבָא קֻרְבַּן **דְּמִתְקַבֵּל בְּרַעֲוָא קֳדָם** יְיָ:


Each citizen shall do this as stated, to bring a fire-offering of pleasing fragrance to [**which will be accepted with pleasure before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:14)


וַאֲרֵי יִתְגַּיַּר עִמְּכוֹן גִּיּוֹרָא אוֹ דִי בֵינֵיכוֹן לְדָרֵיכוֹן וְיַעְבֵּד קֻרְבַּן **דְּמִתְקַבֵּל בַּרַעֲוָא קֳדָם** יְיָ כְּמָא דִי תַעְבְּדוּן כֵּן יַעְבֵּד:


If a proselyte will live with you or among you, for all of your generations and makes a fire-offering of pleasing fragrance to [**which will be accepted with pleasure before**] Adonoy, in the same fashion as you make it, so shall he make it.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:15)


קְהָלָא קְיָמָא חַד לְכוֹן וּלְגִיּוֹרָא דְיִתְגַּיָּר קְיַם עָלָם לְדָרֵיכוֹן כְּוָתְכוֹן כְּגִיּוֹרָא יְהֵי קֳדָם יְיָ:


The assembly has one statute for yourselves and for the proselyte who resides; one statue for all of your generations, just as it is for yourselves, so shall it be for the proselyte, before Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:16)


אוֹרַיְתָא חֲדָא וְדִינָא חַד יְהֵי לְכוֹן וּלְגִיּוֹרָא דְּיִתְגַּיַּר עִמְּכוֹן:


One law and one rule will apply to you, and to the proselyte who lives with you.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:17)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:18)


מַלֵּל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן בְּמֵעַלְכוֹן לְאַרְעָא דִּי אֲנָא מָעֵל יָתְכוֹן לְתַמָּן:


“Speak to Bnei Yisroel and say to them, at your coming to the land into which I am bringing you,


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:19)


וִיהֵי בְּמֵיכַלְכוֹן מִלַּחְמָא דְאַרְעָא תַּפְרְשׁוּן **אַפְרָשׁוּתָא** קֳדָם יְיָ:


When you eat from the bread of the land you shall separate a *terumah* for [**a separation before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:20)


רֵישׁ אַצְוַתְכוֹן חַלְּתָא תַּפְרְשׁוּן אַפְרָשׁוּתָא כְּמָא דִי מַפְרְשִׁין מִן אִדְרָא כֵּן תַּפְרְשׁוּן יָתַהּ:


[From] the first portion of your doughs you shall separate a loaf as a separated portion; like the *terumah*-gift of the granary, so shall you separate it.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:21)


מֵרֵישׁ אַצְוַתְכוֹן תִּתְּנוּן **קֳדָם** יְיָ אַפְרָשׁוּתָא לְדָרֵיכוֹן:


Of the first portion of your doughs you shall give to [**before**] Adonoy as a *terumah*- offering, throughout your generations.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:22)


וַאֲרֵי תִשְׁתְּלוּן וְלָא תַעְבְּדוּן יָת כָּל פִּקּוֹדַיָּא הָאִלֵּין דִּי מַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה:


If you should err, and not fulfill all of these commandments which Adonoy said to Moshe;


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:23)


יָת כָּל דִּי פַקִּיד יְיָ יָתְכוֹן בִּידָא דְמשֶׁה מִן יוֹמָא דִּי פַקִּיד יְיָ וּלְהַלָּא לְדָרֵיכוֹן:


All that Adonoy commanded you through Moshe, from the day on which Adonoy commanded, and from then on, throughout your generations.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:24)


וִיהֵי אִם מֵעֵינֵי כְנִשְׁתָּא אִתְעֲבֵדַת לְשָׁלוּ וְיַעְבְּדוּן כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא תּוֹר בַּר תּוֹרֵי חַד לַעֲלָתָא **לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְרַעֲוָא קֳדָם** יְיָ וּמִנְחָתֵיהּ וְנִסְכֵּיהּ כִּדְחָזֵי וּצְפִיר בַּר עִזֵּי חַד לְחַטָּאתָא:


If it was obscured from the eyes of the congregation, having been done in error, then the entire congregation shall offer one young bullock as a burnt-offering for a pleasing fragrance to [**to be accepted with pleasure before**] Adonoy. Its meal-offering and its wine-offering are according to its law, and one he-goat, as a sin-offering.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:25)


וִיכַפַּר כַּהֲנָא עַל כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא דִּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְיִשְׁתְּבֵק לְהוֹן אֲרֵי שָׁלוּתָא הִיא וְאִנּוּן אַיְתִיאוּ יָת קֻרְבַּנְהוֹן קֻרְבָּנָא קֳדָם יְיָ וְחוֹבַתְהוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ עַל שָׁלוּתְהוֹן:


The *kohein* shall atone on behalf of the entire congregation of Bnei Yisroel, and they shall be forgiven; for they have erred unwittingly and they have brought their offering, as a fire-offering to Adonoy, and their sin-offering before Adonoy (to atone) for their error.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:26)


וְיִשְׁתְּבֵק לְכָל כְּנִשְׁתָּא דִּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וּלְגִיּוֹרָא דְּיִתְגַּיַּר בֵּינֵיהוֹן אֲרֵי לְכָל עַמָּא בְּשָׁלוּתָא:


And it shall be forgiven to the entire congregation of Bnei Yisroel and the stranger amongst them; for the entire people sinned unintentionally.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:27)


וְאִם אֱנַשׁ חַד יֵחוֹב בְּשָׁלוּ וִיקָרֵב עִזָּא בַּת שַׁתַּהּ לְחַטָּאתָא:


If one person will sin erroneously, that person shall bring a yearling she-goat as a sin-offering.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:28)


וִיכַפַּר כַּהֲנָא עַל אֱנַשׁ דְּאִשְׁתְּלִי בְּמֵחֲבֵיהּ בְּשָׁלוּ קֳדָם יְיָ לְכַפָּרָא עֲלוֹהִי וְיִשְׁתְּבֵק לֵיהּ:


The *kohein* shall atone for that erring soul which has sinned unwittingly before Adonoy; to achieve atonement for him, and he will be forgiven.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:29)


יַצִּיבָא בִּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וּלְגִיּוֹרָא דְּיִתְגַּיַּר בֵּינֵיהוֹן אוֹרַיְתָא חֲדָא יְהֵי לְכוֹן לִדְיַעְבֵּד בְּשָׁלוּ


[For] the citizen among Bnei Yisroel, and the proselyte who dwells among them there shall be one law for you all, for he who sins unwittingly.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:30)


וֶאֱנַשׁ דְּיַעְבֵּד **בְּרֵישׁ גְּלִי** מִן יַצִּיבַיָּא וּמִן גִּיּוֹרַיָּא קֳדָם יְיָ הוּא **מַרְגֵּז** וְיִשְׁתֵּצֵי אֲנָשָׁא הַהִיא מִגּוֹ עַמֵּיהּ:


A person who commits a sin high-handedly [**publicly**] among the citizens or among the proselytes, blasphemes [**provokes before**] Adonoy; the soul of that person shall be cut off from among its people.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:31)


אֲרֵי עַל מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ בְּסַר וְיָת פִּקּוֹדוֹהִי אַשְׁנֵי אִשְׁתֵּצָאָה תִשְׁתֵּצֵי אֲנָשָׁא הַהִיא חוֹבַהּ בַּהּ:


For he has denigrated the word of Adonoy and he has violated His commandment; The soul of that person shall certainly be cut off; its iniquity is upon it.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:32)


וַהֲווֹ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּמַדְבְּרָא וְאַשְׁכָּחוּ גַּבְרָא כַּד מְגַבַּב אָעִין בְּיוֹמָא דְשַׁבְּתָא:


Bnei Yisroel were in the desert, and they found a man gathering wood on the day of the Shabbos.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:33)


וְקָרִיבוּ יָתֵיהּ דְּאַשְׁכָּחוּ יָתֵיהּ כַּד מְגַבַּב אָעִין לְוָת משֶׁה וּלְוָת אַהֲרֹן וּלְוָת כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא:


Those who found him brought him near—he who was gathering wood—to Moshe and to Aharon, and to the entire congregation.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:34)


**וְאַסְּרוּ** יָתֵיהּ בְּבֵית מַטְּרָא אֲרֵי לָא אִתְפְּרַשׁ לְהוֹן מָה דְיַעְבְּדוּן לֵיהּ:


They placed [**bound**] him under guard since it was not specified, what was to be done to him.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:35)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵל גַּבְרָא מִרְגַּם יָתֵיהּ בְּאַבְנַיָּא כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא מִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא:


Adonoy said to Moshe: “That man shall be put to death, the entire congregation shall stone him beyond the encampment.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:36)


וְאַפִּיקוּ יָתֵיהּ כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא לְמִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא וּרְגָמוּ יָתֵיהּ בְּאַבְנַיָּא וּמִית כְּמָא דִי פַקִיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה:


The entire congregation brought him outside beyond the encampment, and they stoned him with stones, and he died, just as Adonoy commanded Moshe.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:37)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


And Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:38)


מַלֵּל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן וְיַעְבְּדוּן לְהוֹן כְּרוּסְפְּדִין עַל כַּנְפֵי כְסוּתְהוֹן לְדָרֵיהוֹן וְיִתְּנוּן עַל כְּרוּסְפְּדָא דְכַנְפָא חוּטָא דִתְכֶלְתָּא:


Speak to Bnei Yisroel and tell them to make for themselves, *tzizis*-fringes on the corners of their garments—throughout their generations; and they will place with the fringes of each corner a thread of blue.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:39)


וִיהוֹן לְכוֹן לִכְרוּסְפְּדִין וְתֶחֱזוּן יָתֵיהּ וְתִדְכְּרוּן יָת כָּל פִּקּוּדַיָּא דַיְיָ וְתַעְבְּדוּן יָתְהוֹן וְלָא **תִטְּעוּן** בָּתַר הִרְהוּר לִבְּכוֹן וּבָתַר **חֵיזוּ** עֵינֵיכוֹן דִּי אַתּוּן טָעַן בַּתְרֵיהוֹן:


And it will be to you for fringes and you will look upon it and you will remember all of Adonoy’s commandments and you will perform them; and you will not search [**stray**] after [the desire of] your hearts and after [**the sight**] your eyes which cause you to go astray.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:40)


בְּדִיל דְּתִדְכְּרוּן וְתַעְבְּדוּן יָת כָּל פִּקּוֹדָי וּתְהוֹן קַדִּישִׁין **קֳדָם** אֱלָהָכוֹן:


In order that you will remember and perform all My commandments; and you will be holy unto [**before**] your God.


###### Onkelos Numbers 15:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/15:41)


אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן דִּי אַפֵּקִית יָתְכוֹן מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם לְמֵהֲוֵי לְכוֹן לֵאֱלָהָא אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן:


I am Adonoy, your God, Who brought you out of the land of Egypt to be your God. I am Adonoy your God.



## Chapter 16

###### Onkelos Numbers 16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:1)


**וְאִתְפְּלֵג** קֹרַח בַּר יִצְהָר בַּר קְהָת בַּר לֵוִי וְדָתָן וַאֲבִירָם בְּנֵי אֱלִיאָב וְאוֹן בַּר פֶּלֶת בְּנֵי רְאוּבֵן:


Korach took [**divided**], [Korach], the son of Yitzhar the son of Kehos, the son of Levi, and Dasan and Aviram the sons of Eliav, and On the son of Peles, the sons of Reuvein.


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:2)


וְקָמוּ לְאַפֵּי משֶׁה וְגֻבְרַיָּא מִבְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מָאתָן וְחַמְשִׁין רַבְרְבֵי כְנִשְׁתָּא מְעַרְעֵי זְמַן אֱנָשִׁין דִּשְׁמָא:


They rose up before Moshe with men from Bnei Yisroel [numbering] two hundred and fifty, who were the leaders of the congregation, those who were called to meetings, men of [prominent] name.


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:3)


וְאִתְכְּנָשׁוּ עַל משֶׁה וְעַל אַהֲרֹן וַאֲמָרוּ לְהוֹן סַגִּי לְכוֹן אֲרֵי כָל כְּנִשְׁתָּא כֻּלְּהוֹן קַדִּישִׁין וּבֵינֵיהוֹן **שַׁרְיָא שְׁכִנְתָּא** דַיְיָ **וּמָא דֵין** אַתּוּן מִתְרַבְרְבִין עַל קְהָלָא דַיְיָ:


They gathered against Moshe and Aharon, and they said to them: “You have [taken] too much for yourselves [and] since the entire congregation are all holy and Adonoy is [**the Shechinah of Hashem dwells**] in their midst, why do [**what is this that**] you raise yourselves above the assembly of Adonoy?”


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:4)


וּשְׁמַע משֶׁה וּנְפַל עַל אַפּוֹהִי:


Moshe heard, and he fell upon his face.


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:5)


וּמַלִּיל עִם קֹרַח וְעִם כָּל כְּנִשְׁתֵּיהּ לְמֵימַר בְּצַפְרָא וִיהוֹדַע יְיָ יָת **דְּכָשַׁר** לֵיהּ וְיָת דְּקַדִּישׁ וִיקָרֵב לָקֳדָמוֹהִי וְיָת דִּי יִתְרְעֵי בֵיהּ יְקָרֵב **לְשִׁמּוּשֵׁיהּ**:


He spoke to Korach and to his entire congregation, saying: “[In the] morning, Adonoy will make known who is [**qualified to be**] His, and who is holy, He will bring close to Him; and whoever He chooses, He will bring near to Him [**to His service**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:6)


דָּא עִבִידוּ סָבוּ לְכוֹן מַחְתְּיָן קֹרַח וְכָל כְּנִשְׁתֵּיהּ:


Do this: Take, for yourselves, fire pans, Korach and his entire congregation,


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:7)


וְהָבוּ בְהוֹן אֶשָּׁתָא וְשַׁוִּיוּ עֲלֵיהוֹן קְטוֹרֶת **בּוּסְמִין** קֳדָם יְיָ מְחַר וִיהֵי גַּבְרָא דְּיִתְרְעֵי יְיָ הוּא קַדִּישׁ סַגִּי לְכוֹן בְּנֵי לֵוִי:


and place fire in them and put [**fragrant**] incense upon them before Adonoy, tomorrow; and it will be that the man who will be chosen by Adonoy, he is the holy one; [you have taken] too much upon yourselves, sons of Levi.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:8)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְקֹרַח שְׁמָעוּ כְעַן בְּנֵי לֵוִי:


Moshe said to Korach, “Please listen sons of Levi,


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:9)


הַזְּעֵר לְכוֹן אֲרֵי אַפְרֵשׁ אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל יָתְכוֹן מִכְּנִשְׁתָּא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל לְקָרָבָא יָתְכוֹן **קֳדָמוֹהִי** לְמִפְלַח יָת פָּלְחַן מַשְׁכְּנָא דַיְיָ וּלְמֵקַם קֳדָם כְּנִשְׁתָּא לְשַׁמָּשׁוּתְהוֹן:


is it but a pittance for yourselves that the God of Israel has separated you from the [rest of the] congregation of Israel to bring you closer to [**before**] Him to perform the services of the *Mishkan* of Adonoy, and to stand before the congregation to serve them?


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:10)


וְקָרֵב יָתָךְ וְיָת כָּל אַחָיךְ בְּנֵי לֵוִי עִמָּךְ **וּכְעַן** אַתּוּן בָּעָן אַף **כְּהֻנָּתָא רַבְּתָא**:


He brought you near with all of your brethren the sons of Levi with you, and yet [**now**] you ask to be *kohanim* [**Kohein Gadol**] as well?


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:11)


בְּכֵן אַתְּ וְכָל כְּנִשְׁתָּךְ דְּאִזְדַּמִּנְתּוּן עַל יְיָ וְאַהֲרֹן מָה הוּא אֲרֵי אִתְרַעַמְתּוּן עֲלוֹהִי:


Therefore, you and your entire congregation who are gathered against Adonoy; and what is Aharon that you rail against him?”


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:12)


וּשְׁלַח משֶׁה לְמִקְרֵי לְדָתָן וְלַאֲבִירָם בְּנֵי אֱלִיאָב וַאֲמָרוּ לָא נִסָּק:


Moshe sent to call for Dasan and Avirom the sons of Eliav and they said “We will not go up.


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:13)


הַזְּעֵר אֲרֵי אַסֶּקְתָּנָא מֵאַרְעָא **עָבְדָא** חֲלַב וּדְבָשׁ לְקַטָּלוּתָנָא בְּמַדְבְּרָא אֲרֵי אִתְרַבְרַבְתְּ עֲלָנָא אַף אִתְרַבְרָבָא:


Is it but a pittance that you have brought us up from a land flowing with [**that produces**] milk and honey to kill us in the desert, that you must excercise power over us to make yourself powerful as well?


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:14)


בְּרַם לָא לְאַרְעָא **עָבְדָא** חֲלַב וּדְבָשׁ אַעַלְתָּנָא וִיהַבְתָּא לָנָא אַחֲסָנַת חַקְלִין וְכַרְמִין הַעֵינֵי גֻּבְרַיָּא הָאִנּוּן תְּשַׁלַּח לְעַוָּרָא לָא נִסָּק:


Even into a land flowing with [**that produces**] milk and honey you have not brought us, and [not] given us an inheritance of fields and vineyards if you put those people’s eyes out, [even then] we would not go up.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:15)


וּתְקֵף לְמשֶׁה לַחֲדָא וַאֲמַר **קֳדָם** יְיָ לָא **תְקַבֵּל בְּרַעֲוָא** קֻרְבַּנְהוֹן לָא חֲמָרָא דְחַד מִנְּהוֹן שְׁחָרִית וְלָא אַבְאֵשִׁית יָת חַד מִנְּהוֹן:


Moshe was extremely distressed, and said to [**before**] Adonoy: “Do not turn to [**accept**] their offering [**with pleasure**]; not a donkey did I sequester from any one of them nor have I done ill to any one of them.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:16)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְקֹרַח אַתְּ וְכָל כְּנִשְׁתָּךְ הֱווֹ זְמִינִין לָקֳדָם יְיָ אַתְּ וְאִנּוּן וְאַהֲרֹן מְחָר:


Moshe said to Korach: “You and your entire congregation will be before Adonoy, you, and they, and Aharon, tomorrow.


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:17)


וּסְבוּ גְּבַר מַחְתִּיתֵיהּ וּתְשַׁוּוּן עֲלֵיהוֹן קְטֹרֶת **בּוּסְמִין** וּתְקָרְבוּן קֳדָם יְיָ גְּבַר מַחְתִּיתֵיהּ מָאתָן וְחַמְשִׁין מַחְתְּיָן וְאַתְּ וְאַהֲרֹן גְּבַר מַחְתִּיתֵיהּ:


Each man will take his firepan and place [**fragrant**] incense in them and you will offer before Adonoy each man his firepan, two hundred and fifty firepans, and you and Aharon will each take his own firepan.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:18)


וּנְסִיבוּ גְבַר מַחְתִּיתֵיהּ וִיהָבוּ עֲלֵיהוֹן אֶשָּׁתָא וְשַׁוִּיאוּ עֲלֵיהוֹן קְטֹרֶת בּוּסְמִין וְקָמוּ בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וּמשֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן:


Each man took his firepan and they placed fire in them and they put [**fragrant**] incense in them and they stood at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting along with Moshe and Aharon.


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:19)


וְאַכְנַשׁ עֲלֵיהוֹן קֹרַח יָת כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְאִתְגְּלֵי יְקָרָא דַיְיָ לְכָל כְּנִשְׁתָּא:


Korach congregated against them, the entire congregation at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting, and the glory of Adonoy appeared to the entire congregation.


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:20)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה וְעִם אַהֲרֹן לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe and to Aharon, saying:


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:21)


אִתְּפְּרָשׁוּ מִגּוֹ כְּנִשְׁתָּא הָדָא וְאֵשֵׁצֵי יָתְהוֹן כְּשָׁעָה:


“Separate yourselves from among this congregation, and I will annihalate them in an instant.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:22)


וּנְפָלוּ עַל אַפֵּיהוֹן וַאֲמָרוּ אֵל אֱלָהָא רוּחַיָּא לְכָל בִּשְׂרָא גַּבְרָא חַד יֵחוֹב וְעַל כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא יְהֵי רוּגְזָא:


They fell upon their faces, and they said: “Almighty, the God of the spirits of all flesh; if one man sins shall You be angry with the entire congregation?”


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:23)


וּמַלֵּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:24)


מַלֵּל עִם כְּנִשְׁתָּא לְמֵימָר אִסְתַּלָּקוּ מִסְּחוֹר סְחוֹר לְמַשְׁכְּנָא דְקֹרַח דָּתָן וַאֲבִירָם:


Speak to the congregation, saying: “Depart from the vicinity of the dwelling of Korach, Dasan and Avirom.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:25)


וְקָם משֶׁה וַאֲזַל לְוָת דָּתָן וַאֲבִירָם וַאֲזָלוּ בַתְרוֹהִי סָבֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


Moshe rose up and went to Dasan and Avirom and the elders of Israel went along after him.


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:26)


וּמַלִּיל עִם כְּנִשְׁתָּא לְמֵימָר זוּרוּ כְעַן מֵעִלָּוֵי מַשְׁכְּנֵי גֻּבְרַיָּא **חַיָּבַיָּא** הָאִלֵּין וְלָא **תִקְרְבוּן** בְּכָל דִּי לְהוֹן דִּלְמָא **תִלְקוּן** בְּכָל חוֹבֵיהוֹן:


He spoke to the congregation, saying: “Please get away from the tents of these evil [**guilty**] men, and do not touch [**come near**] anything belonging to them, lest you be swept away [**smitten**] in all their transgressions.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:27)


וְאִסְתַּלָּקוּ מֵעִלָּוֵי מַשְׁכְּנָא דְקֹרַח דָּתָן וַאֲבִירָם מִסְּחוֹר סְחוֹר וְדָתָן וַאֲבִירָם נְפָקוּ קַיְּמִין בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכְּנֵיהוֹן וּנְשֵׁיהוֹן וּבְנֵיהוֹן וְטַפְלְהוֹן:


They withdraw from the dwelling of Korach, Dasan and Avirom, and all around; and Dasan and Avirom went out, and stood at the door of their tents with their wives, children and infants.


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:28)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה בְּדָא תִדְּעוּן אֲרֵי יְיָ שַׁלְּחַנִּי לְמֶעְבַּד יָת כָּל עוֹבָדַיָּא הָאִלֵּין אֲרֵי לָא **מֵרְעוּתִי**:


Moshe said: “This is how you will know that Adonoy has sent me to perform all of these deeds, for, I did not devise them myself [**from my own will**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:29)


אִם כְּמוֹתָא דְכָל אֱנָשָׁא יְמוּתוּן אִלֵּין וּסְעָרָא דְּכָל אֶנָשָׁא יִסְתְּעַר עֲלֵיהוֹן לָא יְיָ שַׁלְּחַנִּי:


If as all men die will these men meet their deaths, and the reckoning (fate) of all men will be reckoned upon them, then Adonoy has not sent me.


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:30)


וְאִם בְּרִיאָה יִבְרֵי יְיָ וּפְתָחַת אַרְעָא יָת פּוּמַהּ וּבְלָעַת יָתְהוֹן וְיָת כָּל דִּי לְהוֹן וְיֶחֱתוּן כַּד חַיִּין לִשְׁאוֹל וְתִדְּעוּן אֲרֵי אַרְגִּיזוּ גֻּבְרַיָּא הָאִלֵּין קֳדָם יְיָ:


If Adonoy will create a [new] creation and the ground will open its mouth and swallow them along with all of their belongings, and they will go down alive to the grave, then you will know that these men have angered Adonoy.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:31)


וַהֲוָה כַּד שֵׁצֵי לְמַלָּלָא יָת כָּל פִּתְגָּמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין וְאִתְבְּזָעַת אַרְעָא דִּי תְחוֹתֵיהוֹן:


As he was finishing speaking all these words the ground beneath them split.


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:32)


וּפְתָחַת אַרְעָא יָת פּוּמַהּ וּבְלָעַת יָתְהוֹן וְיָת **אֱנַשׁ בָּתֵּיהוֹן** וְיָת כָּל אֱנָשָׁא דִּי לְקֹרַח וְיָת כָּל קִנְיָנָא:


The earth opened its mouth and swallowed them and their houses [**household members**] and all the people belonging to Korach and all of the possessions.


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:33)


וּנְחָתוּ אִנּוּן וְכָל דִּי לְהוֹן כַּד חַיִּין לִשְׁאוֹל וַחֲפַת עֲלֵיהוֹן אַרְעָא וַאֲבָדוּ מִגּוֹ קְהָלָא:


They, and all belonging to them, went down alive to the grave, and the earth covered them and they perished from among the assembly.


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:34)


וְכָל יִשְׂרָאֵל דִּי בְּסַחֲרָנֵיהוֹן עֲרָקוּ לְקָלְהוֹן אֲרֵי אֲמָרוּ דִּלְמָא תִבְלְעִנָּנָא אַרְעָא:


All of Yisroel who were around them fled from their uproar, for they said, perhaps the earth will swallow us.


###### Onkelos Numbers 16:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/16:35)


וְאֶשָּׁתָא נְפָקַת מִן **קֳדָם** יְיָ וַאֲכָלַת יָת מָאתָן וְחַמְשִׁין גַּבְרָא מְקָרְבֵי קְטֹרֶת **בּוּסְמַיָּא**:


A fire came forth from [**before**] Adonoy and consumed the two hundred and fifty men who were bringing the [**fragrant**] incense.



## Chapter 17

###### Onkelos Numbers 17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:1)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying;


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:2)


אֲמַר לְאֶלְעָזָר בַּר אַהֲרֹן כַּהֲנָא וְיַפְרֵשׁ יָת מַחְתְּיָתָא מִבֵּין יְקִידַיָּא וְיָת אֶשָּׁתָא יַרְחֵק לְהָלָא אֲרֵי אִתְקַדָּשׁוּ:


“Say to Elazar the son of Aharon, the *kohein* and have him lift up the firepans from the inferno, and you, scatter the fire yonder, for, they were sanctified;


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:3)


יָת מַחְתְּיַת חַיָּבַיָּא הָאִלֵּין דְּאִתְחַיָּבוּ בְּנַפְשָׁתֵיהוֹן וְיַעְבְּדוּן יָתְהֵן טַסִּין רַדִּידִין חוֹפָאָה לְמַדְבְּחָא אֲרֵי קָרֵבֻנּוּן קֳדָם יְיָ וְאִתְקַדָּשׁוּ וִיהֶוְיָן לְאָת לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


[These] firepans of these sinners [who paid] with their souls, and make them into hammered sheets to overlay the altar, for they were brought in offering before Adonoy and they were sanctified; and they shall be for a sign for Bnei Yisroel.


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:4)


וּנְסִיב אֶלְעָזָר כַּהֲנָא יָת מַחְתְּיָתָא דִנְחָשָׁא דְּקָרִיבוּ יוֹקִידַיָּא וְרַדִּידֻנּוּן חוֹפָאָה לְמַדְבְּחָא:


Elazar the *kohein,* took the copper firepans, those that the fire victims brought and hammered them into a covering for the altar,


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:5)


דָּכְרָנָא לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּדִיל דִּי לָא יִקְרַב גְּבַר חִלּוֹנַי דִּי לָא מִזַּרְעָא דְאַהֲרֹן הוּא לְאַסָּקָא קְטֹרֶת **בּוּסְמִין** קֳדָם יְיָ וְלָא יְהֵי כְקֹרַח וְכִכְנִשְׁתֵּיהּ כְּמָא דִי מַלִּיל יְיָ בִּידָא דְמשֶׁה לֵיהּ:


to be a reminder for Bnei Yisroel so that a commoner shall never offer, one who is not a descendant of Aharon, to burn [**fragrant**] incense before Adonoy; so as not to be like Korach and his congregation; as Adonoy spoke to him, through Moshe.


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:6)


וְאִתְרַעֲמוּ כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מִיּוֹמָא דְבַתְרוֹהִי עַל משֶׁה וְעַל אַהֲרֹן לְמֵימָר אַתּוּן **גְּרַמְתּוּן דְּמִית** עַמָּא דַיְיָ:


They complained—the entire congregation of Bnei Yisroel—on the next day against Moshe and Aharon, saying: “You have killed [**caused the death**] the people of Adonoy”


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:7)


וַהֲוָה בְּאִתְכַּנָּשׁוּת כְּנִשְׁתָּא עַל משֶׁה וְעַל אַהֲרֹן וְאִתְפְּנִיאוּ לְמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְהָא חֲפָהִי עֲנָנָא וְאִתְגְּלִי יְקָרָא דַיְיָ:


When the congregation assembled against Moshe and Aharon, they turned to the Tent of Meeting and behold! The cloud covered it, and the Glory of Adonoy appeared.


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:8)


וְעָל משֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן לָקֳדָם מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא:


Moshe and Aharon came to the front of the Tent of Meeting.


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:9)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:10)


**אִתְפְּרָשׁוּ** מִגּוֹ כְּנִשְׁתָּא הָדָא וְאֵשֵׁצֵי יָתְהוֹן כְּשָׁעָה וּנְפָלוּ עַל אַפֵּיהוֹן:


“Remove [**separate**] yourselves from this congregation and I shall annihalate them in an instant,” and they fell upon their faces.


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:11)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְאַהֲרֹן סַב יָת מַחְתִּיתָא וְהַב עֲלַהּ אֶשָּׁתָא מֵעִלָּוֵי מַדְבְּחָא וְשַׁו קְטֹרֶת **בּוּסְמַיָּא** וְאוֹבִיל בִּפְרִיעַ לִכְנִשְׁתָּא וְכַפֵּר עֲלֵיהוֹן אֲרֵי נְפַק רָגְזָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ שָׁרֵי מוֹתָנָא:


Moshe said to Aharon: “Take the firepan and put fire from atop the altar on it and place [**fragrant**] incense on it, and go quickly to the congregation and atone for them, for anger has come forth from before Adonoy; the plague has commenced.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:12)


וּנְסִיב אַהֲרֹן כְּמָא דִי מַלִּיל משֶׁה וּרְהַט לְגוֹ קְהָלָא וְהָא שָׁרֵי מוֹתָנָא בְּעַמָּא וִיהַב יָת קְטֹרֶת בּוּסְמַיָּא וְכַפַּר עַל עַמָּא:


Aharon took, just as Moshe spoke, and he ran to the middle of the assembly and behold! The plague had commenced among the people. He put on incense and he atoned for the people.


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:13)


וְקָם בֵּין מֵתַיָּא וּבֵין חַיַּיָּא **וְאִתְכְּלִי** מוֹתָנָא:


He stood between the dead and between the living and the plague was halted [**ceased**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:14)


וַהֲווֹ דְּמִיתוּ בְּמוֹתָנָא אַרְבְּעַת עֲשַׁר אַלְפִין וּשְׁבַע מְאָה בַּר מִדְּמִיתוּ עַל **פְּלֻגְתָּא** דְקֹרַח:


The dead in the plague totaled fourteen thousand seven hundred, excluding those who died due to Korach’s rebellion [**division**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:15)


וְתָב אַהֲרֹן לְמשֶׁה לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וּמוֹתָנָא אִתְכְּלִי:


Aharon returned to Moshe to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting and the plague was halted.


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:16)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:17)


מַלֵּל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְסַב מִנְּהוֹן חֻטְרָא חֻטְרָא לְבֵית אַבָּא מִן כָּל רַבְרְבָנֵיהוֹן לְבֵית אֲבָהַתְהוֹן תְּרֵי עֲשַׂר חֻטְרִין גְּבַר יָת שְׁמֵיהּ תִּכְתּוֹב עַל חֻטְרֵיהּ:


“Speak to Bnei Yisroel and take from among them a staff, a staff for each father’s [tribal] house, from all the leaders according to their fathers’ houses, twelve staffs; and you shall write each man’s name on his staff.


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:18)


וְיָת שְׁמָא דְאַהֲרֹן תִּכְתּוֹב עַל חֻטְרָא דְלֵוִי אֲרֵי חֻטְרָא חַד לְרֵישׁ בֵּית אֲבָהַתְהוֹן:


You shall write the name of Aharon on the staff of Levi, for, [there is] one staff for the head of their fathers’ house.


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:19)


וְתַצְנְעִנּוּן בְּמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא קֳדָם סַהֲדוּתָא דִּי אֲזַמֵּן **מֵימְרִי** לְכוֹן תַּמָּן:


You shall leave them inside the Tent of Meeting before the testimony where there I [**My word**] meet you.


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:20)


וִיהֵי גַּבְרָא דְּאִתְרְעֵי בֵיהּ חֻטְרֵיהּ יִנְעֵי וַאֲנִיחַ מִן **קֳדָמַי** יָת תּוּרְעֲמַת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל דִּי אִנּוּן מִתְרַעֲמִין עֲלֵיכוֹן:


It shall be that the man whom I choose, his staff shall blossom; and I will abate from Myself [**before Me**] the complaints of Bnei Yisroel that they complain against you.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:21)


וּמַלִּיל משֶׁה עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וִיהָבוּ לֵיהּ כָּל רַבְרְבָנֵיהוֹן חֻטְרָא לְרַבָּא חַד חֻטְרָא לְרַבָּא חַד לְבֵית אֲבָהַתְהוֹן תְּרֵי עֲשַׂר חֻטְרִין וְחֻטְרָא דְאַהֲרֹן בְּגוֹ חֻטְרֵיהוֹן:


Moshe spoke to Bnei Yisroel and all the leaders gave to him a staff for each leader; a staff for each leader, according to their fathers’ house, twelve staffs, and the staff of Aharon [was] among their staffs.


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:22)


וְאַצְנַע משֶׁה יָת חֻטְרַיָּא קֳדָם יְיָ בְּמַשְׁכְּנָא דְּסַהֲדוּתָא:


Moshe placed the staffs before Adonoy in the Tent of the Testimony.


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:23)


וַהֲוָה בְיוֹמָא דְבַתְרוֹהִי וְעַל משֶׁה לְמַשְׁכְּנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא וְהָא נְעָא חֻטְרָא דְאַהֲרֹן לְבֵית לֵוִי וְאַפֵּק לַבְלְבִין וְאָנֵץ נֵץ וְכָפֵת שִׁגְדִּין


It was on the next day, Moshe came inside the Tent of the Testimony, and behold! The staff of Aharon blossomed for the house of Levi, a blossom came forth, and bloomed buds and bore ripe almonds.


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:24)


וְאַפֵּק משֶׁה יָת כָּל חֻטְרַיָּא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ לְוָת כָּל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל **וְאִשְׁתְּמוֹדָעוּ** וּנְסִיבוּ גְּבַר חֻטְרֵיהּ:


Moshe then brought out all the staffs from before Adonoy to all Bnei Yisroel, and each (man) saw [**recognized**], and retrieved his staff.


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:25)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה אָתֵב יָת חֻטְרָא דְאַהֲרֹן לָקֳדָם סַהֲדוּתָא לְמַטְּרָא לְאָת לְעַמָּא סַרְבָּנָא וִיסוּפַן תּוּרְעֲמַתְהוֹן מִן **קֳדָמַי** וְלָא יְמוּתוּן:


Adonoy said to Moshe, “Return the staff of Aharon before the (Ark of) Testimony to be preserved as a symbol to the rebellious people, so that their complaint will end [**from before Me**] and they not die.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:26)


וַעֲבַד משֶׁה כְּמָא דִּי פַקִּיד יְיָ יָתֵיהּ כֵּן עֲבָד:


Moshe did (this), as Adonoy commanded him—so he did.


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:27)


וַאֲמָרוּ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמשֶׁה לְמֵימָר הָא מִנָּנָא **קְטָלַת חַרְבָּא הָא מִנָּנָא בְּלַעַת אַרְעָא וְהָא מִנָּנָא דְּמִיתוּ בְמוֹתָנָא**:


Bnei Yisroel said [the following] to Moshe: “Behold, we perish, we are lost—we are all lost [**killed by sword, some of us swallowed by the earth, some of us perish by plague**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 17:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/17:28)


כֹּל דְּקָרֵב מִקְרַב לְמַשְׁכְּנָא דַיְיָ מָאִית הָא אֲנַחְנָא סַיְפִין לִמְמָת:


Anyone approaching, who comes close to the Mishkon of Adonoy will die—have we been consigned to die?”



## Chapter 18

###### Onkelos Numbers 18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:1)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְאַהֲרֹן אַתְּ וּבְנָיךְ וּבֵית אָבוּךְ עִמָּךְ תְּסַלְּחוּן עַל חוֹבֵי מַקְדְּשָׁא וְאַתְּ וּבְנָיךְ עִמָּךְ תְּסַלְּחוּן עַל חוֹבֵי כְהֻנַּתְכוֹן:


Adonoy said to Aharon: “You, your sons, and your father’s house with you will bear the sin of the Sanctuary, and you, and your sons with you will bear the sin of your *kehunah*.


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:2)


וְאַף יָת אַחָיךְ שִׁבְטָא דְלֵוִי שִׁבְטָא דְאָבוּךְ קָרֵב לְוָתָךְ וְיִתּוֹסְפוּן עֲלָךְ וִישַׁמְּשֻׁנָּךְ וְאַתְּ וּבְנָיךְ עִמָּךְ קֳדָם מַשְׁכְּנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא:


Also, your brothers, the tribe of Levi—the tribe of your father—bring to you, and they shall join you, and serve you, you and your sons with you, before the Tent of Testimony.


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:3)


וְיִטְּרוּן מַטַּרְתָּךְ וּמַטְּרַת כָּל מַשְׁכְּנָא בְּרַם לְמָנֵי קוּדְשָׁא וּלְמַדְבְּחָא לָא יִקְרְבוּן וְלָא יְמוּתוּן אַף אִנּוּן אַף אַתּוּן:


They shall guard their duties to you and all the duties of the Tent, but to the holy furniture or to the Altar they must not approach, that neither they, nor you, shall die.


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:4)


וְיִתּוֹסְפוּן עֲלָךְ וְיִטְּרוּן יָת מַטְּרַת מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לְכָל פָּלְחַן מַשְׁכְּנָא וְחִלּוֹנַי לָא יִקְרַב לְוָתְכוֹן:


They shall join you and guard the duties of the Tent of Meeting—all the service of the Tent, but a commoner, [a non-*kohein*] should not approach you.


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:5)


וְתִטְּרוּן יָת מַטְּרַת קוּדְשָׁא וְיָת מַטְּרַת מַדְבְּחָא וְלָא יְהֵי עוֹד רָגְזָא עַל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


You shall discharge the sacred duties and the duties of the Altar, so that there will be no more wrath on Bnei Yisroel.


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:6)


וַאֲנָא הָא קָרֵיבִית יָת אֲחֵיכוֹן לֵוָאֵי מִגּוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְכוֹן מַתְּנָא יְהִיבִין **קֳדָם** יְיָ לְמִפְלַח יָת פָּלְחַן מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא:


Behold, I have brought your brothers the Levites from among Bnei Yisroel, given to you as a gift—for [**before**] Adonoy—to perform the work of the Tent of Meeting.


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:7)


וְאַתְּ וּבְנָיךְ עִמָּךְ תִּטְּרוּן יָת כְּהֻנַּתְכוֹן לְכָל פִּתְגַּם מַדְבְּחָא וּלְמִגָּו לְפָרוּכְתָּא וְתִפְלְחוּן פָּלְחַן מַתְּנָא אֵיהַב יָת כְּהֻנַּתְכוֹן וְחִלּוֹנַי דְּיִקְרַב יִתְקְטָל:


You and your sons with you shall guard your *kehunah* [duties] regarding the Altar and all that is within the Curtain—you shall serve—the service, as a gift, I give the *kehunah* to you, and the commoner who approaches shall be killed.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:8)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם אַהֲרֹן וַאֲנָא הָא יְהָבִית לָךְ יָת מַטְּרַת אַפְרָשׁוּתָי לְכָל קוּדְשַׁיָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לָךְ יְהַבְתִּנּוּן לִרְבוּ וְלִבְנָיךְ לִקְיַם עֳלָם:


Adonoy spoke to Aharon: “Behold I have given you the guarding of my *terumah*-gifts, and all the hallowed things of Bnei Yisroel, to you and your children I give them for consecration, as a portion forever.


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:9)


דֵּין יְהֵי לָךְ מִקּוֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשַׁיָּא **מוֹתַר** מִן אֶשָּׁתָא כָּל קוּרְבָּנֵיהוֹן לְכָל מִנְחַתְהוֹן וּלְכָל חַטְוַתְהוֹן וּלְכָל אֲשַׁמְהוֹן דִּי יְתִיבוּן **קֳדָמַי** קוֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשִׁין דִּי לָךְ הוּא וְלִבְנָיךְ:


This is yours from the most holy [**that remain**] from the fire, [of] all their sacrifices—their *minchah*-offerings, their sin-offerings and their guilt-offerings which they return to [**before**] Me shall be most holy to you and your sons.


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:10)


בְּקֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשִׁין תֵּכְלֻנֵּיהּ כָּל דְּכוּרָא יֵיכוּל יָתֵיהּ קוּדְשָׁא יְהֵי לָךְ:


You shall eat it in the supremely holy (place), every male may eat it—it shall be holy to you.


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:11)


וְדֵין לָךְ אַפְרָשׁוּת מַתְּנַתְהוֹן לְכָל אֲרַמוּת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לָךְ יְהַבְתִּנּוּן וְלִבְנָיךְ וְלִבְנָתָיךְ עִמָּךְ לִקְיַם עֳלָם כָּל דִּדְכֵי בְּבֵיתָךְ יֵיכוּל יָתֵיהּ:


Their *terumah*-gift is yours, of all of the wave-offerings of Bnei Yisroel I give to you and to your sons and daughters that are with you as an everlasting portion; all those ritually pure in your house may eat it.


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:12)


כָּל טוּב מְשַׁח וְכָל טוּב חֲמַר וְעִבּוּר רֵאשִׁיתְהוֹן דִּי יִתְּנוּן קֳדָם יְיָ לָךְ יְהַבְתִּנּוּן:


The best of the oil, and the best of the wine and grain—their *terumah*-gift that they present to Adonoy—I have given to you.


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:13)


בִּכּוּרֵי כָּל דִּי בְאַרְעֲהוֹן דִּי יַיְתוּן קֳדָם יְיָ דִּי לָךְ יְהֵי כָּל דִּדְכֵי בְּבֵיתָךְ יֵיכְלֻנֵּיהּ:


The first fruits of all that is in their land, which they bring before Adonoy, shall be yours; all those ritually pure in your house may eat it.


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:14)


כָּל חֶרְמָא בְיִשְׂרָאֵל דִּי לָךְ יְהֵי:


Everything consecrated in Israel shall be yours.


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:15)


**כָּל פָּתַח וַלְדָּא** לְכָל בִּשְׂרָא דִּי יְקָרְבוּן קֳדָם יְיָ בֶּאֱנָשָׁא וּבִבְעִירָא יְהֵי לָךְ בְּרַם מִפְרַק תִּפְרוּק יָת בּוּכְרָא דֶאֱנָשָׁא וְיָת בּוּכְרָא דִבְעִירָא מְסַאֲבָא תִּפְרוֹק:


Every firstling [**all that open the womb**] of every being which they offer before Adonoy—man or beast—shall be yours; but you must redeem the firstborn of man and redeem the firstborn of unclean beasts.


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:16)


וּפוּרְקָנֵיהּ מִבַּר יַרְחָא תִּפְרוֹק בְּפֻרְסָנֵיהּ כְּסַף חֲמֵשׁ סִלְעִין בְּסִלְעֵי קוּדְשָׁא עַשְׂרִין מָעִין הוּא:


Its redemption—from a month old you should redeem them—the valuation is five silver *sela’im* of holy silver *shekalim* which is twenty *gerah.*


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:17)


בְּרַם בּוּכְרָא דְתוֹרָא אוֹ בוּכְרָא דְאִמְרָא אוֹ בוּכְרָא דְעִזָּא לָא תִפְרוּק קוּדְשָׁא אִנּוּן יָת דִּמְהוֹן תִּזְרוּק עַל מַדְבְּחָא וְיָת תַּרְבֵּיהוֹן תַּסֵּק קוּרְבַּן **לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְרַעֲוָא קֳדָם** יְיָ:


However, a firstborn ox, a firstborn sheep or firstborn goat do not redeem because they are holy; sprinkle their blood on the altar and burn their fats as a fire-offering as a pleasing fragrance to [**accepted with pleasure before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:18)


וּבִשְׂרְהוֹן יְהֵי לָךְ כְּחַדְיָא דַאֲרָמוּתָא וּכְשׁוֹקָא דְיַמִּינָא לָךְ יְהֵי:


Their flesh is yours; like the breast which is waved and like the right thigh—it shall be yours.


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:19)


כֹּל אַפְרָשׁוּת קוּדְשַׁיָּא דִּי יִפְרְשׁוּן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל קֳדָם יְיָ יְהָבִית לָךְ וְלִבְנָיךְ וְלִבְנָתָיךְ עִמָּךְ לִקְיַם עֳלָם קְיַם מְלַח עֳלָם הוּא קֳדָם יְיָ לָךְ וְלִבְנָיךְ עִמָּךְ:


All of the sacred *terumah*-gifts that Bnei Yisroel offer to Adonoy, I have given to you, your sons and daughters with you as an everlasting portion; an eternal covenant of salt before Adonoy to you and your children that are with you.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:20)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְאַהֲרֹן בְּאַרְעֲהוֹן לָא תַחֲסִין וָחֳלַק לָא יְהֵי לָךְ בֵּינֵיהוֹן **מַתְּנַן דִּי יְהָבִית לָךְ** אִנּוּן חוּלְקָךְ וְאַחֲסַנְתָּךְ בְּגוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


Adonoy said to Aharon: “You will have no inheritance in their land, and you will not have a share among them; I am [**the gift that I have given you is**] your inheritance and portion among Bnei Yisroel.


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:21)


וְלִבְנֵי לֵוִי הָא יְהָבִית כָּל מַעַשְׂרָא בְיִשְׂרָאֵל לְאַחֲסָנָא חֲלַף פּוּלְחַנְהוֹן דִּי אִנּוּן פָּלְחִין יָת פּוּלְחַן מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא:


Behold, I have given to the sons of Levi all the tithes in Israel for an inheritance in return for their service which they perform—the service of the Tent of Meeting.


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:22)


וְלָא יִקְרְבוּן עוֹד בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לְקַבָּלָא חוֹבָא לִמְמָת:


Bnei Yisroel shall no longer approach the Tent of Meeting, to become guilty and die.


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:23)


וְיִפְלְחוּן לֵוָאֵי אִנּוּן יָת פּוּלְחַן מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְאִנּוּן יְקַבְּלוּן חוֹבֵיהוֹן קְיַם עֳלָם לְדָרֵיכוֹן וּבְגוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לָא יַחְסְנוּן אַחֲסָנָא:


The Levi[tes]—[they] shall perform the service of the Tent of Meeting and they will bear their guilt; it is an everlasting decree—throughout your generations—among Bnei Yisroel they (the Levites) shall have no inheritance.


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:24)


אֲרֵי יָת מַעַשְׂרָא דִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל דִּי יַפְרְשׁוּן **קֳדָם** יְיָ אַפְרָשׁוּתָא יְהָבִית לְלֵוָאֵי לְאַחֲסָנָא עַל כֵּן אֲמָרִית לְהוֹן בְּגוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לָא יַחְסְנוּן אַחֲסָנָא:


For the tithe of Bnei Yisroel which they must set aside to [**before**] Adonoy as a *terumah*-gift I gave to the Levites as an inheritance; therefore, I told them: ‘among Bnei Yisroel they shall have no inheritance.’”


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:25)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:26)


וּלְלֵוָאֵי תְמַלֵּל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן אֲרֵי תִסְּבוּן מִן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל יָת מַעַשְׂרָא דִּי יְהָבִית לְכוֹן מִנְּהוֹן בְּאַחֲסַנְתְּכוֹן וְתַפְרְשׁוּן מִנֵּיהּ **אַפְרָשׁוּתָא** קֳדָם יְיָ מַעַשְׂרָא מִן מַעַשְׂרָא:


“Speak to the Levites and say to them: ‘When you take the tithe from Bnei Yisroel, which I gave you from them for your inheritance, set aside from it the *terumah*-gift of [**separation before**] Adonoy, a tithe of the tithe.


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:27)


וְתִתְחַשַּׁב לְכוֹן **אַפְרָשׁוּתְכוֹן** כְּעִבּוּרָא מִן אִדְרָא וְכַמְּלֵאָתָא מִן מַעְצַרְתָּא:


Your *terumah*-gift [**separation**] will be considered the same as grain from the granary and [wine and oil of] fully ripened fruit from the cistern.


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:28)


כֵּן תַּפְרְשׁוּן אַף אַתּוּן אַפְרָשׁוּתָא קֳדָם יְיָ מִכֹּל מַעַשְׂרָתֵיכוֹן דִּי תִסְּבוּן מִן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְתִתְּנוּן מִנֵּיהּ יָת **אַפְרָשׁוּתָא קֳדָם** יְיָ לְאַהֲרֹן כַּהֲנָא:


So must you, too, set aside the *terumah*-gift of Adonoy from all your tithes which you take from Bnei Yisroel; give from them the *terumah*-gift of [**separation before**] Adonoy to Aharon the *kohein.*


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:29)


מִכֹּל מַתְּנַתְכוֹן תַּפְרְשׁוּן יָת כָּל **אַפְרָשׁוּתָא** דַיְיָ מִכָּל שׁוּפְרֵיהּ יָת מַקְדְּשֵׁיהּ מִנֵּיהּ:


From all your gifts you must set aside all of the *terumah*-gifts [**separations**] of Adonoy; from each thing—its finest part—the part that is to be consecrated.’


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:30)


וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן בְּאַפְרָשׁוּתְכוֹן יָת שׁוּפְרֵיהּ מִנֵּיהּ וְיִתְחַשַּׁב לְלֵוָאֵי כַּעֲלָלַת אִדְרָא וְכַעֲלָלַת מַעְצַרְתָּא:


Say to them: ‘When you set aside the finest part of it, it will be considered for the Levites like the produce of the granary and the produce of the cistern.


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:31)


וְתֵיכְלוּן יָתֵיהּ בְּכָל אֲתַר אַתּוּן **וֶאֱנַשׁ בָּתֵּיכוֹן** אֲרֵי אַגְרָא הוּא לְכוֹן חֲלַף פָּלְחַנְכוֹן בְּמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא:


You may consume it anywhere—you and [**the members of**] your household; since it is the reward for your service in the Tent of Meeting.


###### Onkelos Numbers 18:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/18:32)


וְלָא תְקַבְּלוּן עֲלוֹהִי חוֹבָא בְּאַפְרָשׁוּתְכוֹן יָת שׁוּפְרֵיהּ מִנֵּיהּ וְיָת קוּדְשַׁיָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לָא תַחֲלוּן וְלָא תְמוּתוּן:


You will not bear a sin because of it, when you separate its best part; but sacred things of Bnei Yisroel you must not profane, and you will not die.”’



## Chapter 19

###### Onkelos Numbers 19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:1)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה וְעִם אַהֲרֹן לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe and Aharon, saying.


###### Onkelos Numbers 19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:2)


דָּא גְּזֵרַת אוֹרַיְתָא דִּי פַקִּיד יְיָ לְמֵימָר מַלֵּל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְיִסְּבוּן לָךְ תּוֹרְתָא סִמּוּקְתָּא שְׁלֶמְתָּא דִּי לֵית בָּהּ מוּמָא דִּי לָא סְלִיק עֲלַהּ נִירָא:


This is the statute of the Torah which Adonoy commanded, saying; speak to Bnei Yisroel that they shall take to you a red, perfect cow without a blemish, upon which no yoke was laid.


###### Onkelos Numbers 19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:3)


וְתִתְּנוּן יָתַהּ לְאֶלְעָזָר כַּהֲנָא וְיַפֵּק יָתַהּ לְמִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא וְיִכּוֹס יָתַהּ קֳדָמוֹהִי:


Give it to Elozor the *kohein*; he shall take it outside the camp, and someone shall slaughter it in his presence.


###### Onkelos Numbers 19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:4)


וְיִסַּב אֶלְעָזָר כַּהֲנָא מִדְּמַהּ בְּאֶצְבְּעֵיהּ וְיַדִּי לָקֳבֵל אַפֵּי מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא מִדְּמַהּ שְׁבַע זִמְנִין:


Elozor the *kohein* shall take some of its blood with his finger and sprinkle it directly facing the Tent of Meeting, some of its blood seven times.


###### Onkelos Numbers 19:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:5)


וְיוֹקֵד יָת תּוֹרְתָא לְעֵינוֹהִי יָת מַשְׁכַּהּ וְיָת בִּשְׂרַהּ וְיָת דְּמַהּ עַל אָכְלַהּ יוֹקֵד:


Someone shall burn the cow in his presence; its skin, flesh, blood, with its waste (that are in its intestines) shall be burned.


###### Onkelos Numbers 19:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:6)


וְיִסַּב כַּהֲנָא אָעָא דְאַרְזָא וְאֵזוֹבָא וּצְבַע זְהוֹרִי וְיִרְמֵי לְגוֹ יְקִידַת תּוֹרְתָא:


The *kohein* shall take a piece of cedar wood, hyssop, and crimson wool, and throw them into the burning of the cow.


###### Onkelos Numbers 19:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:7)


וִיצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹהִי כַּהֲנָא וְיַסְחֵי בִשְׂרֵיהּ בְּמַיָּא וּבָתַר כֵּן יֵעוּל לְמַשְׁרִיתָא וִיהֵי מְסָאָב כַּהֲנָא עַד רַמְשָׁא:


Then the *kohein* shall wash his garments and bathe his body in water, and afterwards he may enter the camp; [however,] the *kohein* shall be impure until the evening.


###### Onkelos Numbers 19:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:8)


וּדְמוֹקֵד יָתַהּ יְצַבַּע לְבוֹשׁוֹהִי בְּמַיָּא וְיַסְחֵי בִשְׂרֵיהּ בְּמַיָּא וִיהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא:


Also, the one who burns it must wash his garments and bathe his body in water; he shall remain unclean until the evening.


###### Onkelos Numbers 19:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:9)


וְיִכְנוֹשׁ גְּבַר דְּכֵי יָת קִטְמָא דְתוֹרְתָא וְיַצְנַע לְמִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא בַּאֲתַר דְּכֵי וּתְהֵי לִכְנִשְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמַטָּרָא לְמֵי אַדָּיוּתָא חַטָּאתָא הִיא:


A ritually clean person shall gather the cow’s ashes and place [it] outside the camp in a clean place; it shall remain a keepsake for the community of Bnei Yisroel for sprinkling water for purification.


###### Onkelos Numbers 19:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:10)


וִיצַבַּע דְּכַנֵּישׁ יָת קִטְמָא דְתוֹרְתָא יָת לְבוּשׁוֹהִי וִיהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא וּתְהֵי לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וּלְגִיּוֹרַיָּא דְּיִתְגַיְּרוּן בֵּינֵיהוֹן לִקְיַם עֳלָם:


The one who gathers the cow’s ashes shall wash his garments, and remain unclean until the evening; it shall be for Bnei Yisroel and for the proselyte who lives among them an eternal statute.


###### Onkelos Numbers 19:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:11)


דְּיִקְרַב בְּמֵתָא לְכָל נַפְשָׁא דֶאֱנָשָׁא וִיהֵי מְסָאָב שַׁבְעָא יוֹמִין:


One who touches the corpse of any [human] soul shall become unclean for seven days.


###### Onkelos Numbers 19:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:12)


הוּא **יַדֵּי** עֲלוֹהִי בְּיוֹמָא תְלִיתָאָה וּבְיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה יִדְכֵּי וְאִם לָא **יַדֵּי** עֲלוֹהִי בְּיוֹמָא תְלִיתָאָה וּבְיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה לָא יִדְכֵּי:


He shall cleanse himself [**be sprinked**] with it on the third day and on the seventh day, so that he may become clean; if he does not have himself cleansed [**be sprinked**] on the third day and on the seventh day, he will not become clean.


###### Onkelos Numbers 19:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:13)


כָּל דְּיִקְרַב בְּמִיתָא בְנַפְשָׁא דֶאֱנָשָׁא דִי יְמוּת וְלָא **יַדֵּי** עֲלוֹהִי יָת מַשְׁכְּנָא דַיְיָ סָאָב וְיִשְׁתֵּצֵי אֱנָשָׁא הַהוּא מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל אֲרֵי מֵי אַדָּיוּתָא לָא אִזְדְּרִיקוּ עֲלוֹהִי מְסָאָב יְהֵי עוֹד סְאוֹבְתֵיהּ בֵּיהּ:


Whoever touches a corpse of a [human] soul, who died and does not have himself cleansed [**be sprinked**] defiles the *Mishkan* of Adonoy (if he enters it), that soul will be cut off from Yisroel; because the sprinkling water was not sprinkled on him, he will be impure his impurity remains.


###### Onkelos Numbers 19:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:14)


דָּא אוֹרַיְתָא אֱנַשׁ אֲרֵי יְמוּת בְּמַשְׁכְּנָא כָּל דְּעָלֵל לְמַשְׁכְּנָא וְכָל דִּי בְּמַשְׁכְּנָא יְהֵי מְסָאָב שַׁבְעָא יוֹמִין:


This is the law [regarding] a person who dies in a tent; anyone who enters the tent and everything that is in the tent shall be unclean for seven days.


###### Onkelos Numbers 19:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:15)


וְכֹל מַן **דַּחֲסַף** פְּתִיחַ דְּלֵית מְגוּפַת שְׁיִיעַ מַקַּף עֲלוֹהִי מְסָאָב הוּא:


Any open [**earthenware**] utensil that has no cover fastened to it, shall be unclean.


###### Onkelos Numbers 19:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:16)


וְכֹל דִּי יִקְרַב עַל אַפֵּי חַקְלָא בִּקְטֵל חַרְבָּא אוֹ בְמֵתָא אוֹ בְגַרְמָא דֶאֱנָשָׁא אוֹ בְקִבְרָא יְהֵי מְסָאָב שַׁבְעָא יוֹמִין:


Anyone who touches, in an open field, one slain by the sword, a corpse, human bone, or grave, shall be unclean for seven days.


###### Onkelos Numbers 19:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:17)


וְיִסְּבוּן לְדִמְסָאָב מֵעֲפַר יְקֵדַת חַטָּאתָא וְיִתְּנוּן עֲלוֹהִי מֵי מַבּוּעַ לְמָן:


They shall take for the unclean person of the ashes from the burnt purification-offering and he shall place upon them living [spring] water in a vessel.


###### Onkelos Numbers 19:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:18)


וְיִסַּב אֵזוֹבָא וְיִטְבּוֹל בְּמַיָּא גְּבַר דְּכֵי וְיַדֵּי עַל מַשְׁכְּנָא וְעַל כָּל מָנַיָּא וְעַל נַפְשָׁתָא דִּי הֲווֹ תַמָּן וְעַל דְּיִקְרַב בְּגַרְמָא אוֹ בְקָטִילָא אוֹ בְמֵיתָא אוֹ בְקִבְרָא:


He shall take hyssop and dip it into the water; [this is done by] a ritually clean person, he shall sprinkle it on the tent, on all the utensils and people that were there, and on anyone who touched the bone, murder victim, corpse, or grave.


###### Onkelos Numbers 19:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:19)


וְיַדֵּי דַכְיָא עַל מְסָאֲבָא בְּיוֹמָא תְלִיתָאָה וּבְיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה וִידַכִּנֵּיהּ בְּיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה וִיצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹהִי וְיַסְחֵי בְמַיָּא וְיִדְכֵּי בְרַמְשָׁא:


The ritually clean person shall sprinkle upon the unclean person on the third day and on the seventh day; he shall purify him on the seventh day when he must wash his garments and bathe in water, and then he becomes clean in the evening.


###### Onkelos Numbers 19:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:20)


וּגְבַר דִּי יִסְתָּאַב וְלָא **יַדֵּי** עֲלוֹהִי וְיִשְׁתֵּצֵי **אֱנָשָׁא** הַהוּא מִגּוֹ קְהָלָא אֲרֵי יָת מַקְדְּשָׁא דַיְיָ סָאִיב מֵי אַדָּיוּתָא לָא אִזְדְּרִיקוּ עֲלוֹהִי מְסָאָב הוּא:


A person who became ritually unclean [and enters the Sanctuary] without purifying himself [**being sprinkled**], that soul [**person**] will be cut off from the community; for he defiled the Sanctuary of Adonoy, the sprinkling water was not sprinkled upon him, he remains unclean.


###### Onkelos Numbers 19:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:21)


וּתְהֵי לְהוֹן לִקְיַם עֳלָם וּדְיַדֵּי מֵי אַדָּיוּתָא יְצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹהִי וּדְיִקְרַב בְּמֵי אַדָּיוּתָא יְהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא:


This shall be the statute for all times; one who sprinkles the sprinkling waters shall wash his garments, and one who touches the sprinkling waters shall remain unclean until the evening.


###### Onkelos Numbers 19:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/19:22)


וְכֹל דִּי יִקְרַב בֵּיהּ מְסָאָבָא יְהֵי מְסָאָב וֶאֱנַשׁ דְּיִקְרַב בֵּיהּ יְהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא:


Anything which the unclean person touches, shall become unclean; and anyone touching him shall be ritually unclean until the evening.



## Chapter 20

###### Onkelos Numbers 20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:1)


וַאֲתוֹ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא לְמַדְבְּרָא דְצִין בְּיַרְחָא קַדְמָאָה וִיתֵב עַמָּא **בִּרְקָם** וּמִיתַת תַּמָּן מִרְיָם וְאִתְקְבָרַת תַּמָּן:


The entire community of Bnei Yisroel came to the wilderness of Tzin, in the first month. The people settled in Kodeish [**Rekom**], and Miriam died and was buried there.


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:2)


וְלָא הֲוָה מַיָּא לִכְנִשְׁתָּא וְאִתְכְּנָשׁוּ עַל משֶׁה וְעַל אַהֲרֹן:


There was no water for the community, and they assembled against Moshe and Aharon.


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:3)


וּנְצָא עַמָּא עִם משֶׁה וַאֲמָרוּ לְמֵימַר וּלְוֵי דְמִתְנָא בְּמוֹתָא דְאַחָנָא קֳדָם יְיָ:


The people quarreled with Moshe and said, “Would that we had died by our brothers’ death before Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:4)


וּלְמָא אַעֶלְתּוּן יָת קְהָלָא דַיְיָ לְמַדְבְּרָא הָדֵין לִמְמַת תַּמָּן אֲנַחְנָא וּבְעִירָנָא:


Why did you bring the congregation of Adonoy into this wilderness for us and our livestock to die there?


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:5)


וּלְמָא אַסֶּקְתּוּנָא מִמִּצְרַיִם לְאַיְתָאָה יָתָנָא לְאַתְרָא בִישָׁא הָדֵין לָא אָתַר **כְּשַׁר** לְבֵית זְרַע **וְאַף לָא** תֵינִין וְגוּפְנִין וְרִמּוֹנִין וּמַיָּא לֵית לְמִשְׁתֵּי:


Why did you take us out of Egypt and bring us to this terrible place? It is not a place [**fit**] for seed, [**nor for**] figs, grapes, or pomegranates; and there is no water to drink!”


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:6)


וְעַל משֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן מִן קֳדָם קְהָלָא לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וּנְפָלוּ עַל אַפֵּיהוֹן וְאִתְגְּלֵי יְקָרָא דַיְיָ לְהוֹן:


Moshe and Aharon moved away from the assembly to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting, and fell on their faces; the glory of Adonoy appeared to them.


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:7)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying.


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:8)


סַב יָת חוּטְרָא וְאַכְנֵשׁ יָת כְּנִשְׁתָּא אַתְּ וְאַהֲרֹן אָחוּךְ וּתְמַלְּלוּן עִם כֵּיפָא לְעֵינֵיהוֹן וְיִתֵּן מוֹהִי וְתַפֵּק לְהוֹן מַיָּא מִן כֵּיפָא וְתַשְׁקֵי יָת כְּנִשְׁתָּא וְיָת בְּעִירְהוֹן:


“Take the staff and assemble the community, you and Aharon your brother, and speak to the rock in their presence that it may give forth its water; you will then bring forth for them water from the rock, and give drink [to] the community and their livestock.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:9)


וּנְסִיב משֶׁה יָת חוּטְרָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ כְּמָא דִי פַּקְּדֵיהּ:


Moshe took the staff from before Adonoy, as He instructed him.


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:10)


וְאַכְנִישׁוּ משֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן יָת קְהָלָא לָקֳדָם כֵּיפָא וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן שְׁמָעוּ כְעַן סַרְבָנַיָּא הֲמִן כֵּיפָא הָדֵין נַפֵּק לְכוֹן מַיָּא:


Moshe and Aharon assembled the community before the rock; [Moshe] said to them, “Listen, you rebels! Can we extract water from this rock for you?”


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:11)


וַאֲרֵם משֶׁה יָת יְדֵיהּ וּמְחָא יָת כֵּיפָא בְּחוּטְרֵיהּ תַּרְתֵּין זִמְנִין וּנְפָקוּ מַיָּא סַגִּיאֵי וּשְׁתִיאַת כְּנִשְׁתָּא וּבְעִירְהוֹן:


And Moshe raised his hand and struck the rock with his staff twice; water rushed out abundantly, and the community and their livestock drank.


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:12)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה וּלְאַהֲרֹן חֲלַף דִּי לָא הֵמַנְתּוּן **בְּמֵימְרִי** לְקַדָּשׁוּתִי לְעֵינֵי בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּכֵן לָא תַעֲלוּן יָת קְהָלָא הָדֵין לְאַרְעָא דִּי יְהָבִית לְהוֹן:


Adonoy said to Moshe and Aharon, “Because you did not believe in Me [**My word**] to sanctify Me in the presence of Bnei Yisroel; therefore, you will not bring this congregation into the land that I have given them.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:13)


אִנּוּן מֵי מַצּוּתָא דִּי נְצוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל **קֳדָם** יְיָ וְאִתְקַדַּשׁ בְּהוֹן:


They are the waters of dispute where Bnei Yisroel contended with [**before**] Adonoy, and He was sanctified through them.


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:14)


וּשְׁלַח משֶׁה אִזְגַּדִּין **מֵרְקָם** לְוָת מַלְכָּא דֶאֱדוֹם כִּדְנַן אֲמַר אָחוּךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל אַתְּ יְדַעַתְּ יָת כָּל עָקְתָא דִּי אַשְׁכַּחְתָּנָא:


Moshe sent emissaries from Kodeish [**Rekom**] to the King of Edom (saying), “This is what your brother Yisroel declares; you know of all the hardship that we have encountered.


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:15)


וּנְחָתוּ אֲבָהָתָנָא לְמִצְרַיִם וִיתֵבְנָא בְמִצְרַיִם יוֹמִין סַגִּיאִין וְאַבְאִישׁוּ לָנָא מִצְרָאֵי וְלַאֲבָהָתָנָא:


Our forefathers went down to Egypt and we remained in Egypt for a long time; the Egyptians mistreated us and our forefathers.


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:16)


וְצַלֵּינָא קֳדָם יְיָ וְקַבִּיל צְלוֹתָנָא וּשְׁלַח מַלְאָכָא וְאַפְּקָנָא מִמִּצְרָיִם וְהָא אֲנַחְנָא בִרְקַם קַרְתָּא דְּבִסְטַר תְּחוּמָךְ:


Then we cried out [**prayed**] to Adonoy, and He heard our voice [**accept our prayer**], He sent an emissary and led us out of Egypt; now we are in Kodeish [**Rekom**], a city at the edge of your border.


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:17)


נְעִבַּר כְּעַן בְּאַרְעָךְ לָא נְעִבַּר בַּחֲקַל וּבִכְרַם וְלָא נִשְׁתֵּי מֵי גוֹב בְּאֹרַח מַלְכָּא נֵזֵל לָא נִסְטֵי לְיַמִּינָא וְלִשְׂמָאלָא עַד דִּנְעִבַּר תְּחוּמָךְ:


Please let us pass through your land, we will not pass through any field or vineyard, and we will not drink well water; we will travel by the king’s road, we will not turn aside to the right or left, until we have passed through your territory.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:18)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ אֱדוֹמָאָה לָא תְעִבַּר **בִּתְחוּמִי** דִּילְמָא **בִּדְקָטְלִין** בְּחַרְבָּא אֶפּוֹק לָקֳדָמוּתָךְ:


Edom responded, “You shall not pass through me (my land) [**my border**], lest I go out against you [**to kill you**] with the sword!”


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:19)


וַאֲמָרוּ לֵיהּ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּאֹרַח **כְּבִישָׁא** נִסַּק וְאִם מֵיּמָךְ נִשְׁתֵּי אֲנָא וּבְעִירַי וְאֶתֵּן דְּמֵיהוֹן לְחוֹד **לֵית פִּתְגַּם דְּבִישׁ** בְּרַגְלַי אֲעִבַּר:


Bnei Yisroel said, “We will go up by the [**paved**] highway, and if we or our cattle drink your water we will pay for it, it is just nothing, we only want [**with no evil intent**] to pass through on foot.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:20)


וַאֲמַר לָא תְעִבָּר וּנְפַק אֱדוֹמָאָה לָקֳדָמוּתֵיהּ **בְּחֵיל** רַב וּבִידָא תַקִּיפָא:


Then (Edom) responded, “Do not pass through!” Edom came to confront [Bnei Yisroel] with a massive number of people [**an army**] and a strong hand.


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:21)


וְסָרֵיב אֱדוֹמָאָה לְמִשְׁבַק יָת יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמֶעֱבַּר בִּתְחוּמֵיהּ וּסְטָא יִשְׂרָאֵל מִלְּוָתֵיהּ:


Edom refused to allow Yisroel to pass through its territory, and Yisroel turned aside from him.


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:22)


וּנְטָלוּ מֵרְקָם וַאֲתוֹ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא לְהֹר טוּרָא:


They traveled from Kodeish, and the entire community of Bnei Yisroel came to Mount Hor.


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:23)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה וּלְאַהֲרֹן בְּהֹר טוּרָא עַל תְּחוּם אַרְעָא דֶאֱדוֹם לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe and Aharon at Mount Hor, at the border of the land of Edom, saying.


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:24)


יִתְכְּנֵשׁ אַהֲרֹן לְעַמֵּיהּ אֲרֵי לָא יֵיעוֹל לְאַרְעָא דִּי יְהָבִית לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל עַל דִּי סָרִיבְתּוּן עַל מֵימְרִי לְמֵי מַצּוּתָא:
