“Aharon will now be gathered (die) to his people, for he shall not enter the land that I have given to Bnei Yisroel, because you defied My word at the waters of dispute.


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:25)


דְּבַר יָת אַהֲרֹן וְיָת אֶלְעָזָר בְּרֵיהּ וְאַסֵּיק יָתְהוֹן לְהֹר טוּרָא:


Take Aharon and his son Elozor, and bring them up to Mount Hor.


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:26)


וְאַשְׁלַח יָת אַהֲרֹן יָת לְבוּשׁוֹהִי וְתַלְבֵּשִׁנּוּן יָת אֶלְעָזָר בְּרֵיהּ וְאַהֲרֹן יִתְכְּנֵשׁ וִימוּת תַּמָּן:


Remove Aharon’s vestments and dress his son Elozor in them; Aharon will be gathered in and die there.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:27)


וַעֲבַד משֶׁה כְּמָא דִי פַּקִּיד יְיָ וּסְלִיקוּ לְהֹר טוּרָא לְעֵינֵי כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא:


Moshe did as Adonoy commanded; and they went up Mount Hor in the presence of the entire community.


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:28)


וְאַשְׁלַח משֶׁה יָת אַהֲרֹן יָת לְבוּשׁוֹהִי וְאַלְבֵּישׁ יָתְהוֹן יָת אֶלְעָזָר בְּרֵיהּ וּמִית אַהֲרֹן תַּמָּן בְּרֵישׁ טוּרָא וּנְחַת משֶׁה וְאֶלְעָזָר מִן טוּרָא:


Moshe then removed Aharon’s vestments and dressed Aharon’s son, Elozor, in them; Aharon died there on top of the mountain, and Moshe and Elozor descended from the mountain.


###### Onkelos Numbers 20:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/20:29)


וַחֲזוֹ כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא אֲרֵי מִית אַהֲרֹן וּבְכוֹ יָת אַהֲרֹן תְּלָתִין יוֹמִין כֹּל בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל:


The entire community saw that Aharon died; and they wept for Aharon thirty days, the entire house of Yisroel.



## Chapter 21

###### Onkelos Numbers 21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:1)


וּשְׁמַע כְּנַעֲנָאָה מַלְכָּא דַעֲרָד יָתֵב דָּרוֹמָא אֲרֵי אֲתָא יִשְׂרָאֵל אֹרַח מְאַלְלַיָּא **וְאַגַּח קְרָבָא** בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל וּשְׁבָא מִנֵּיהּ שִׁבְיָא:


The Canaanite King of Arad heard—who lived in the south—that Yisroel had come by the route of the spies; he attacked [**waged war with**] Yisroel, and he took from them a captive.


###### Onkelos Numbers 21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:2)


וְקַיֵּם יִשְׂרָאֵל קְיָם **קֳדָם** יְיָ וַאֲמַר אִם מִמְסַר תִּמְסַר יָת עַמָּא הָדֵין בִּידִי וַאֲגַמַּר יָת קִרְוֵיהוֹן:


Yisroel made a vow to [**before**] Adonoy and said, “If You will deliver this people into my hand, I shall consecrate their cities.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:3)


וְקַבֵּיל יְיָ **צְלוֹתֵיהּ** דְּיִשְׂרָאֵל וּמְסַר יָת כְּנַעֲנָאָה וְגַמַּר יָתְהוֹן וְיָת קִרְוֵיהוֹן וּקְרָא שְׁמָא דְאַתְרָא חָרְמָה:


Adonoy heard Yisroel’s voice [**prayers**], and He delivered the Canaanites (into Yisroel’s hand), and he (Yisroel) destroyed them and [consecrated] their cities; the region was named Chormo.


###### Onkelos Numbers 21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:4)


וּנְטָלוּ מְהֹר טוּרָא אֹרַח יַמָּא דְסוּף לְאַקָּפָא יָת אַרְעָא דֶאֱדוֹם וַעֲקַת **נַפְשָׁא** דְעַמָּא בְּאוֹרְחָא:


They then traveled from Mount Hor by the Reed Sea route, in order to circle around the land of Edom; traveling was insufferable to the [**soul of the**] people.


###### Onkelos Numbers 21:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:5)


**וְאִתְרַעַם** עַמָּא **בְּמֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ וְעִם משֶׁה נְצוֹ לְמָא אַסֶּקְתּוּנָא מִמִּצְרַיִם לִמְמַת בְּמַדְבְּרָא אֲרֵי לֵית לַחְמָא וְלֵית מַיָּא וְנַפְשָׁנָא עָקַת בְּמַנָּא הָדֵין **דְּמֵיכְלֵיהּ קָלִיל**:


The people spoke out [**complained**] against [**the word of**] God and [**they fought**] Moshe, “Why did you bring us up out of Egypt to die in the wilderness? There is no food and no water, and we are disgusted with this rotten bread [**which is eaten simply**].”


###### Onkelos Numbers 21:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:6)


וְגָרֵי יְיָ בְּעַמָּא יָת חִיוַן קָלָן וּנְכִיתוּ יָת עַמָּא וּמִית עַם סַגִּי מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל:


Adonoy sent against the people the consuming snakes, and they bit the people, and many people of Yisroel died.


###### Onkelos Numbers 21:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:7)


וַאֲתָא עַמָּא לְמשֶׁה וַאֲמָרוּ חַבְנָא אֲרֵי **אִתְרַעַמְנָא קֳדָם** יְיָ וְעִמָּךְ **נְצֵינָא** צַלִּי **קֳדָם** יְיָ וְיַעֲדִּי מִנָּנָא יָת חִוְיָא וְצַלִּי משֶׁה עַל עַמָּא:


The people came to Moshe and said, “We have sinned by speaking against [**complaining before**] Adonoy and [**fighting with**] you; pray to [**before**] Adonoy that He remove the snakes from us.” Moshe prayed on behalf of the people.


###### Onkelos Numbers 21:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:8)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה עֲבֵיד לָךְ קָלְיָא וְשַׁוֵּי יָתֵיהּ עַל אָת וִיהֵי כָּל דְּיִתְנְכִית וְיֶחֱזֵי יָתֵיהּ וְיִתְקַיָּם:


Adonoy said to Moshe, “Make yourself [the image of] a consuming snake, and place it upon a banner; and anyone bitten will look at it and live.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 21:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:9)


וַעֲבַד משֶׁה חִוְיָא דִנְחָשָׁא וְשַׁוְיֵיהּ עַל אָת וַהֲוָה כַּד נָכִית חִוְיָא יָת גַּבְרָא וּמִסְתַּכֵּל לְחִוְיָא דִנְחָשָׁא וּמִתְקַיָּם:


Moshe made a copper snake and placed it on the banner; whenever a snake bit a person, he would gaze upon the copper snake and live.


###### Onkelos Numbers 21:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:10)


וּנְטָלוּ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וּשְׁרוֹ בְּאֹבֹת:


Bnei Yisroel traveled on and camped in Ovos.


###### Onkelos Numbers 21:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:11)


וּנְטָלוּ מֵאֹבֹת וּשְׁרוֹ בִּמְגִיזַת עֲבָרָאֵי בְּמַדְבְּרָא דִּי עַל אַפֵּי מוֹאָב מִמַּדְנַח שִׁמְשָׁא:


They traveled from Ovos and camped in the wasteland passes in the wilderness, facing Moav, eastward of the sun.


###### Onkelos Numbers 21:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:12)


מִתַּמָּן נְטָלוּ וּשְׁרוֹ בְּנַחֲלָא דְזָרֶד:


They traveled from there and camped in the valley of Zered.


###### Onkelos Numbers 21:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:13)


מִתַּמָּן נְטָלוּ וּשְׁרוֹ מֵעִבְרָא דְאַרְנוֹן דִּי בְמַדְבְּרָא דְּנָפֵק מִתְּחוּם אֱמוֹרָאָה אֲרֵי אַרְנוֹן תְּחוּם מוֹאָב בֵּין מוֹאָב וּבֵין אֱמוֹרָאָה:


They traveled from there and camped opposite Arnon, in the wilderness, extending from the Emorite border; Arnon being the Moav border between Moav and the Emorite.


###### Onkelos Numbers 21:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:14)


עַל כֵּן יִתְאַמַּר בְּסִפְרָא קְרָבִין דַּעֲבַד יְיָ עַל יַמָּא דְסוּף **וּגְבוּרַן** דְּעַל נַחֲלֵי אַרְנוֹן:


Concerning this will be told in the Book of the Wars of Adonoy, “He gave at the Reeds (Sea) and [**the might at**] the valleys of Arnon.


###### Onkelos Numbers 21:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:15)


וְשָׁפוּךְ נַחֲלַיָּא דְּמִדַּבְּרִין לָקֳבֵל לְחַיָּת וּמִסְתְּמִיךְ לִתְחוּם מוֹאָב:


And the spillage in the valleys when it turned to dwell at Ar, and then leaned on the border of Moav.


###### Onkelos Numbers 21:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:16)


וּמִתַּמָּן אִתְיְהִיבַת לְהוֹן בֵּירָא הִיא בֵירָא דִּי אֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה כְּנוֹשׁ יָת עַמָּא וְאֶתֵּן לְהוֹן מַיָּא:


From there, to the well; this is the well of which Adonoy said to Moshe, ‘Gather the people and I will give them water.’”


###### Onkelos Numbers 21:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:17)


בְּכֵן שַׁבַּח יִשְׂרָאֵל יָת **תּוּשְׁבַּחְתָּא** הָדָא סַקִּי בֵירָא שַׁבָּחוּ לָהּ:


It was then that Yisroel sang this song [**of praise**]: “Arise, O well, sing to it!


###### Onkelos Numbers 21:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:18)


בֵּירָא דְּחָפְרוּהָ רַבְרְבַיָּא כְּרוּהָ רֵישֵׁי עַמָּא סַפְרַיָּא בְּחוּטְרֵיהוֹן וּמִמַּדְבְּרָא **אִתְיְהִיבַת לְהוֹן**:


The well dug by princes, that the nobles of the nation excavated, through the lawgiver, with their staffs; from the wilderness a gift [**was given to them**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 21:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:19)


**וּמִדְּאִתְיְהִיבַת** לְהוֹן נָחֲתָא עִמְּהוֹן לְנַחֲלַיָּא וּמִנַחֲלַיָּא **סָלְקָא** עִמְּהוֹן לְרָמָתָא:


The gift [**given to them**] (traveled) to the valley, and from the valley [**rose with them**] to the heights.


###### Onkelos Numbers 21:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:20)


וּמֵרָמָתָא לְחֵילַיָּא דִּי בְּחַקְלֵי מוֹאָב רֵישׁ רָמָתָא וּמִסְתַּכְיָא עַל אַפֵּי בֵית יְשִׁימוֹן:


And from the heights to the valley that is in the field of Moav, at the top of the peak that overlooks the wastelands.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 21:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:21)


וּשְׁלַח יִשְׂרָאֵל אִזְגַּדִּין לְוָת סִיחוֹן מַלְכָּא דֶאֱמוֹרָאָה לְמֵימָר:


Yisroel sent emissaries to Sichon, King of the Emorite, saying.


###### Onkelos Numbers 21:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:22)


אֲעִבַר בְּאַרְעָךְ לָא נִסְטֵי בַּחֲקַל וּבִכְרַם לָא נִשְׁתֵּי מֵי גוֹב בְּאֹרַח מַלְכָּא נֵזֵל עַד דִּי נְעִבַר תְּחוּמָךְ:


“Let me pass through your land, we will not turn aside into the fields and vineyards, we will not drink well water; we will go by the King’s road until we have passed through your territory.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 21:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:23)


וְלָא שְׁבַק סִיחוֹן יָת יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמֶעְבַּר בִּתְחוּמֵיהּ וּכְנַשׁ סִיחוֹן יָת כָּל עַמֵּיהּ וּנְפַק לָקֳדָמוּת יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמַדְבְּרָא וַאֲתָא לְיָהַץ **וִאַגִּיחַ קְרָבָא** בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל:


But Sichon did not allow Yisroel to pass through his territory; Sichon gathered all his people and went out against Yisroel to the wilderness; he came to Yohatz and attacked [**waged war with**] Yisroel.


###### Onkelos Numbers 21:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:24)


וּמְחָהִי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְפִתְגַּם דְּחָרֶב וִירֵת יָת אַרְעֵיהּ מְאַרְנוֹנָא עַד יוּבְקָא עַד בְּנֵי עַמּוֹן אֲרֵי תַקִּיף תְּחוּמָא דִּבְנֵי עַמּוֹן:


Yisroel defeated him with the edge of the sword and took possession of his land; from Arnon to the Yabok, as far as Amon[’s border], because Amon’s border was impregnable.


###### Onkelos Numbers 21:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/21:25)


**וּכְבַשׁ** יִשְׂרָאֵל יָת כָּל קִרְוַיָּא הָאִלֵּין וִיתֵב יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּכָל קִרְוֵי אֱמוֹרָאָה בְּחֶשְׁבּוֹן וּבְכָל **כַּפְרָנָהָא**:

Moav said to the elders of Midian, “Now this assembly will lick up [**consume**] everything around us, just as the ox laps up the herbs of the field.” Balak the son of Tzipor was King of Moav at that time.

He sent emissaries to Bil’am the son of Beor, to Pesor—which is by the river [**Peras**], the land of his people—to call him, saying: “Behold, a nation came out of Egypt, behold they have covered [**obscured the sunshine from**] the face of the earth, and is located across from me.

And now, please come and curse this nation for me, for it is too mighty for me, perhaps it will enable us to strike at [**wage war with**] him, and banish them from the land; for I know, that whomever you bless is blessed, and whomever you curse is cursed.”

הָא עַמָּא דִּי נְפַק מִמִּצְרַיִם **וַחֲפָא יָת עֵין שִׁמְשָׁא** דְאַרְעָא כְּעַן אִיתָא לוֹט לִי יָתֵיהּ מָאִים אֵיכוּל לְאַגָּחָא בֵיהּ קְרָב וַאֲתָרֵכִנֵּיהּ:

Bil’am answered and said to Balak's servants: “Even if Balak would give me silver and gold enough to fill his house, I cannot [**have no permission**] transgress the word of Adonoy my God, by doing anything small or great.


###### Onkelos Numbers 22:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/22:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/22:19)


וּכְעַן אוֹרִיכוּ כְעַן הָכָא אַף אַתּוּן בְּלֵילְיָא וְאִדַּע מָא יוֹסֵף יְיָ לְמַלָּלָא עִמִּי:


Now, you also, please remain here overnight, and I will get to know what more will Adonoy tell me.”

וַאֲתָא **מֵימַר מִן קֳדָם** יְיָ לְבִלְעָם בְּלֵילְיָא וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ אִם לְמִקְרֵי לָךְ אֲתוֹ גֻבְרַיָּא קוּם אִזֵּל עִמְּהוֹן וּבְרַם יָת פִּתְגָּמָא דִּי אֲמַלֵּל עִמָּךְ יָתֵיהּ תַּעְבֵּד:


God[**’s word**] appeared [**before**] to Bil’am that night and said to him: “If the men have come to call you, arise and go with them; however, whatever I will say to you, you will do.”

Bil’am arose in the morning, saddled [**readied**] his donkey, and went with the Moavite dignitaries.

God showed anger because he went, and an angel of Adonoy placed himself in the way to thwart him, as he was riding on his donkey accompanied by his two attendants.

But the angel of Adonoy stood in a narrow path of the vineyards [**at a place**] with a barrier at either side.

When the donkey saw the angel of Adonoy, she was pressed against the wall and pressed Bil’am's foot against the wall; and he struck her even more.

Adonoy’s angel passed further ahead and stood in a narrow place, where there was no room to turn [either] to the right or to the left.

When the donkey saw the angel of Adonoy, it crouched beneath Bil’am; Bil’am[**’s anger was provoked**] became angry and beat the donkey with the stick.

Adonoy opened the mouth of the donkey, and she said to Bil’am: “What have I done to you, that you have hit me these three times?”

Bil’am said to the donkey: “Because you have ridiculed me; would that I had a sword in my hand, I would kill you now.”

וַאֲמָרַת אַתָּנָא לְבִלְעָם הֲלָא אֲנָא אַתָּנָךְ דִּי רְכַבְתְּ עֲלַי **מִדְּאִיתָךְ** עַד יוֹמָא הָדֵין הֲמֵילַף אַלִּיפְנָא לְמֶעְבַּד לָךְ כְּדֵין וַאֲמַר לָא:


The donkey replied to Bil’am: “Am I not the very same donkey that you have been riding on all your life [**from the time I became yours**] until this very day? Was it ever my habit to do this to you?” And he said: “No.”

Adonoy then enabled Bil’am to see, and he observed the angel of Adonoy standing in the way with his sword drawn in his hand. He bowed his head and prostrated himself on his face.

The angel of Adonoy said to him: “Why did you hit your donkey these three times? Behold, I came out to obstruct you, because your way is [**it is clear to me that you desire to proceed on a path**] contrary to me.

And when the donkey saw me, it turned aside these three times; had she not turned aside before me as [she did] now I would surely have killed you and she, I would let live.”

Bil’am said to the angel of Adonoy: “I have sinned because I did not know that you were standing in the way before me; now therefore, if it is wicked in your eyes, I shall return.”

The angel of Adonoy said to Bil’am: “Go with the men, but only the word that I will speak to you shall you speak” Bil’am then continued to go with Balak's dignitaries.

When Balak heard that Bil’am was coming, he went out to meet him, to the city of Moav, which is on the boundary of Arnon—at the very edge of the boundary.

Balak said to Bil’am: “Did I not send so many [dignitaries] to invite you? Why did you not come to me? Am I really [**can you truthfully say that I am**] incapable of honoring you?”

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/22:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/22:38)


וַאֲמַר בִּלְעָם לְבָלָק הָא אָתֵיתִי לְוָתָךְ כְּעַן הֲמֵיכַל יָכִלְנָא לְמַלָּלָא מִדָּעָם פִּתְגָּמָא דִּישַׁוִּי יְיָ בְּפוּמִי יָתֵיהּ אֲמַלֵּל:


Bil’am replied to Balak: “Now that I have come to you, have I any power to say anything? Only the word that God puts in my mouth will I speak.”

Bil’am went with Balak and they came to Kiryas Chutzos [**the city of his territory**].

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/22:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/22:40)


וּנְכֵס בָּלָק תּוֹרִין וְעָאן וּשְׁלַח לְבִלְעָם וּלְרַבְרְבַיָּא דִּי עִמֵּיהּ:


Balak slaughtered cattle and sheep and he sent some to Bil’am and to the dignitaries that were with him.

In the morning, Balak took Bil’am and he led him up to Bamos Ba’al [**the high place of his idol**]; from there he saw part of the people.



## Chapter 23

###### Onkelos Numbers 23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/23:1)


וַאֲמַר בִּלְעָם לְבָלָק בְּנֵה לִי הָכָא שַׁבְעָא מַדְבְּחִין וְאַתְקֵן לִי הָכָא שַׁבְעָא תוֹרִין וְשַׁבְעָא דִכְרִין:


Bil’am said to Balak: “Build seven altars for me here, and prepare for me seven bulls and seven rams.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/23:2)


וַעֲבַד בָּלָק כְּמָא דִי מַלֵּיל בִּלְעָם וְאַסֵּק בָּלָק וּבִלְעָם תּוֹר וּדְכַר עַל כָּל מַדְבְּחָא:


Balak did as Bil’am requested. Balak and Bil’am then sacrificed as burnt-offerings an ox and a ram on each altar.


###### Onkelos Numbers 23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/23:3)


וַאֲמַר בִּלְעָם לְבָלָק אִתְעַתַּד עַל עֲלָתָךְ וְאֵיהַךְ מָאִים יְעָרַע **מֵימַר מִן קֳדָם** יְיָ לָקֳדָמוּתִי וּפִתְגָּמָא דְיַחֲזִנַּנִי וְאֵחַוֵּי לָךְ וַאֲזַל יְחִידִי:


Bil’am said to Balak: “Stand beside your burnt-offering and I will go away. Perhaps, [**a word from before**] Adonoy will appear to me, and whatever He will show me I will tell you.” And he went away alone.


###### Onkelos Numbers 23:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/23:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/23:4)


וַעֲרַע **מֵימַר מִן קֳדָם** יְיָ לְוָת בִּלְעָם וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ יָת שַׁבְעָא מַדְבְּחִין סַדָּרִית וְאַסֵּקִית תּוֹר וּדְכַר עַל כָּל מַדְבְּחָא:


[**A word from before**] God chanced to appear to Bil’am, and he said to Him: “I have set up the seven altars and I offered [as burnt-offerings] an ox and a ram on the altar.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 23:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/23:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/23:5)


וְשַׁוִּי יְיָ פִּתְגָּמָא בְּפוּמָא דְבִלְעָם וַאֲמַר תּוּב לְוָת בָּלָק וּכְדֵין תְּמַלֵּל:


Adonoy put a message in Bil’am's mouth and He said: “Return to Balak and tell him as follows.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 23:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/23:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/23:6)


וְתָב לְוָתֵיהּ וְהָא מְעַתַּד עַל עֲלָתֵיהּ הוּא וְכָל רַבְרְבֵי מוֹאָב:


When he returned, Balak was still standing next to his burnt-offering together with all the dignitaries of Moav.


###### Onkelos Numbers 23:7

Bil’am set forth his parable, and said: “From Aram has Balak, King of Moav, brought me, out of the mountains of the east [saying]: 'Come, curse Yaakov for me; go, invoke wrath against Yisroel.

## Chapter 24

###### Onkelos Numbers 24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:1)


וַחֲזָא בִלְעָם אֲרֵי **תַקִּין קֳדָם** יְיָ לְבָרָכָא יָת יִשְׂרָאֵל וְלָא הֲלַךְ כִּזְמַן בִּזְמַן אֶלָּהֵן לָקֳדָמוּת נְחָשַׁיָּא וְשַׁוִּי לְמַדְבְּרָא אַפּוֹהִי:


When Bil’am saw that it was favorable [**fitting before**] to Adonoy to bless Yisroel, he did not approach, as at each time, towards sorcery; but he set his face toward the wilderness.


###### Onkelos Numbers 24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:2)


וּזְקֵף בִּלְעָם יָת עֵנוֹהִי וַחֲזָא יָת יִשְׂרָאֵל שָׁרָן לְשִׁבְטוֹהִי וּשְׁרַת עֲלוֹהִי רוּחַ **נְבוּאָה מִן קֳדָם** יְיָ:


Bil’am raised his eyes and saw Israel encamped according to its tribes, and the spirit of God rested upon him [**prophesy from before Hashem**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:3)


וּנְטַל מַתְלֵיהּ וַאֲמַר אֵמַר בִּלְעָם בְּרֵיהּ בְּעוֹר וְאֵמַר גַּבְרָא **דְּשַׁפִּיר חָזֵי**:


He set forth his parable, and said: “This is the declaration of Bil’am, the son of Be’or; this is the declaration of the man of the open-socketed eye [**who sees well**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 24:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:4)


אֵמַר דִּשְׁמַע מֵימַר **מִן קֳדָם** אֵל וְחֵזוּ **מִן קֳדָם** שַׁדַּי חָזֵי שְׁכִיב **וּמִתְגְּלֵי לֵיהּ**:


This is the declaration of one who hears [**from before**] God's utterance, who sees the vision of [**from before**] the Almighty; fallen, with opened eyes [**it is apparent to him**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 24:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:5)


מָא טָבָא **אַרְעָךְ** יַעֲקֹב בֵּית מֵישְׁרָיךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


How goodly are your tents [**land**], Yaakov, your dwelling places, Yisroel.


###### Onkelos Numbers 24:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:6)


כְּנַחֲלִין **דְּמִדַּבְּרִין כְּגִנַּת שַׁקְיָא דְעַל פְּרָת** כְּבוּסְמַיָּא דִּנְצִיב יְיָ כְּאַרְזִין **דִּנְצִיבִין** עַל מַיָּא:


They stretch forth like streams, like gardens by the river [**flowing like irrigated gardens near Peras**], like the aloe trees that Adonoy has planted; like cedar trees [**standing**] by the water.


###### Onkelos Numbers 24:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:7)


**יִסְגֵּי מַלְכָּא דְּיִתְרַבָּא מִבְּנוֹהִי וְיִשְׁלוֹט בְּעַמְמִין סַגִּיאִין** וְיִתְקוֹף מֵאֲגַג מַלְכֵּיהּ וְתִתְנְטֵל מַלְכוּתֵיהּ:


Water flows out from his buckets, and his seed will have an abundant flow [**the anointed king shall grow and rule over many nations**]; his king will be greater than Agag and his monarchy shall be uplifted.


###### Onkelos Numbers 24:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:8)


אֱלָהָא דְּאַפֵּקִנּוּן מִמִּצְרַיִם **תָּקְפָּא וְרוּמָא דִּילֵיהּ יֵיכְלוּן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל נִכְסֵי עַמְמַיָּא סַנְאֵיהוֹן וּבְבִזַּת מַלְכֵּיהוֹן יִתְפַּנְּקוּן וְאַרְעַתְהוֹן יַחְסְנוּן**:


God Who has brought him out of Egypt, has shown His great strength to him [**might and loftiness is His**]; He will consume His enemy nations, crushing their bones and dip His arrows [in their blood] [**Bnei Yisroel will eat from the wealth of the nations—their enemies, and will enjoy the spoils of their kings, and will inhabit their land**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 24:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:9)


**יְנוּחַ יִשְׁרֵי בִתְקוֹף** כְּאַרְיָא וּכְלֵיתָא **לֵית מַלְכוּ דִתְזַעְזְעִינֵיהּ** בְּרִיכָךְ יְהוֹן בְּרִיכִין וְלִיטָיךְ יְהוֹן לִיטִין:


He crouches and lies [**rests and dwells with might**] like a lion, and, being a lion, who would dare rouse him? [**No kingdom can cause him fear.**] those who bless you are blessed, and those who curse you are cursed.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 24:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:10)


וּתְקֵף רוּגְזָא דְבָלָק בְּבִלְעָם וּשְׁקָפִנּוּן לִידוֹהִי וַאֲמַר בָּלָק לְבִלְעָם לְמֵילַט סַנְאַי קְרִיתָךְ וְהָא בָּרָכָא מְבָרְכַת לְהוֹן דְּנַן תְּלַת זִמְנִין:


Balak became enraged at Bil’am and clapped his hands together; Balak then said to Bil’am: “I called you to curse my enemies, but you have blessed them these three times.


###### Onkelos Numbers 24:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:11)


וּכְעַן **אֵזִיל** לָךְ לְאַתְרָךְ אֲמָרִית יְקָרָא אֲיַקְּרִנָּךְ וְהָא מְנָעָךְ יְיָ מִן יְקָר:


Now, hurry [**go**] back home! I said I would exceedingly honor you, but Adonoy has held you back from honor.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 24:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:12)


וַאֲמַר בִּלְעָם לְבָלָק הֲלָא אַף לְאִזְגַדָּיךְ דִּי שַׁלְּחַת לְוָתִי מַלֵּלִית לְמֵימָר:


Bil’am said to Balak: “Even to your emissaries whom you sent to me I said:


###### Onkelos Numbers 24:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:13)


אִם יִתֶּן לִי בָלָק מְלֵי בֵיתֵיהּ כְּסַף וּדְהַב לֵית **לִי רְשׁוּ** לְמֶעְבַּר עַל **גְּזֵרַת מֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ לְמֶעְבַּד טַבְתָא אוֹ בִישְׁתָא **מֵרְעוּתִי** דִּימַלֵּל יְיָ יָתֵיהּ אֲמַלֵּל:


‘Even if Balak would give me his house full of silver and gold, I cannot transgress the word of Adonoy to do good or evil [**from**] on my own [**will**];’ only what Adonoy declares will I speak.


###### Onkelos Numbers 24:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:14)


וּכְעַן הָא אֲנָא אָזֵל לְעַמִּי אִיתָא אֲמַלְכִינָךְ **מָא דְתַעְבֵּד** וַאֲחַוֵּי לָךְ מָא דְיַעְבֵּד עַמָּא הָדֵין לְעַמָּךְ בְּסוֹף יוֹמַיָּא:


And now that I am returning to my people, come, I will advise you [**what you should do and I will show you**] what this people will do to your people in the end of days.”

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:15)


וּנְטַל מַתְלֵיהּ וַאֲמַר אֵמַר בִּלְעָם בְּרֵיהּ בְּעוֹר וְאֵמַר גַּבְרָא **דְּשַׁפִּיר חָזֵי**:


He set forth his parable, and said: “This is the declaration of Bil’am the son of Beor, the declaration of the man, of the open-sockered eye [**sees well**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 24:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:16)


אֵמַר דִּשְׁמַע מֵימַר מִן **קֳדָם** אֵל וִידַע מַדַּע מִן **קֳדָם** עִלָּאָה חֵזוּ מִן קֳדָם שַׁדַּי חָזֵי שְׁכִיב **וּמִתְגְּלֵי לֵיהּ**:


This is the declaration of one who hears God's utterances and knows the thoughts of the Most Exalted; who sees the vision of the Almighty; fallen, with opened eyes [**and is apparent to him**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 24:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:17)


חֲזִיתֵיהּ וְלָא כְעַן סְכִיתֵיהּ **וְלָא אִיתוֹהִי קָרִיב כַּד יְקוּם מַלְכָּא** מִיַּעֲקֹב **וְיִתְרַבָּא מְשִׁיחָא** מִיִּשְׁרָאֵל **וְיִקְטוֹל רַבְרְבֵי** מוֹאָב **וְיִשְׁלוֹט בְּכָל בְּנֵי אֱנָשָׁא**:


I see it but not now, I perceive it but not in the near future; a star [**king**] has gone forth from Yaakov, and a staff has arisen [**the Moshiach will be magnified by**] from Yisroel, which will smash the corners [**kill the leaders**] of Moav, and impale all of the sons of Sheis [**will rule over mankind**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 24:18

וִיהֵי אֱדוֹם יְרֻתָּא וִיהֵי יְרֻתָּא שֵׂעִיר לְבַעֲלֵי דְבָבוֹהִי וְיִשְׂרָאֵל **יַצְלַח בְּנִכְסִין**:


Edom will be possession and Seir shall become the possession of his enemies; but Yisroel will triumph [**be successful in enterprise**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 24:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:19)


וְיֵחוּת חַד **מִדְּבֵית** יַעֲקֹב וְיוֹבֵד מְשֵׁזֵב מִקִּרְיַת עַמְמַיָּא:


A ruler shall come from [**the house of**] Yaakov, who will annihilate the survivors of the city.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 24:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:20)


וַחֲזָא יָת עֲמַלְקָאָה וּנְטַל מַתְלֵיהּ וַאֲמַר רֵישׁ **קְרָבַיָּא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל** הֲוָה עֲמַלְקָאָה וְסוֹפֵיהּ לְעָלְמָא יֵיבָד:


When Bil’am saw Amalek, he declared his parable, and said: “Amalek was the first among nations [**to fight with Yisroel**], and his end will be total destruction.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 24:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:21)


וַחֲזָא יָת **שַׁלְמָאָה** וּנְטַל מַתְלֵיהּ וַאֲמַר תַּקִּיף בֵּית מוֹתְבָךְ וְשַׁוִּי **בִכְרַךְ תַּקִּיף מְדוֹרָךְ**:


[When] he saw the Keinite [**Shalmites**], he set forth his parable, and said: “How powerful is your dwelling place, and set in a cliff your nest [**your dwelling in a forified city**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 24:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:22)


אֲרֵי אִם יְהֵי **לְשֵׁצָאָה שַׁלְמָאָה** עַד מָא אֲתוּרָאָה יִשְׁבִּנָּךְ:


For, if Kayin is to be uprooted [**the Shalmites are to be destroyed**], until where will Ashur take you captive?”


###### Onkelos Numbers 24:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:23)


וּנְטַל מַתְלֵיהּ וַאֲמַר **וַי לְחַיָּבַיָּא דְּיֵחוֹן כַּד יַעֲבֵּד אֱלָהָא יָת אִלֵּין**:


He set forth his parable, and said: “Alas! Who can survive from God's devastation? [**Woe to those evil people who are living when Hashem does this.**]


###### Onkelos Numbers 24:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:24)


**וְסִיעַן יִצְטָרְחַן מֵרוֹמָאֵי** וִיעַנּוּן לְאָתוּר **וְיִשְׁתַּעְבְּדוּן לְעֵבַר פְּרָת** וְאַף אִנּוּן לְעָלְמָא יֵיבְדוּן:


Warships will come from Kittim [**groups will beg help from Rome**] and destroy Ashur, and cause suffering to those on the other side [**will enslave those beyond Peras**], but they, too, shall be lost for eternity.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 24:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/24:25)

Bil’am arose, went, and returned home; and Balak too, went on his way.



## Chapter 25

###### Onkelos Numbers 25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:1)


וִיתֵב יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּשִׁטִּין וְשָׁרֵי עַמָּא לְמִטְעֵי בָּתַר בְּנַת מוֹאָב:


Yisroel settled in Shittim and the people [of Yisroel] began acting promiscuously with the daughters of Moav.


###### Onkelos Numbers 25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:2)


וּקְרָא לְעַמָּא לְדִבְחֵי **טַעֲוַתְהוֹן** וַאֲכַל עַמָּא וּסְגִידוּ **לְטַעֲוַתְהוֹן**:


They [girls of Moav] invited the people [of Yisroel] to their idolatrous sacrifices [**abominations**]; and the people ate and prostrated themselves before their gods [**abominations**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:3)


וְאִתְחַבַּר יִשְׂרָאֵל לְבַעֲלָא פְעוֹר וּתְקֵף רָגְזָא דַיְיָ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל:


Yisroel became united with Ba’al Pe’or; and the rage of Adony flared against Yisroel.


###### Onkelos Numbers 25:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:4)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה דְּבַר יָת כָּל רֵישֵׁי עַמָּא **וְדוּן וּקְטוֹל דְּחַיַּב קְטוֹל קֳדָם** יְיָ לָקֳבֵל שִׁמְשָׁא וִיתוּב תְּקוֹף רוּגְזָא דַיְיָ מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל:


Adonoy said to Moshe: “Take all the leaders of the people and hang them, [**judge and execute those guilty by death**] before Adonoy, facing the sun; thereby removing the rage of Adony against Yisroel.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 25:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:5)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְדַיָּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל קְטוּלוּ גְּבַר גַּבְרוֹהִי דְּאִתְחַבָּרוּ לְבַעֲלָא פְעוֹר:


Moshe said to the judges of Yisroel: “Each of you must execute his men who were united with Ba’al Pe’or.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 25:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:6)


וְהָא גַּבְרָא מִבְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אֲתָא וְקָרֵב לְוָת אֲחוֹהִי יָת מִדְיָנֵיתָא לְעֵינֵי משֶׁה וּלְעֵינֵי כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְאִנּוּן בָּכָן בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא:


And behold, a man came from among Bnei Yisroel, and brought the Midianite woman to his brethren, before the eyes of Moshe and before the eyes of the entire congregation of Yisroel, who were weeping at the entrance to the Tent of Meeting.


###### Onkelos Numbers 25:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:7)


וַחֲזָא פִּינְחָס בַּר אֶלְעָזָר בַּר אַהֲרֹן כַּהֲנָא וְקָם מִגּוֹ כְנִשְׁתָּא וּנְסֵיב רָמְחָא בִּידֵיהּ:


When he saw this—Pinchas the son of Elazar [and grand]son of Aharon the *Kohein*—he rose up from the midst of the community and took a spear in his hand.


###### Onkelos Numbers 25:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:8)


וְעַל בָּתַר גַּבְרָא בַר יִשְׂרָאֵל לְקוּבָתָא וּבְזַע יָת תַּרְוֵיהוֹן יָת גַּבְרָא בַר יִשְׂרָאֵל וְיָת אִתְּתָא לִמְעָהָא וְאִתְכְּלִי מוֹתָנָא מֵעַל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


He followed the Israelite man into the inner chamber of the tent and pierced both of them through—the Israelite man and the woman through her belly—and the plague stopped from [afflicting] the Bnei Yisroel.


###### Onkelos Numbers 25:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:9)


וַהֲווֹ דְמִיתוּ בְמוֹתָנָא עַשְׂרִין וְאַרְבְּעָא אַלְפִין:


Those that died in the plague, [numbered] twenty-four thousand.


###### Onkelos Numbers 25:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:10)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe saying.


###### Onkelos Numbers 25:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:11)


פִּינְחָס בַּר אֶלְעָזָר בַּר אַהֲרֹן כַּהֲנָא אֲתֵב יָת חֵימְתִי מֵעַל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל בִּדְקַנֵּי יָת קִנְאָתִי בֵּינֵיהוֹן וְלָא שֵׁיצֵיתִי יָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּקִנְאָתִי:


“Pinchas, the son of Elozor, [and grand] son of Aharon the *kohein,* has turned My anger away from Bnei Yisroel by his vengeance for Me among them, so that I did not destroy Bnei Yisroel in My vengeance.


###### Onkelos Numbers 25:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:12)


בְּכֵן אֱמַר הָא אֲנָא **גְזַר** לֵיהּ יָת קְיָמִי שְׁלָם:


Therefore tell [him], that I give [**decree for**] him My covenant [of] peace.


###### Onkelos Numbers 25:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:13)


וּתְהֵי לֵיהּ וְלִבְנוֹהִי בַתְרוֹהִי קְיַם כְּהֻנַּת עֳלָם חֲלַף דִּי קַנֵּי **קֳדָם** אֱלָהֵיהּ וְכַפַּר עַל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


It shall be for him and his descendants after him a covenant of eternal *kehunah* because he was zealous for [**before**] his God and made atonement for Bnei Yisroel.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 25:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:14)


וְשׁוּם גַּבְרָא בַר יִשְׂרָאֵל קְטִילָא דִּי אִתְקְטֵל עִם מִדְיָנֵיתָא זִמְרִי בַּר סָלוּא רַב בֵּית אַבָּא לְבֵית שִׁמְעוֹן:


The name of the slain Israelite man, who was killed along with the Midianite woman, was Zimri, son of Salu, leader of a paternal house of Shimon.


###### Onkelos Numbers 25:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:15)


וְשׁוּם אִתְּתָא דְאִתְקְטִילַת מִדְיָנֵתָא כָּזְבִּי בַת צוּר רֵישׁ אֻמֵּי בֵּית אַבָּא בְּמִדְיָן הוּא:


The name of the slain Midianite woman was Kozbi, daughter of Tzur, the national leader of a paternal house in Midian.


###### Onkelos Numbers 25:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:16)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe saying:


###### Onkelos Numbers 25:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:17)


אָעֵק יָת מִדְיָנָאֵי וְתִקְטוֹל יָתְהוֹן:


“Antagonize the Midianites and kill them,


###### Onkelos Numbers 25:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/25:18)


אֲרֵי מְעִיקִין אִנּוּן לְכוֹן בְּנִכְלֵיהוֹן דִּי נְכִילוּ לְכוֹן עַל עֵסַק פְּעוֹר וְעַל עֵסַק כָּזְבִּי בַת רַבָּא דְמִדְיָן אֲחַתְהוֹן דְּאִתְקְטִילַת בְּיוֹמָא דְמוֹתָנָא עַל עֵסַק פְּעוֹר:


for they antagonized you with their guiles in the matter of Pe’or, and in the matter of Kozbi the daughter of a Midianite prince, their sister, who was killed on the day of the plague that resulted from Pe’or.”



## Chapter 26

###### Onkelos Numbers 26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:1)


וַהֲוָה בָּתַר מוֹתָנָא וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה וּלְאֶלְעָזָר בַּר אַהֲרֹן כַּהֲנָא לְמֵימָר:


It was after the plague. Adonoy spoke to Moshe and Elozor, son of Aharon the *kohein,* saying:


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:2)


**קַבִּילוּ** יָת חֻשְׁבַּן כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מִבַּר עַשְׂרִין שְׁנִין וּלְעֵלָּא לְבֵית אֲבָהַתְהוֹן כָּל נָפֵק חֵילָא בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל:


“[**Take the**] count [**of**] the whole community of Bnei Yisroel twenty years of age and above, according to their paternal house, every one who is fit for duty in the army of Yisroel.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:3)


וּמַלִּיל משֶׁה וְאֶלְעָזָר כַּהֲנָא אֲמָרוּ **לְמִמְנֵי** יָתְהוֹן בְּמֵישְׁרַיָּא דְמוֹאָב עַל יַרְדֵּנָא דִירֵחוֹ לְמֵימָר:


Moshe and Elozor the *kohein* spoke with [**to count**] them on the plains of Moav, by the Yardein near Yereicho, saying:


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:4)


מִבַּר עַשְׂרִין שְׁנִין וּלְעֵלָּא כְּמָא דִי פַקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה וּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל דִּי נְפָקוּ מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם:


“[Count those] twenty years of age and above, as Adonoy commanded Moshe and Bnei Yisroel who had left Egypt.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:5)


רְאוּבֵן בּוּכְרָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל בְּנֵי רְאוּבֵן חֲנוֹךְ זַרְעִית חֲנוֹךְ לְפַלּוּא זַרְעִית פַּלּוּא:


Reuvein, [was] Yisroel’s firstborn; the descendants of Reuvein are: the Chanochite family from Chanoch, the Paluite family from Palu,


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:6)


לְחֶצְרוֹן זַרְעִית חֶצְרוֹן לְכַרְמִי זַרְעִית כַּרְמִי:


the Chetzronite family from Chetzron and the Karmite family from Karmi.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:7)


אִלֵּין זַרְעֲיַת רְאוּבֵן וַהֲווֹ מִנְיָנֵיהוֹן אַרְבְּעִין וּתְלָתָא אַלְפִין וּשְׁבַע מְאָה וּתְלָתִין:


These are Reuvein’s families and they numbered forty-three thousand, and seven hundred and thirty.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:8)


וּבְנֵי פַלּוּא אֱלִיאָב:


Palu’s son[s were] was Eliav.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:9)


וּבְנֵי אֱלִיאָב נְמוּאֵל וְדָתָן וַאֲבִירָם הוּא דָתָן וַאֲבִירָם מְעַרְעֵי כְנִשְׁתָּא דְּאִתְכְּנָשׁוּ עַל משֶׁה וְעַל אַהֲרֹן בִּכְנִשְׁתָּא דְקֹרַח בְּאִתְכַּנּוֹשֵׁיהוֹן עַל יְיָ:


Eliav’s sons were Nemueil, Dasan and Aviram. They are Dasan and Aviram, the communal leaders, who incited against Moshe and Aharon in the assembly of Korach when they incited against Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:10)


וּפְתָחַת אַרְעָא יָת פּוּמָהּ וּבְלָעַת יָתְהוֹן וְיָת קֹרַח בְּמוֹתָא דִכְנִשְׁתָּא כַּד אֲכָלַת אֶשָּׁתָא יָת מָאתָן וְחַמְשִׁין גַּבְרָא וַהֲווֹ לְאָת:


The earth opened its mouth and swallowed them and Korach when that assembly died; when fire consumed the two hundred and fifty men; they became a symbol.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:11)


וּבְנֵי קֹרַח לָא מִיתוּ:


But the sons of Korach did not die.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:12)


בְּנֵי שִׁמְעוֹן לְזַרְעֲיַתְהוֹן לִנְמוּאֵל זַרְעִית נְמוּאֵל לְיָמִין זַרְעִית יָמִין לְיָכִין זַרְעִית יָכִין:


The descendants of Shimon by their families are: the Nemuelite family from Nemuel, the Yaminite family from Yamin, the Yachinite family from Yachin,


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:13)


לְזֶרַח זַרְעִית זָרַח לְשָׁאוּל זַרְעִית שָׁאוּל:


the Zarchite family from Zerach, [and] the Shaulite family from Shaul.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:14)


אִלֵּין זַרְעֲיַת שִׁמְעוֹן עַשְׂרִין וּתְרֵין אַלְפִין וּמָאתָן:


These are the families of Shimon—twenty- two thousand and two hundred.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:15)


בְּנֵי גָד לְזַרְעֲיַתְהוֹן לִצְפוֹן זַרְעִית צְפוֹן לְחַגִּי זַרְעִית חַגִּי לְשׁוּנִי זַרְעִית שׁוּנִי:


The descendants of Gad by their families are: The Tzefonite family from Tzefon, the Chagite family from Chagi, the Shunite family from Shuni,


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:16)


לְאָזְנִי זַרְעִית אָזְנִי לְעֵרִי זַרְעִית עֵרִי:


the Oznite family from Ozni, the Eirite family from Eiri,


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:17)


לַאֲרוֹד זַרְעִית אֲרוֹדִי לְאַרְאֵלִי זַרְעִית אַרְאֵלִי:


the Arodite family from Arod, and the Areilite family from Areili.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:18)


אִלֵּין זַרְעֲיַת בְּנֵי גָד לְמִנְיָנֵיהוֹן אַרְבְּעִין אַלְפִין וַחֲמֵשׁ מְאָה:


These are the families of Gad’s descendants and they numbered forty thousand and five hundred.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:19)


בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה עֵר וְאוֹנָן וּמִית עֵר וְאוֹנָן בְּאַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן:


The sons of Yehudah are Eir and Onan; Eir and Onan died in the land of Canaan.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:20)


וַהֲווֹ בְנֵי יְהוּדָה לְזַרְעֲיַתְהוֹן לְשֵׁלָה זַרְעִית שֵׁלָה לְפֶרֶץ זַרְעִית פֶּרֶץ לְזֶרַח זַרְעִית זָרַח:


The descendants of Yehudah by their families are: The Sheilanite family from Sheila, the Partzite family from Peretz, the Zarchite family from Zerach.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:21)


וַהֲווֹ בְנֵי פֶרֶץ לְחֶצְרוֹן זַרְעִית חֶצְרוֹן לְחָמוּל זַרְעִית חָמוּל:


The descendants of Peretz were: The Chetzronite family from Chetzron, and the Chamulite family from Chamul.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:22)


אִלֵּין זַרְעֲיַת יְהוּדָה לְמִנְיָנֵיהוֹן שַׁבְעִין וְשִׁתָּא אַלְפִין וַחֲמֵשׁ מְאָה:


These are the families of Yehudah and they numbered seventy-six thousand and five hundred.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:23)


בְּנֵי יִשָׂשכָר לְזַרְעֲיַתְהוֹן תּוֹלָע זַרְעִית תּוֹלָע לְפֻוָּה זַרְעִית פּוּנִי:


The descendants of Yissachar by their families are: The Tolaite family from Tola, the Punite family from Puva,


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:24)


לְיָשׁוּב זַרְעִית יָשׁוּב לְשִׁמְרוֹן זַרְעִית שִׁמְרוֹן:


the Yashuvite family from Yashuv, and the Shimronite family from Shimron.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:25)


אִלֵּין זַרְעֲיַת יִשָׂשׂכָר לְמִנְיָנֵיהוֹן שִׁתִּין וְאַרְבְּעָא אַלְפִין וּתְלַת מְאָה:


These are the families of Yissachar and they numbered sixty-four thousand and three hundred.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:26)


בְּנֵי זְבוּלֻן לְזַרְעֲיַתְהוֹן לְסֶרֶד זַרְעִית סֶרֶד לְאֵלוֹן זַרְעִית אֵלוֹן לְיַחְלְאֵל זַרְעִית יַחְלְאֵל:


The descendants of Zevulun by their families are: The Sardite family from Sered, the Eilonite family from Eilon, [and] the Yachle’eilite family from Yachle’eil.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:27)


אִלֵּין זַרְעֲיַת זְבוּלֻן לְמִנְיָנֵיהוֹן שִׁתִּין אַלְפִין וַחֲמֵשׁ מְאָה:


These are the families of Zevulun and they numbered sixty thousand and five hundred.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:28)


בְּנֵי יוֹסֵף לְזַרְעֲיַתְהוֹן מְנַּשֶּׁה וְאֶפְרָיִם:


The descendants of Yoseif by their families are: Menashe and Ephraim.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:29)


בְּנֵי מְנַשֶּׁה לְמָכִיר זַרְעִית מָכִיר וּמָכִיר אוֹלִיד יָת גִּלְעָד לְגִלְעָד זַרְעִית גִּלְעָד:


The descendants of Menashe are: The Machirite family from Machir; Machir’s son was Gil’ad, the Gil’adite family from Gil’ad.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:30)


אִלֵּין בְּנֵי גִּלְעָד אִיעֶזֶר זַרְעִית אִיעֶזֶר לְחֵלֶק זַרְעִית חֵלֶק:


The descendants of Gil’ad are: The I’ezerite family from I’ezer, the Chelkite family from Cheilek,


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:31)


וְאַשְׂרִיאֵל זַרְעִית אַשְׂרִיאֵלִי וְשֶׁכֶם זַרְעִית שֶׁכֶם:


the Asrieilite family from Asrieil, the Shechemite family from Shechem,


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:32)

the Shemidaite family from Shemida, and the Chefrite family from Cheifer.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:33)


וּצְלָפְחָד בַּר חֵפֶר לָא הֲווֹ לֵיהּ בְּנִין אֶלָּהֵן בְּנָן וְשׁוּם בְּנַת צְלָפְחָד מַחְלָה וְנוֹעָה חָגְלָה מִלְכָּה וְתִרְצָה:


Tzelofchad the son of Cheifer had no sons, only daughters; The name[s] of Tzelofchad’s daughters were Machlah, No’ah, Choglah, Milkah and Sirtzah.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:34)

These are the families of Menashe and they numbered fifty-two thousand and seven hundred.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:35)

The descendants of Ephraim by their families are: The Shusalchite family from Shuselach, the Bachrite family from Becher, and the Tachanite family from Tachan.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:36)

These are the sons of Shuselach: The Eiranite family from Eiran.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:37

These are the families of Ephraim’s descendants, and they numbered thirty-two thousand and five hundred; these are the descendants of Yoseif by their families.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:38

The descendants of Binyamin by their families were: The Balite family from Bela, the Ashbelite family from Ashbeil, the Achiramite family from Achiram,

the Shufamite family from Shfufam, and the Chufamite family from Chufam.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:40)

The sons of Bela were Ard and Na’aman [giving rise to] the Ardite family, and the Na’amite family from Na’aman.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:41)

These are the descendants of Binyamin by their families, and they numbered forty-five thousand and six hundred.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:42)

These are the descendants of Don by their families: The Shuchamite family from Shucham, these are Don’s families.

All Shuchamite families numbered sixty-four thousand and four hundred.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:44)

The descendants of Asher by their families are: The Yimnite family from Yimnah, the Yishvite family from Yishvi, [and] the Bri’ite family from Briah .


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:45)

The descendants of Briah are: The Chevronite family from Chever, the Malkieilite family from Malkieil.

The name of Asher’s daughter was Serach.

אִלֵּין זַרְעֲיַת בְּנֵי אָשֵׁר לְמְנְיְנֵיהוֹן חַמְשִׁין וּתְלָתָא אַלְפִין וְאַרְבַּע מְאָה:


These are the families of Asher and they numbered fifty-three thousand and four hundred.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:48](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:48)

The descendants of Naftali by their families are: the Yachtze’eilite family from Yachtze’eil, the Gunite family from Guni,


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:49

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:49](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:49)

the Yitzrite family from Yeitzer, and the Shileimite family from Shileim.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:50

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:50](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:50)


אִלֵּין זַרְעֲיַת נַפְתָּלִי לְזַרְעֲיַתְהוֹן וּמִנְיָנֵיהוֹן אַרְבְּעִין וְחַמְשָׁא אַלְפִין וְאַרְבַּע מְאָה:


These are the families of Nafatli and they numbered forty-five thousand and four hundred.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:51

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:51](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:51)


אִלֵּין מִנְיָנֵי בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל שִׁית מְאָה וְחַד אַלְפִין שְׁבַע מְאָה וּתְלָתִין:


The total number of Bnei Yisroel was six hundred and one thousand, seven hundred and thirty.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:52

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:52](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:52)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe saying.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:53

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:53](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:53)


לְאִלֵּין תִּתְפְּלֵג אַרְעָא בְּאַחֲסָנָא בְּמִנְיַן שְׁמָהָן:


“The land shall be apportioned among these, as an inheritance according to the number of names.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:54

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:54](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:54)


לְסַגִּיאֵי תַּסְגּוּן אַחֲסַנְתְּהוֹן וְלִזְעִירֵי תַּזְעֲרוּן אַחֲסַנְתְּהוֹן גְּבַר לְפוּם מִנְיָנוֹהִי תִּתְיְהַב אַחֲסַנְתֵּיהּ:


To the large [tribe], increase their inheritance, and to the small, diminish their inheritance; each person, according to his number, shall be given his inheritance.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:55

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:55](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:55)


בְּרַם בְּעַדְבָא תִּתְפְּלֵג יָת אַרְעָא לִשְׁמָהָן שִׁבְטֵי אֲבָהַתְהוֹן יַחְסְנוּן:


Only by lot should the land be divided, according to the names of their fathers’ tribes should they inherit it.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:56

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:56](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:56)


עַל פּוּם עַדְבָא תִּתְפְּלֵג אַחֲסַנְתְּהוֹן בֵּין סַגִּיאֵי לִזְעִירֵי:


By word of the lottery should their inheritance be divided, with regard to whether they are many or few.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:57

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:57](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:57)


וְאִלֵּין מִנְיָנֵי לֵוָאֵי לְזַרְעֲיַתְהוֹן לְגֵרְשׁוֹן זַרְעִית גֵּרְשׁוֹן לִקְהָת זַרְעִית קְהָת לִמְרָרִי זַרְעִית מְרָרִי:


These are the numbered ones of the Levites by their families: The Gershonite family of Gershon, the Kehosite family of Kehos, [and] the Merorite family of Merori.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:58

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:58](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:58)


אִלֵּין זַרְעֲיַת לֵוִי זַרְעִית לִבְנִי זַרְעִית חֶבְרוֹן זַרְעִית מַחְלִי זַרְעִית מוּשִׁי זַרְעִית קֹרַח וּקְהָת אוֹלִיד יָת עַמְרָם:


These are the families of Levi: the Livnite family, the Chevronite family, the Machlite family, the Mushite family, and the Korchite family; Kehos’ son was Amram.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:59

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:59](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:59)


וְשׁוּם אִתַּת עַמְרָם יוֹכֶבֶד בַּת לֵוִי דִילֵדַת יָתַהּ לְלֵוִי בְּמִצְרָיִם וִילֵדַת לְעַמְרָם יָת אַהֲרֹן וְיָת משֶׁה וְיָת מִרְיָם אֲחָתְהוֹן:


The name of Amram’s wife was Yocheved, daughter of Levi, whom [her mother] bore to Levi in Egypt. She bore to Amram, Aharon, Moshe, and their sister Miriam.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:60

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:60](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:60)


וְאִתְיְלִיד לְאַהֲרֹן יָת נָדָב וְיָת אֲבִיהוּא יָת אֶלְעָזָר וְיָת אִיתָמָר:


To Aharon were borne, Nadav and Avihu, Elozor and Isamar.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:61

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:61](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:61)


וּמִית נָדָב וַאֲבִיהוּא בְּקָרוֹבֵהוֹן אֶשָּׁתָא נוּכְרֵתָא קֳדָם יְיָ:


Nadav and Avihu died when they offered a forbidden fire before Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:62

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:62](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:62)


וַהֲווֹ מִנְיָנֵיהוֹן עַשְׂרִין וּתְלָתָא אַלְפִין כָּל דְּכוּרָא מִבַּר יַרְחָא וּלְעֵלָּא אֲרֵי לָא אִתְמְנִיאוּ בְּגוֹ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אֲרֵי לָא אִתְיְהִיבַת לְהוֹן אַחֲסָנָא בְּגוֹ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


They numbered twenty-three thousand, for all males from one month and older. They were not counted among Bnei Yisroel, since no inheritance was given to them among Bnei Yisroel.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:63

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:63](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:63)


אִלֵּין מִנְיָנֵי משֶׁה וְאֶלְעָזָר כַּהֲנָא דִּי מְנוֹ יָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּמֵישְׁרַיָּא דְמוֹאָב עַל יַרְדֵּנָא דִירֵחוֹ:


This is the census of Moshe and Elozor the *kohein* who counted the Bnei Yisroel in the plains of Moav, at Yardein Yereicho.


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:64

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:64](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:64)


וּבְאִלֵּין לָא הֲוָה גְבַר מִמִּנְיָנֵי משֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן כַּהֲנָא דִּי מְנוֹ יָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְסִינָי:


Among this [census] there was no man who had been included in the census of Moshe and Aharon the *kohein,* who had counted Bnei Yisroel in the wilderness of Sinai,


###### Onkelos Numbers 26:65

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:65](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/26:65)


אֲרֵי אֲמַר יְיָ לְהוֹן מֵמַת יְמוּתוּן בְּמַדְבְּרָא וְלָא אִשְׁתְּאַר מִנְהוֹן אֱנַשׁ אֶלָּהֵן כָּלֵב בַּר יְפֻנֶּה וִיהוֹשֻׁעַ בַּר נוּן:


because Adonoy said to them that they would die in the desert. No one was left with the exception of Kaleiv, son of Yefuneh and Yehoshua, son of Nun.



## Chapter 27

###### Onkelos Numbers 27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:1)


וְקָרֵיבָא בְּנַת צְלָפְחָד בַּר חֵפֶר בַּר גִּלְעָד בַּר מָכִיר בַּר מְנַשֶּׁה לְזַרְעֲיַת מְנַשֶּׁה בַּר יוֹסֵף וְאִלֵּין שְׁמָהַת בְּנָתֵיהּ מַחְלָה נוֹעָה וְחָגְלָה וּמִלְכָּה וְתִרְצָה:


And they approached—the daughters of Tzelofchad, son of Cheifer, son of Gil’ad, son of Machir, son of Menashe, of the families of Menashe the son of Yoseif. The names of his daughters were: Machlah, No’ah, Choglah, Milkah and Sirtzah.


###### Onkelos Numbers 27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:2)


וְקָמָא קֳדָם משֶׁה וְקֳדָם אֶלְעָזָר כַּהֲנָא וְקֳדָם רַבְרְבַיָּא וְכָל כְּנִשְׁתָּא בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לְמֵימָר:


They stood before Moshe and before Elozor the *kohein,* and before the princes, and the entire community at the Tent of Meeting entrance, saying:


###### Onkelos Numbers 27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:3)


אָבוּנָא מִית בְּמַדְבְּרָא וְהוּא לָא הֲוָה בְּגוֹ כְנִשְׁתָּא דְּאִזְדַּמָּנוּ עַל יְיָ בִּכְנִשְׁתָּא דְקֹרַח אֲרֵי בְחוֹבֵיהּ מִית וּבְנִין לָא הֲווֹ לֵיהּ:


“Our father died in the wilderness, but he was not among the group that banded together against Adonoy, in the company of Korach, but he died because of his own sin without leaving any sons.


###### Onkelos Numbers 27:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:4)


לְמָא **יִתְמְנַע** שְׁמָא דְאָבוּנָא מִגּוֹ זַרְעִיתֵיהּ אֲרֵי לֵית לֵיהּ בָּר הַב לָנָא אַחֲסָנָא בְּגוֹ אֲחֵי אָבוּנָא:


Why should the name of our father be omitted [**held back**] from his family because he did not have a son? Give us a possession [of land] among our father’s brothers.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 27:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:5)


וְקָרֵב משֶׁה יָת דִּינְהֶן קֳדָם יְיָ:


Moshe brought their case before Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Numbers 27:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:6)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe saying:


###### Onkelos Numbers 27:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:7)


יָאוּת בְּנַת צְלָפְחָד מְמַלְּלָן מִתַּן תִּתֵּן לְהוֹן אֲחוּדַת אַחֲסָנָא בְּגוֹ אֲחֵי אֲבוּהֶן וְתַעֲבַר יָת אַחֲסָנַת אֲבוּהֶן לְהֵן:


“Tzelofchad’s daughters speak justly, give them a hereditary portion of land among their father’s brothers, and transfer the inheritance of their father to them.


###### Onkelos Numbers 27:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:8)


וְעִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל תְּמַלֵּל לְמֵימָר גְּבַר אֲרֵי יְמוּת וּבַר לֵית לֵיהּ וְתַעְבְּרוּן יָת אַחֲסַנְתֵּיהּ לִבְרַתֵּיהּ:


Speak to Bnei Yisroel and tell them, that if a man dies and has no son, his hereditary property shall be transferred to his daughter.


###### Onkelos Numbers 27:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:9)


וְאִם לֵית לֵיהּ בְּרַתָּא וְתִתְּנוּן יָת אַחֲסַנְתֵּיהּ לַאֲחוֹהִי:


If he has no daughter, you shall give his hereditary property to his brothers.


###### Onkelos Numbers 27:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:10)


וְאִם לֵית לֵיהּ אַחִין וְתִתְּנוּן יָת אַחֲסַנְתֵּיהּ לַאֲחֵי אֲבוּהִי:


If he has no brothers, you shall give his hereditary property to his father’s brothers.


###### Onkelos Numbers 27:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:11)


וְאִם לֵית אַחִין לַאֲבוּהִי וְתִתְּנוּן יָת אַחֲסַנְתֵּיהּ לְקָרִיבֵיהּ דְּקָרִיב לֵיהּ מִזַּרְעִיתֵיהּ וְיֵרַת יָתַהּ וּתְהֵי לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לִגְזֵרַת דִּין כְּמָא דִי פַּקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה:


If his father had no brothers, you shall give his hereditary property to the relative closest to him in his family who will then inherit it. This shall remain a decreed law for Bnei Yisroel, as Adonoy had commanded Moshe.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 27:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:12)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה סַק לְטוּרָא דַעֲבָרָאֵי הָדֵין וַחֲזֵי יָת אַרְעָא דִּי יְהָבִית לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


Adonoy said to Moshe: “Go up this Avarim Mountain and look at the land that I have given to Bnei Yisroel.


###### Onkelos Numbers 27:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:13)


וְתֶחֱזֵי יָתָהּ וְתִתְכְּנֵשׁ לְעַמָּיךְ אַף אָתְּ כְּמָא דִי אִתְכְּנִישׁ אַהֲרֹן אָחוּךְ:


[After] you have seen it, you too will be gathered to your people, just as your brother Aharon was gathered.


###### Onkelos Numbers 27:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:14)


כְּמָא דִי סָרֵיבְתּוּן עַל מֵימְרִי בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְצִן בְּמַצּוּת כְּנִשְׁתָּא לְקַדָּשׁוּתִי בְמַיָּא לְעֵינֵיהוֹן אִנּוּן מֵי מַצּוּת **רְקַם** מַדְבְּרָא דְצִן:


Because you disobeyed My words in the wilderness of Tzin, when the community quarreled, [you were] to sanctify Me through the water, before their eyes.” Those were the waters of dispute at Kadeish [**Rekam**], in the wilderness of Tzin.


###### Onkelos Numbers 27:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:15)


וּמַלִּיל משֶׁה עִם יְיָ לְמֵימָר:


Moshe spoke to Adonoy, saying:


###### Onkelos Numbers 27:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:16)


יְמַנֵּי יְיָ אֱלָהָא רוּחַיָּא לְכָל בִּסְרָא גְּבַר עַל כְּנִשְׁתָּא:


“Let Adonoy, God of the spirits, appoint a man over the community,


###### Onkelos Numbers 27:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:17)


דִּי יִפּוֹק קֳדָמֵיהוֹן וְדִי יֵעוֹל קֳדָמֵיהוֹן וְדִי יַפֵּקִנּוּן וְדִי יְעֵלִנּוּן וְלָא תְהֵי כְּנִשְׁתָּא דַיְיָ כְּעָנָא דִי לֵית לְהֵן רָעֵי:


who will go forth before them, and who will come back before them, and who would lead them out and bring them in, so that the community of Adonoy shall not be like sheep that have no shepherd.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 27:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:18)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה דְבַר לָךְ יָת יְהוֹשֻׁע בַּר נוּן גְּבַר דִּי רוּחַ **נְבוּאָה** בֵיהּ וְתִסְמוֹךְ יָת יְדָךְ עֲלוֹהִי:


Adonoy said to Moshe: “Take to yourself Yehoshua son of Nun, a man in whom there is spirit [**of prophecy**], and lay your hand on him.


###### Onkelos Numbers 27:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:19)


וּתְקֵים יָתֵיהּ לָקֳדָם אֶלְעָזָר כַּהֲנָא וְלָקֳדָם כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא וּתְפַקֵּד יָתֵיהּ לְעֵינֵיהוֹן:


Stand him before Elozor the *kohein* and before the entire community, and command him before their eyes.


###### Onkelos Numbers 27:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:20)


וְתִתֵּן מִזִּיוָךְ עֲלוֹהִי בְּדִיל דִּי **יְקַבְּלוּן מִנֵּיהּ** כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא דִּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


Bestow some of your radiance on him, so that the entire community of Bnei Yisroel will hear [**be receptive to him**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 27:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:21)


וְלָקֳדָם אֶלְעָזָר כַּהֲנָא יְקוּם וְיִשְׁאַל לֵיהּ בְּדִין אוּרַיָּא קֳדָם יְיָ עַל מֵימְרֵיהּ יְהוֹן נָפְקִין וְ**עַל מֵימְרֵיהּ** יְהוֹן עַלִּין הוּא וְכָל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל עִמֵּיהּ וְכָל כְּנִשְׁתָּא:


He shall stand before Elozor the *kohein,* and ask, of him, through the judgment of the *Urim* before Adonoy. By his word they will come out and [**by his word they will**] go in—he, all Bnei Yisroel with him, and the entire community.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 27:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:22)


וַעֲבַד משֶׁה כְּמָא דִי פַּקִּיד יְיָ יָתֵיהּ וּדְבַר יָת יְהוֹשֻׁעַ וַאֲקִימֵיהּ קֳדָם אֶלְעָזָר כַּהֲנָא וּקֳדָם כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא:


Moshe did as Adonoy commanded him. He took Yehoshua and presented him to Elozor the *kohein* and to the entire community.


###### Onkelos Numbers 27:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/27:23)


וּסְמַךְ יָת יְדוֹהִי עֲלוֹהִי וּפַקְּדֵיהּ כְּמָא דִי מַלִּיל יְיָ בִּידָא דְמשֶׁה:


He laid his hands on him and commanded him, just as Adonoy had commanded through Moshe.



## Chapter 28

###### Onkelos Numbers 28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/28:1)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe saying:


###### Onkelos Numbers 28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/28:2)


פַּקֵּד יָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן יָת קוּרְבָּנִי לְחֵם **סִדּוּר** לְקוּרְבָּנִי **לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְרַעֲוָא** תִּטְּרוּן לְקָרָבָא **קֳדָמַי** בְּזִמְנֵיהּ:


“Command Bnei Yisroel and say to them, ‘My offering, My food of [**prepared for**] My fires, a pleasing aroma [**to be accepted willingly**] to Me, you shall be vigilant to offer to [**before**] Me at its prescribed time’.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/28:3)


וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן דֵּין קוּרְבָּנָא דִּי תְקָרְבוּן קֳדָם יְיָ אִמְּרִין בְּנֵי שְׁנָא שַׁלְמִין תְּרֵין לְיוֹמָא עֲלָתָא תְדִירָא:


Say to them: “This is the fire-offering that you shall offer to Adonoy; yearling lambs without blemish, two each day, as a constant (daily) burnt-offering.


###### Onkelos Numbers 28:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/28:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/28:4)


יָת אִמְּרָא חַד תַּעֲבֵּד בְּצַפְרָא וְיָת אִמְּרָא תִנְיָנָא תַּעֲבֵּד בֵּין שִׁמְשַׁיָּא:


Offer one lamb in the morning and offer the second lamb in the afternoon.


###### Onkelos Numbers 28:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/28:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/28:5)


וְחַד מִן עַשְׂרָא **בִּתְלַת סְאִין** סוּלְתָּא לְמִנְחָתָא דְּפִילָא בִּמְשַׁח כְּתִישָׁא רַבְעוּת הִינָא:


And one tenth of an *eiphah* [**three se’in**] of fine flour as a meal-offering, mixed with beaten oil measuring one fourth of a *hin.*


###### Onkelos Numbers 28:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/28:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/28:6)


עֲלָתָא תְּדִירָא דְאִתְעֲבִידָא בְּטוּרָא דְסִינַי **לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְרַעֲוָא** קוּרְבָּנָא **קֳדָם** יְיָ:


[This is] a constant (daily) burnt-offering as offered on Mount Sinai, for a pleasing aroma [**to be accepted willingly**], a fire-offering to [**before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Numbers 28:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/28:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/28:7)


וְנִסְכֵּיהּ רַבְעוּת הִינָא לְאִמְּרָא חַד בְּקוּדְשָׁא יִתְנַסַּךְ נִסּוּךְ **דַּחֲמַר עַתִּיק קֳדָם** יְיָ:


Its libation [shall be] one fourth of a *hin* for the one lamb, in the Holy [Sanctuary], you shall pour an intoxicating libation [**of aged wine**] to [**before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Numbers 28:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/28:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/28:8)


וְיָת אִמְּרָא תִנְיָנָא תַּעְבֵּד בֵּין שִׁמְשַׁיָּא כְּמִנְחַת צַפְרָא וּכְנִסְכֵּיהּ תַּעְבֵּד קֻרְבַּן **דְּמִתְקַבַּל בְּרַעֲוָא קֳדָם** יְיָ:


Offer the second lamb in the afternoon, with the same meal-offering of the morning together with its libation you shall offer it, a fire-offering of a pleasing aroma [**accepted willingly**] to [**before**] Adonoy.

## Chapter 29

###### Onkelos Numbers 29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/29:1)


וּבְיַרְחָא שְׁבִיעָאָה בְּחַד לְיַרְחָא מְעָרַע קַדִּישׁ יְהֵי לְכוֹן כָּל עִבִידַת פָּלְחַן לָא תַעְבְּדוּן יוֹם **יַבָּבָא יְהֵי לְכוֹן**:


The first day of the seventh month shall be a sacred holiday to you when you shall not do any work of consequence. It shall be a day of sounding the ram’s horn [**wailing for you**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/29:2)


וְתַעַבְּדוּן עֲלָתָא **לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְרַעֲוָא קֳדָם** יְיָ תּוֹר בַּר תּוֹרֵי חַד דְּכַר חַד אִמְּרִין בְּנֵי שְׁנָא שַׁבְעָא שַׁלְמִין:


You shall bring a burnt-offering of a pleasing aroma [**accepted willingly**] to [**before**] Adonoy, [consisting of] one young bull, one ram, and seven yearling lambs, [all] without blemish.


###### Onkelos Numbers 29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/29:3)


וּמִנְחַתְהוֹן סֻלְתָּא דְּפִילָא בִמְשָׁח תְּלָתָא עִשְׂרוֹנִין לְתוֹרָא תְּרֵין עִשְׂרוֹנִין לְדִכְרָא:


Their meal-offering [shall be] fine flour mixed with [olive] oil, three tenths [of an *eiphah*] for the bull, two tenths [of an *eiphah*] for the one ram,


###### Onkelos Numbers 29:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/29:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/29:4)

and one tenth [of an *eiphah*] for each of the seven lambs.


###### Onkelos Numbers 29:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/29:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/29:5)


וּצְפִיר בַּר עִזֵּי חַד לְחַטָּאתָא לְכַפָּרָא עֲלֵיכוֹן:


[You should also bring] one he-goat as a sin-offering to make atonement for you.


###### Onkelos Numbers 29:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/29:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/29:6)


בַּר מֵעֲלַת יַרְחָא וּמִנְחָתַהּ וַעֲלַת תְּדִירָא וּמִנְחָתַהּ וְנִסְכֵּיהוֹן **כִּדְחָזֵי לְהוֹן** **לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְרַעֲוָא** קֻרְבָּנָא **קֳדָם** יְיָ:


Aside from the [Rosh] Chodesh burnt-offering and its meal-offering, and the constant (daily) burnt-offering and its required meal-offering and libations [**appropriate for them**], for a pleasing aroma [**accepted willingly**], a fire-offering to [**before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Numbers 29:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/29:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/29:7)


וּבְעַשְׂרָא לְיַרְחָא שְׁבִיעָאָה הָדֵין מְעָרַע קַדִּישׁ יְהֵי לְכוֹן וּתְעַנּוּן יָת נַפְשָׁתֵיכוֹן כָּל עִבִידְתָּא לָא תַעַבְּדוּן:


On the tenth day of this seventh month there shall be a sacred holiday to you, when you shall fast and not do any work.


###### Onkelos Numbers 29:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/29:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/29:8)


וּתְקָרְבוּן עֲלָתָא **קֳדָם** יְיָ **לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְרַעֲוָא** תּוֹר בַּר תּוֹרֵי חַד דְּכַר חַד אִמְּרִין בְּנֵי שְׁנָא שַׁבְעָא שַׁלְמִין יְהוֹן לְכוֹן:


You shall bring a burnt-offering [**before**] Adonoy, for a pleasing aroma [**accepted willingly**], [consisting of] one young bull, one ram, and seven yearling lambs. They shall [all] be without blemish.

Their meal-offering [shall be] fine flour mixed with [olive] oil, three tenths [of an *eiphah*] for the bull, two tenths [of an *eiphah*] for the one ram,
