## Chapter 30

###### Onkelos Numbers 30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:1)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל כְּכֹל דִּי פַקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה:


Moshe spoke to Bnei Yisroel all that Adonoy had commanded him.


###### Onkelos Numbers 30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:2)


וּמַלִּיל משֶׁה עִם רֵישֵׁי שִׁבְטַיָּא לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמֵימָר דֵּין פִּתְגָּמָא דִּי פַּקִּיד יְיָ:


Moshe spoke to the tribal leaders of Bnei Yisroel saying: “This is the word that Adonoy has commanded.


###### Onkelos Numbers 30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:3)


גְּבַר אֲרֵי יִדַּר נְדַר **קֳדָם** יְיָ אוֹ יְקַיֵּם קְיָם לְמֵסַר אֵסַר עַל נַפְשֵׁיהּ לָא יְבַטַּל פִּתְגָּמֵיהּ כְּכָל דְּיִפּוֹק מִפּוּמֵיהּ יַעֲבֵּד:


If a man makes a vow to [**before**] Adonoy or makes an oath; to initiate a prohibition himself; he may not profane his word. He shall do all that he said.


###### Onkelos Numbers 30:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:4)


וְאִתְּתָא אֲרֵי תִדַּר נְדַר **קֳדָם** יְיָ וְתֵסַר אֵיסַר בְּבֵית אָבוּהָא בְּרַבְיוּתָהָא:


[But] if a woman makes a vow to [**before**] Adonoy or obligates herself in her father’s home while still in her youth,


###### Onkelos Numbers 30:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:5)


וְיִשְׁמַע אָבוּהָא יָת נִדְרָהּ וְאֵסָרָהּ דִּי אֲסָרַת עַל נַפְשַׁהּ וְיִשְׁתּוֹק לָהּ אָבוּהָא וִיקוּמוּן כָּל נִדְרָהָא וְכָל אֵסָרֵי דִּי אֲסָרַת עַל נַפְשַׁהּ יְקוּמוּן:


and her father hears her vow or her self-imposed prohibition and remains silent, then all her vows shall stand and the self-imposed prohibition shall stand.


###### Onkelos Numbers 30:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:6)


וְאִם אַעְדִּי אָבוּהָא יָתַהּ בְּיוֹמָא דִשְׁמַע כָּל נִדְרָהָא וֶאֱסָרָהָא דִּי אֲסָרַת עַל נַפְשַׁהּ לָא יְקוּמוּן **וּמִן קֳדָם** יְיָ יִשְׁתְּבֵק לָהּ אֲרֵי אַעְדִּי אָבוּהָא יָתַהּ:


But if her father restrained her on the day he heard it, then all her vows and self imposed prohibitions shall not stand. [**From before**] Adonoy will forgive her because her father has restrained her.


###### Onkelos Numbers 30:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:7)


וְאִם מֶהֱוָה תֶהֱוֵי לִגְבַר וְנִדְרָהָא עֲלַהּ אוֹ פֵּרוּשׁ סִפְוָתָהָא דִּי אֲסָרַת עַל נַפְשָׁהּ:


If she is [married] to a man, and her vows were upon herself, or an utterance of self-imposed prohibitions,


###### Onkelos Numbers 30:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:8)


וְיִשְׁמַע בַּעֲלַהּ בְּיוֹמָא דִשְׁמַע וְיִשְׁתּוֹק לַהּ וִיקוּמוּן נִדְרָהָא וֶאֱסָרָהָא דִּי אֲסָרַת עַל נַפְשַׁהּ יְקוּמוּן:


and her husband heard about them and remained silent on the day he heard, then her vows shall stand, and the self-imposed prohibitions shall stand.


###### Onkelos Numbers 30:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:9)


וְאִם בְּיוֹמָא דִשְׁמַע בַּעֲלַהּ אַעְדִּי יָתַהּ וּבַטַּל יָת נִדְרָהָא דִּי עֲלַהּ וְיָת פֵּרוּשׁ סִפְוָתָהָא דִּי אֲסָרַת עַל נַפְשַׁהּ **וּמִן קֳדָם** יְיָ יִשְׁתְּבֵיק לַהּ:


But, if on the day her husband hears about it, he restrains her, he then annuls her vow which was upon her, and self-imposed verbal prohibitions, and [**from before**] Adonoy will forgive her.


###### Onkelos Numbers 30:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:10)


וּנְדַר אַרְמְלָא וּמְתַרְכָא כֹּל דִּי אֲסָרַת עַל נַפְשַׁהּ יְקוּם עֲלַהּ:


The vow of a widow or a divorced woman—everything she prohibited for herself shall remain incumbent upon her.


###### Onkelos Numbers 30:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:11)


וְאִם בֵּית בַּעֲלַהּ נְדָרַת אוֹ אֲסָרַת אֵסַר עַל נַפְשַׁהּ בְּקִיּוּם:


But, if she took the vow in her husband’s home, or has taken a self-imposed prohibitions upon herself, with an oath,


###### Onkelos Numbers 30:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:12)


וְיִשְׁמַע בַּעֲלַהּ וְיִשְׁתּוֹק לַהּ לָא אַעְדִּי **יָתְהוֹן** וִיקוּמוּן כָּל נִדְרָהָא וְכָל אֱסָרֵי דִּי אֲסָרַת עַל נַפְשַׁהּ יְקוּמוּן:


and her husband heard and remained silent, [and] did not restrain her [**them**], then all her vows shall stand, and all her self-imposed prohibitions shall stand.


###### Onkelos Numbers 30:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:13)


וְאִם בַּטָּלָא יְבַטֵּל יָתְהוֹן בַּעְלַהּ בְּיוֹמָא דִי שְׁמַע כָּל אַפָּקוּת סִפְוָתָהָא לְנִדְרָהָא וּלְאֵסַר נַפְשַׁהּ לָא יְקוּמוּן בַּעֲלַהּ בַּטֵּלִנּוּן **וּמִן קֳדָם** יְיָ יִשְׁתְּבֵיק לַהּ:


But, if her husband annuls them on the day he hears them, then, whatever emanates from her lips regarding her vows and self-imposed prohibitions shall not stand. Her husband has annulled them, and [**from before**] Adonoy will forgive her.


###### Onkelos Numbers 30:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:14)


כָּל נְדַר וְכָל קִיּוּמַת אֵסָר לְסַגָּפָא נְפָשׁ בַּעֲלַהּ יְקַיְּמִנּוּן וּבַעֲלַהּ יְבַטְּלִנּוּן:


Every vow and every oath of self-denial, her husband can [either] let it stand, or her husband can annul it.


###### Onkelos Numbers 30:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:15)


וְאִם מִשְׁתַּק יִשְׁתּוֹק לַהּ בַּעֲלַהּ מִיּוֹם לְיוֹם וִיקַיֵּם יָת כָּל נִדְרָהָא אוֹ יָת כָּל אֱסָרָהָא דִּי עֲלַהּ קַיַּם יָתְהוֹן אֲרֵי שָׁתֵק לַהּ בְּיוֹמָא דִשְׁמָע:


But, if her husband remains silent from one day to the next, then he has upheld all her vows or all her prohibitions that she has assumed. He has upheld them because he remained silent on the day he heard.


###### Onkelos Numbers 30:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:16)


וְאִם בַּטָּלָא יְבַטֵּל יָתְהוֹן בָּתַר דִּשְׁמָע וִיקַבַּל יָת חוֹבַהּ:


If he annuls them after having heard them, he must bear her transgression.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 30:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/30:17)


אִלֵּין קְיָמַיָּא דִּי פַקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה בֵּין גְּבַר לְאִתְּתֵיהּ בֵּין אַבָּא לִבְרַתֵּיהּ בְּרַביוּתָהָא בֵּית אָבוּהָא:


These are the statutes that Adonoy commanded Moshe, regarding a man and his wife, a father and his daughter in her youth, while in her father’s house.



## Chapter 31

###### Onkelos Numbers 31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:1)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying.


###### Onkelos Numbers 31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:2)


אִתְפְּרַע **פֻּרְעֲנוּת** בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מִן מִדְיָנָאֵי בָּתַר כֵּן תִּתְכְּנֵשׁ לְעַמָּיךְ:


“Take revenge for [**retaliate for the evil done to**] Bnei Yisroel against the Midianites, afterward, you shall be gathered to your people (die).”


###### Onkelos Numbers 31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:3)


וּמַלִּיל משֶׁה עִם עַמָּא לְמֵימַר זְרִיזוּ מִנְּכוֹן גֻּבְרִין לְחֵילָא וִיהוֹן עַל מִדְיָן לְמֵיהַב פֻּרְעֲנוּת **דִּין עַמָּא** דַיְיָ בְּמִדְיָן:


Moshe spoke to the people, saying: “Arm men from among yourselves for the army and they will be against Midian, to bring revenge [**for the nation**] of Adonoy against Midian.


###### Onkelos Numbers 31:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:4)


אַלְפָא לְשִׁבְטָא אַלְפָא לְשִׁבְטָא לְכֹל שִׁבְטַיָּא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל תְּשַׁלְּחוּן לְחֵילָא:


One thousand from each tribe, one thousand from each tribe, for all of the tribes of Yisroel, you shall send into the army.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 31:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:5)


**וְאִתְבְּחָרוּ** מֵאַלְפַיָּא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל אַלְפָא לְשִׁבְטָא תְּרֵי עֲשַׂר אַלְפִין מְזָרְזֵי חֵילָא:


They were handed over [**chosen**] from among the thousands of Yisroel, one thousand from each tribe, twelve thousand armed men for the army.


###### Onkelos Numbers 31:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:6)


וּשְׁלַח יָתְהוֹן משֶׁה אַלְפָא לְשִׁבְטָא לְחֵילָא יָתְהוֹן וְיָת פִּינְחָס בַּר אֶלְעָזָר כַּהֲנָא לְחֵילָא וּמָנֵי דְקוּדְשָׁא וַחֲצַצְרַת **יַבֶּבְתָּא** בִּידֵיהּ:


Moshe sent, one thousand from each tribe to the army, them, and Pinchas, son of Elozor the *kohein* to the army, with the sacred vessels and the trumpets [**of wailing**] in his hand.


###### Onkelos Numbers 31:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:7)


וְאִתְחַיָּלוּ עַל מִדְיָן כְּמָא דִי פַּקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה וּקְטָלוּ כָּל דְּכוּרָא:


They mounted a campaign against Midian, as Adonoy had commanded Moshe, and they killed every male.


###### Onkelos Numbers 31:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:8)


וְיָת מַלְכֵי מִדְיָן קְטָלוּ עַל קְטִילֵיהוֹן יָת אֱוִי וְיָת רֶקֶם וְיָת צוּר וְיָת חוּר וְיָת רֶבַע חַמְשָׁא מַלְכֵי מִדְיָן וְיָת בִּלְעָם בַּר בְּעוֹר קְטָלוּ בְחַרְבָּא:


They killed the Midianite kings upon those they had slain: Evi, Rekem, Tzur, Chur and Reva—[the] five kings of Midian. And Bil’am son of Beor they killed with the sword.


###### Onkelos Numbers 31:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:9)


וּשְׁבוֹ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל יָת נְשֵׁי מִדְיָן וְיָת טַפְלְהוֹן וְיָת כָּל בְּעִירְהוֹן וְיָת כָּל גֵּיתֵיהוֹן וְיָת כָּל נִכְסֵיהוֹן בְּזוֹ:


Bnei Yisroel took captive the Midianite women and their children. All their livestock, flocks and wealth they took as booty.


###### Onkelos Numbers 31:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:10)


וְיָת כָּל קִרְוֵיהוֹן בְּמוֹתְבָנֵיהוֹן וְיָת כָּל **בֵּית סִגְדַתְהוֹן** אוֹקִידוּ בְּנוּרָא:


All their residential cities and palaces [**houses of worship**] they set on fire.


###### Onkelos Numbers 31:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:11)


**וּשְׁבוֹ** יָת כָּל עֲדָאָה וְיָת כָּל דְּבַרְתָּא בֶּאֱנָשָׁא וּבִבְעִירָא:


They took [**captured**] all the booty, all that had been seized, of man and livestock,


###### Onkelos Numbers 31:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:12)


וְאַיְתִיּוּ לְוָת משֶׁה וּלְוָת אֶלְעָזָר כַּהֲנָא וּלְוָת כְּנִשְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל יָת שִׁבְיָא וְיָת דְּבַרְתָּא וְיָת עֲדָאָה לְמַשְׁרִיתָא לְמֵישְׁרַיָּא דְמוֹאָב דִּי עַל יַרְדְּנָא דִירֵחוֹ:


And they brought to Moshe and to Elozor the *kohein* and to the entire community of Bnei Yisroel, the captives, and all that had been seized, and the booty, to the camp, at the plains of Moav, which was by the Yardein near Yereicho.


###### Onkelos Numbers 31:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:13)


וּנְפָקוּ משֶׁה וְאֶלְעָזָר כַּהֲנָא וְכָל רַבְרְבֵי כְנִשְׁתָּא לָקֳדָמוּתְהוֹן לְמִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא:


They went out—Moshe, Elozor the *kohein* and the princes of the community—to greet them outside the camp.


###### Onkelos Numbers 31:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:14)


וּרְגֵיז משֶׁה עַל דִּמְמַנָּן עַל חֵילָא רַבָּנֵי אַלְפִין וְרַבָּנֵי מָאֲוָתָא דַּאֲתוֹ מֵחֵיל קְרָבָא:


Moshe was angry with the commanders of the army, at the officers of the thousands and at the officers of the hundreds who had returned from the army service of the war.

And they said to Moshe: “Your servants have taken a census of the soldiers who went out to war [**who are with us**] and not one of us is missing.


###### Onkelos Numbers 31:50

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:50](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:50)


וְקָרֶבְנָא יָת קוּרְבָּנָא דַיְיָ גְּבַר דְּאַשְׁכַּח מַאן דִּדְהָב שִׁירִין וְשִׁבְּכִין עִזְקָן קַדָּשִׁין וּמָחוֹךְ לְכַפָּרָא עַל נַפְשָׁתָנָא קֳדָם יְיָ:


We have therefore brought an offering for Adonoy. Any man who found a gold article—an anklet, or a bracelet, a ring, earring or clasp [has dedicated it for Adonoy] to atone for ourselves before Adonoy.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 31:51

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:51](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:51)


וּנְסִיב משֶׁה וְאֶלְעָזָר כַּהֲנָא יָת דַּהֲבָא מִנְּהוֹן כָּל מַאן דְּעוֹבָדָא:


Moshe and Elozor the *kohein* took the gold from them, every fashioned article.


###### Onkelos Numbers 31:52

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:52](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:52)


וַהֲוָה כָּל דְּהַב אַפְרָשׁוּתָא דִּי אַפְרִישׁוּ קֳדָם יְיָ שִׁתָּא עֲשַׂר אַלְפִין שְׁבַע מְאָה וְחַמְשִׁין סִלְעִין מִן רַבָּנֵי אַלְפִין וּמִן רַבָּנֵי מָאֲוָתָא:


All the gold of the offering that they set apart for Adonoy was 16,750 *shekolim,* from the officers who were over divisions of the thousands and from the officers who were over divisions of the hundreds.


###### Onkelos Numbers 31:53

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:53](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:53)


גַּבְרֵי דְּחֵילָא בָּזוּ גְּבַר לְנַפְשֵׁיהּ:


The men of war, took booty for themselves.


###### Onkelos Numbers 31:54

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:54](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/31:54)


וּנְסִיב משֶׁה וְאֶלְעָזָר כַּהֲנָא יָת דַּהֲבָא מִן רַבָּנֵי אַלְפִין וּמָאֲוָתָא וְאַיְתִיאוּ יָתֵיהּ לְמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא דָּכְרָנָא לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל קֳדָם יְיָ:


Moshe and Elozor the *kohein* took the gold from the officers who were over divisions of the thousands and the hundreds, and they brought it to the Tent of Meeting as a remembrance for Bnei Yisroel before Adonoy.



## Chapter 32

###### Onkelos Numbers 32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:1)


וּבְעִיר סַגִּי הֲוָה לִבְנֵי רְאוּבֵן וְלִבְנֵי גָד תַּקִּיף לַחֲדָא וַחֲזוֹ יָת אֲרַע יַעְזֵר וְיָת אֲרַע גִּלְעָד וְהָא אַתְרָא אֲתַר **כְּשַׁר** לְבֵית בְּעִיר:


Very large herds belonged to the children (tribes) of Reuvein and Gad—[an] extremely large [number]; and they saw the land of Ya’zeir and the land of Gilad, and behold, the region was an area [**suited**] for livestock.


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:2)


וַאֲתוֹ בְנֵי גָד וּבְנֵי רְאוּבֵן וַאֲמָרוּ לְמשֶׁה וּלְאֶלְעָזָר כַּהֲנָא וּלְרַבְרְבֵי כְּנִשְׁתָּא לְמֵימָר:


The tribes of Reuvein and Gad came and spoke to Moshe and to Elozor the *kohein,* and to the princes of the community, saying:


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:3)


**מַכְלֶלְתָּא וּמַלְבֶּשְׁתָּא וְכוּמְרִין וּבֵית נִמְרִין וּבֵית חוּשְׁבָּנָא וּבַעֲלֵי דְבָבָא וְכִימָא וְסִיעַת בֵּית קְבֻרְתָּא דְמשֶׁה** וּבְעוֹן:


“Ataros, Divon, Ya’zeir, [**Machlelta and Malbeshta and Kumrin,**] Nimrah [**Beis Nimrin**], Cheshbon [**Beis Chushbana**], Elalei [**Baalei Debaba**], Sevam [**Kima**], Nevo [**Moshe’s burial place**] and Be’on,


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:4)


אַרְעָא דִּי מְחָא יְיָ יָת יָתְבָהָא קֳדָם כְּנִשְׁתָּא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל אֲרַע **כָּשְׁרָא** לְבֵית בְּעִיר הִיא וּלְעַבְדָּיךְ אִית בְּעִיר:


the land that Adonoy struck down [**its inhabitants**] before Bnei Yisroel, is an area [**suited**] for livestock; and your servants have livestock.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:5)


וַאֲמָרוּ אִם אַשְׁכַחְנָא רַחֲמִין בְּעֵינָיךְ תִּתְיְהִיב יָת אַרְעָא הָדָא לְעַבְדָּיךְ לְאַחֲסָנָא לָא תְעַבְרִנָּנָא יָת יַרְדְּנָא:


They said: “If we have found favor in your eyes, let this land be given to your servants as a possession. Do not bring us over the Yardein.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:6)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לִבְנֵי גָד וְלִבְנֵי רְאוּבֵן הַאֲחֵיכוֹן יֵעֲלוּן לִקְרָבָא וְאַתּוּן תֵּיתְבוּן הָכָא:


Moshe said to the children (tribes) of Gad and Reuvein: “Should your brothers go out to war while you settle here?


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:7)


וּלְמָא תוֹנוּן יָת לִבָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מִלְּמֶעְבַּר לְאַרְעָא דִּיהַב לְהוֹן יְיָ:


Why do you dishearten Bnei Yisroel from crossing over to the land that Adonoy has given them?


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:8)


כְּדֵין עֲבָדוּ אֲבָהַתְכוֹן כַּד שְׁלָחִית יָתְהוֹן **מֵרְקַם גֵּיאָה** לְמֶחֱזֵי יָת אַרְעָא:


This is what your fathers did when I sent them from Kadeish Barnei’a [**Rekam Geyah**] to look at the land.


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:9)


וּסְלִיקוּ עַד נַחְלָא דְאִתְכָּלָא וַחֲזוֹ יָת אַרְעָא וְאוֹנִיאוּ יָת לִבָּא דִּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל **בְּדִיל דְּלָא לְמֵעַל** לְאַרְעָא דִּיהַב לְהוֹן יְיָ:


They went up to Nachal Eshkol and saw the land. They then disheartened Bnei Yisroel from coming [**so as not to go up**] into the land that Adonoy has given them.


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:10)


וּתְקֵף רוּגְזָא דַיְיָ בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא וְקַיֵּים לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy displayed His anger on that day and He swore, saying:


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:11)


אִם יֶחֱזוּן גֻּבְרַיָּא דִּסְלִיקוּ מִמִּצְרַיִם מִבַּר עַשְׂרִין שְׁנִין וּלְעֵלָּא יָת אַרְעָא דִּי קַיֵּמִית לְאַבְרָהָם לְיִצְחָק וּלְיַעֲקֹב אֲרֵי לָא אַשְׁלִימוּ בָּתַר **דַּחַלְתִּי**:


’None of the men who came up from Egypt will see—those who are twenty years and above—the land that I swore to Avraham, Yitzchok and Yaakov, for they did not follow Me fully [**fear Me**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:12)


אֱלָהֵן כָּלֵב בַּר יְפֻנֶּה קְנִזָּאָה וִיהוֹשֻׁעַ בַּר נוּן אֲרֵי אַשְׁלִימוּ בָּתַר **דַּחַלְתָּא** דַיְיָ:


Except for Kaleiv, son of Yefuneh, the Kenizite, and Yehoshua, son of Nun, because they followed [**the fear of**] Adonoy fully.’


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:13)


וּתְקֵף רוּגְזָא דַיְיָ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל וְטָרְדִנּוּן בְּמַדְבְּרָא אַרְבְּעִין שְׁנִין עַד דְּסַף כָּל דָּרָא דַּעֲבִיד דְּבִישׁ **קֳדָם** יְיָ:


Adonoy displayed anger against Yisroel and He made them wander in the wilderness for forty years, until the end (death) of the entire generation that had done evil in the eyes of [**before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:14)


וְהָא קַמְתּוּן חֲלַף אֲבָהַתְכוֹן **תַּלְמִידֵי** גֻּבְרַיָּא חַיָּבַיָא לְאוֹסָפָא עוֹד עַל **תְּקוֹף** רוּגְזָא דַיְיָ לְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


Behold, you have now risen in the place of your fathers as a group [**disciples**] of sinners, to add to the [**mighty**] wrath of Adonoy against Yisroel.


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:15)


אֲרֵי תְתוּבוּן מִבָּתַר **דַּחַלְתֵּיהּ** וְיוֹסֵף עוֹד **לְאַחֲרוּתְהוֹן** בְּמַדְבְּרָא וּתְחַבְּלוּן לְכָל עַמָּא הָדֵין:


If you will turn away from following [**fearing**] Him, He will again place [**delay**] them in the wilderness. and you will destroy this entire nation.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:16)


וְקָרִיבוּ לְוָתֵיהּ וַאֲמָרוּ חַטְרִין דְּעָן נִבְנֵי לִבְעִירָנָא הָכָא וְקִרְוִין לְטַפְלָנָא:


They (the tribes of Reuvein and Gad) approached him (Moshe) and said: “We will build enclosures for our flocks here and cities for our children.


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:17)


וַאֲנַחְנָא נִזְדָּרֵז מַבְעִין קֳדָם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל עַד דִּי נְעֵלִנּוּן לְאַתְרֵיהוֹן וְיֵתְבוּן טַפְלָנָא בְּקִרְוִין כְּרִיכִין מִן קֳדָם יָתְבֵי אַרְעָא:


We will then arm ourselves rapidly, and will be ready to go before Bnei Yisroel until we have brought them to their place. Our children will dwell in fortified cities because of the inhabitants of the land.


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:18)


לָא נְתוּב לְבֵיתָנָא עַד דְּיַחְסְנוּן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל גְּבַר אַחֲסַנְתֵּיהּ:


We will not return to our homes until each person of Bnei Yisroel takes possession of his hereditary property.


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:19)


אֲרֵי לָא נַחְסִין עִמְּהוֹן מֵעִבְרָא לְיַרְדְּנָא וּלְהָלָא אֲרֵי קַבֵּלְנָא אַחֲסַנְתָּנָא לָנָא מֵעִבְרָא לְיַרְדְּנָא מַדִּינְחָא:


But we do not want to take possession with them across the Yardein and beyond, for, our inheritance came to us to the east of the Yardein.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:20)


וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן משֶׁה אִם תַּעְבְּדוּן יָת פִּתְגָּמָא הָדֵין אִם תִּזְדָּרְזוּן קֳדָם **עַמָּא** דַיְיָ לִקְרָבָא:


Moshe said to them: “If you will do this, if you will arm yourselves before [**the nation of**] Adonoy and go forth to the war,


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:21)


וְיַעֲבֵּר לְכוֹן כָּל דִּמְזָרֵז יָת יַרְדְּנָא קֳדָם **עַמָּא** דַיְיָ עַד דִּיתָרֵךְ יָת בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבוֹהִי מִן קֳדָמוֹהִי:


and every armed man will cross the Yardein, before [**the nation of**] Adonoy, until He has driven His enemies before Him,


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:22)


וְתִתְכְּבֵשׁ אַרְעָא קֳדָם **עַמָּא** דַיְיָ וּבָתַר כֵּן תְּתוּבוּן וּתְהוֹן זַכָּאִין מִן **קֳדָם** יְיָ וּמִיִּשְׂרָאֵל וּתְהֵי אַרְעָא הָדָא לְכוֹן לְאַחֲסָנָא קֳדָם יְיָ:


and the land is conquered before [**the nation of**] Adonoy—afterwards you will return. You will then be vindicated from [**before**] Adonoy and from Yisroel, and this land will become yours permanently, before Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:23)


וְאִם לָא תַעַבְּדוּן כֵּן הָא חַבְתּוּן **קֳדָם** יְיָ וְתִדְעוּן חוֹבַתְכוֹן דִּי תַשְׁכַּח יָתְכוֹן:


But if you will not do this, behold, you will have sinned against [**before**] Adonoy and you must realize that your sin will find you!


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:24)


בְּנוּ לְכוֹן קִרְוִין לְטַפְלְכוֹן וְחַטְרִין לְעַנְכוֹן וּדְיִפּוֹק מִפּוּמְכוֹן תַּעַבְּדוּן:


Build for yourselves cities for your children and enclosures for your sheep. But what you have expressed verbally, you must fulfill.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:25)


וַאֲמָרוּ בְנֵי גָד וּבְנֵי רְאוּבֵן לְמשֶׁה לְמֵימָר עַבְדָּיךְ יַעְבְּדוּן כְּמָא דִּי רִבּוֹנִי מְפַקֵּד:


The tribe of Gad and Reuvein said to Moshe saying: “Your servants will do as my master instructs.


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:26)


טַפְלָנָא נְשָׁנָא גֵּיתָנָא וְכָל בְּעִירָנָא יְהוֹן תַּמָּן בְּקִרְוֵי גִלְעָד:


Our children, wives, cattle and all livestock will remain there in the cities of Gil’ad.


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:27)


וְעַבְדָּיךְ יַעְבְּרוּן כָּל דִמְזָרֵז חֵילָא קֳדָם **עַמָּא** דַיְיָ לִקְרָבָא כְּמָא דִי רִבּוֹנִי מְמַלֵּל:


And your servants will cross over—all those in the army—before [**the nation of**] Adonoy, to the war, as my master commands.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:28)


וּפַקִּיד לְהוֹן משֶׁה יָת אֶלְעָזָר כַּהֲנָא וְיָת יְהוֹשֻׁעַ בַּר נוּן וְיָת רֵישֵׁי אֲבָהַת שִׁבְטַיָּא לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


Moshe commanded to them, Elozor the *kohein,* Yehoshua, son of Nun, and the heads of the tribes of Bnei Yisroel.


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:29)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְהוֹן אִם יַעְבְּרוּן בְּנֵי גָד וּבְנֵי רְאוּבֵן עִמְּכוֹן יָת יַרְדְּנָא כָּל דִּמְזָרַז לִקְרָבָא קֳדָם **עַמָּא** דַיְיָ וְתִתְכְּבֵשׁ אַרְעָא קֳדָמֵיכוֹן וְתִתְּנוּן לְהוֹן יָת אַרְעָא דְגִלְעָד לְאַחֲסָנָא:


Moshe said to them: “If the tribe of Gad and Reuvein will cross the Yardein with you, every man that is armed for battle before [**the nation of**] Adonoy, and the land is conquered before you, you shall give them the land of Gil’ad as a permanent possession.


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:30)


וְאִם לָא יַעְבְּרוּן מְזָרְזִין עִמְּכוֹן וְיַחְסְנוּן בֵּינֵיכוֹן בְּאַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן:


But if they do not cross over with you, armed for battle, then they shall take possession among you in the land of Canaan.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:31)


וַאֲתִיבוּ בְנֵי גָד וּבְנֵי רְאוּבֵן לְמֵימָר יָת דִּי מַלִּיל יְיָ לְעַבְדָּיךְ כֵּן נַעְבֵּד:


The tribes of Gad and Reuvein answered, saying: “As Adonoy has spoken to your servants, so shall we do.


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:32)


נַחְנָא נְעִבַר מְזָרְזִין קֳדָם **עַמָּא** דַיְיָ לְאַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן וְעִמָּנָא אֲחוּדַת אַחֲסַנְתָּנָא מֵעִבְרָא לְיַרְדְּנָא:


We will cross over armed for battle before [**the nation of**] Adonoy, to the land of Canaan, and we shall [then] have our permanent inheritance on this side of the Yardein.”


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:33)


וִיהַב לְהוֹן משֶׁה לִבְנֵי גָד וְלִבְנֵי רְאוּבֵן וּלְפַלְגּוּת שִׁבְטָא דִּמְנַשֶּׁה בַר יוֹסֵף יָת מַלְכְּוַת סִיחוֹן מַלְכָּא דֶאֱמוֹרָאָה וְיָת מַלְכְּוַת עוֹג מַלְכָּא **דְמַתְנָן** אַרְעָא לְקִרְוָהָא בִּתְחוּמִין קִרְוֵי אַרְעָא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר:


Moshe gave to them—to the tribe of Gad, to the tribe of Reuvein and to half the tribe of Menashe son of Yoseif—the empire of Sichon, king of the Emorites and the empire of Og, king of the Bashan [**Masnon**], the land along with the cities within their borders, even the cities of the surrounding land.


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:34)


וּבְנוֹ בְנֵי גָד יָת דִּיבוֹן וְיָת עֲטָרוֹת וְיָת עֲרֹעֵר:


The sons of Gad built up Divon, Ataros, Aro’eir,


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:35)


וְיָת עַטְרוֹת שׁוֹפָן וְיָת יַעְזֵר **וְרָמָתָא**:


Atros Shofan, Yazeir, Yogbehah [**the Heights**],


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:36)


וְיָת בֵּית נִמְרָה וְיָת בֵּית הָרָן קִרְוִין כְּרִיכָן וְחַטְרִין דְּעָן:


Beis Nimrah, and Beis Haran as fortified cities and sheep pens.


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:37)


וּבְנֵי רְאוּבֵן בְּנוֹ יָת חֶשׁבּוֹן וְיָת אֶלְעָלֵא וְיָת קִרְיָתָיִם:


The tribe of Reuvein built up Cheshbon, El’alei, Kiryasayim,


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:38)


וְיָת נְבוֹ וְיָת בַּעַל מְעוֹן מַקְּפַן שְׁמָהָן וְיָת שִׂבְמָה וּקְרוֹ בִשְׁמָהָן יָת שְׁמָהַת קִרְוַיָּא דִּי בְנוֹ:


Nevo, Ba’al Me’on—their names having been changed, and Sivmah. They retained the names of the other cities that they built.


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:39)


וַאֲזָלוּ בְּנֵי מָכִיר בַּר מְנַשֶּׁה לְגִלְעָד וְכִבְשׁוּהָ וְתָרִיךְ יָת אֱמוֹרָאָה דִי בַהּ:


The sons of Machir, son of Menashe, went to Gil’ad and captured it, and drove out the Amorites who were in it.


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:40)


וִיהַב משֶׁה יָת גִּלְעָד לְמָכִיר בַּר מְנַשֶּׁה וִיתֵב בַּהּ:


Moshe gave the Gil’ad to Machir, son of Menashe, and he settled in it.


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:41)


וְיָאִיר בַּר מְנַשֶּׁה אֲזַל וּכְבַשׁ יָת כַּפְרָנֵיהוֹן וּקְרָא יָתְהֶן כַּפְרָנֵי יָאִיר:


Yair, son of Menashe, went and conquered their villages and called them Chavos Yair.


###### Onkelos Numbers 32:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/32:42)


וְנֹבַח אֲזַל וּכְבַשׁ יָת קְנַת וְיָת כַּפְרָנָהָא וּקְרָא לַהּ נֹבַח בִּשְׁמֵיהּ:


Novach went and conquered Kenas and its suburbs, and called it Novach, after his own name.



## Chapter 33

###### Onkelos Numbers 33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:1)


אִלֵּין מַטְּלָנֵי בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל דִּי נְפָקוּ מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם לְחֵילֵיהוֹן בִּידָא דְמשֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן:


These are the journeys of Bnei Yisroel who went out of Egypt in organized groups under the leadership of Moshe and Aharon.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:2)


וּכְתַב משֶׁה יָת מַפְּקָנֵיהוֹן לְמַטְּלָנֵיהוֹן עַל מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ וְאִלֵּין מַטְּלָנֵיהוֹן לְמַפְּקָנֵיהוֹן:


Moshe recorded their departures for their journeys according to Adonoy’s command; these were the departure for their journeys.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:3)


וּנְטָלוּ מְרַעְמְסֵס בְּיַרְחָא קַדְמָאָה בְּחַמְשָׁא עַשְׂרָא יוֹמָא לְיַרְחָא קַדְמָאָה מִבָּתַר יוֹמָא דְפִּסְחָא נְפָקוּ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל **בְּרֵישׁ גְּלֵי** לְעֵינֵי כָּל מִצְרָאֵי:


They journeyed from Ra’meses in the first month—on the fifteenth day of the first month—on the day after the [Korban] *Pesach,* Bnei Yisroel left triumphantly [**publicly**] before the eyes of all Egyptians.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:4)


וּמִצְרָאֵי מְקַבְּרִין יָת דִּי קְטַל יְיָ בְּהוֹן כָּל בּוּכְרָא **וּבְטַעֲוַתְהוֹן** עֲבַד יְיָ דִּינִין:


Egypt was burying that which Adonoy had struck among them—all their firstborn—and on their idols [**abominations**] Adonoy had inflicted punishments.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:5)


וּנְטָלוּ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מֵרַעְמְסֵס וּשְׁרוֹ בְּסֻכֹּת:


Bnei Yisroel journeyed from Ra’meses and camped in Succos.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:6)


וּנְטָלוּ מִסֻּכֹּת וּשְׁרוֹ בְאֵתָם דִּי בִּסְטַר מַדְבְּרָא:


They journeyed from Succos and camped in Eisom which is on the edge of the wilderness.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:7)


וּנְטָלוּ מֵאֵתָם וְתָב עַל פּוּם חִירָתָא דִּי קֳדָם בְּעֵל צְפוֹן וּשְׁרוֹ קֳדָם מִגְדֹּל:


They journeyed from Eisom and turned back to Pi Hachiros facing Ba’al Tzefon, and they camped by Migdol.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:8)


וּנְטָלוּ מִן **פּוּם** חִירָתָא וַעֲבָרוּ בְּגוֹ יַמָּא לְמַדְבְּרָא וַאֲזָלוּ מַהֲלַךְ תְּלָתָא יוֹמִין בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְאֵתָם וּשְׁרוֹ בְּמָרָה:


They journeyed from before [**Pi**] Hachiros and crossed amidst the sea toward the wilderness. They traveled for three days in the wilderness of Eisom, and camped in Moroh.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:9)


וּנְטָלוּ מִמָּרָה וַאֲתוֹ לְאֵילִם וּבְאֵילִם תְּרֵי עֲשַׂר מַבּוּעִין דְּמַיִּן וְשַׁבְעִין דִּקְלִין וּשְׁרוֹ תַמָּן:


They journeyed from Moroh and came to Eilim. In Eilim were twelve springs of water and seventy palm trees, and they camped there.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:10)


וּנְטָלוּ מֵאֵילִם וּשְׁרוֹ עַל יַמָּא דְסוּף:


They journeyed from Eilim and camped by the Sea of Reeds.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:11)


וּנְטָלוּ מִיַּמָּא דְסוּף וּשְׁרוֹ בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְסִין:


They journeyed from the Sea of Reeds and camped in the wilderness of Sin.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:12)


וּנְטָלוּ מִמַּדְבְּרָא דְסִין וּשְׁרוֹ בְּדָפְקָה:


They journeyed from the wilderness of Sin and camped in Dofkoh.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:13)


וּנְטָלוּ מִדָּפְקָה וּשְׁרוֹ בְּאָלוּשׁ:


They journeyed from Dofkoh and camped in Alush.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:14)


וּנְטָלוּ מֵאָלוּשׁ וּשְׁרוֹ בִּרְפִידִם וְלָא הֲוָה תַמָּן מַיָּא לְעַמָּא לְמִשְׁתֵּי:


They journeyed from Alush and camped in Refidim, [but] there was no water there for the people to drink.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:15)


וּנְטָלוּ מֵרְפִידִם וּשְׁרוֹ בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְסִינָי:


They journeyed from Refidim and camped in the wilderness of Sinai.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:16)


וּנְטָלוּ מִמַּדְבְּרָא דְסִינָי וּשְׁרוֹ **בְּקִבְרֵי דִמְשַׁאֲלֵי**:


They journeyed from the wilderness of Sinai and camped in Kivros Hata’avoh [**Graves of the Demanders**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:17)

They journeyed from Kivros Hata’avoh [**Graves of the Demanders**] and camped in Chatzeiros.

They journeyed from Avronoh and camped in Etzyon Gover.

They journeyed from Etzyon Gover and camped in the wilderness of Sin, which is Kodeish [**Rekom**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:37

They journeyed from Kodeish [**Rekom**] and camped in Hor Hohor, at the edge of the land of Edom.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:38)


וּסְלֵק אַהֲרֹן כַּהֲנָא לְהוֹר טוּרָא עַל מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ וּמִית תַּמָּן בִּשְׁנַת אַרְבְּעִין לְמֵפַּק בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם בְּיַרְחָא חֲמִשָּׁאָה בְּחַד לְיַרְחָא:


Then Aharon the *kohein* ascended Hor Hohor by the word of Adonoy and died there; in the fortieth year of Bnei Yisroel’s exodus from Egypt, on the first day of the fifth month.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:39)


וְאַהֲרֹן בַּר מְאָה וְעַשְׂרִין וּתְלַת שְׁנִין כַּד מִית בְּהוֹר טוּרָא:


Aharon was 123 years old at his death on Hor Hohor.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:40)


וּשְׁמַע כְּנַעֲנָאָה מַלְכָּא דַעֲרָד וְהוּא יָתֵב בִּדְרוֹמָא בְּאַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן בְּמֵיתֵי בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


The Canaanite, the king of Arod heard that Bnei Yisroel were approaching—he dwelt in the south of the land of Canaan.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:41)


וּנְטָלוּ מֵהוֹר טוּרָא וּשְׁרוֹ בְּצַלְמוֹנָה:


They journeyed from Hor Hohor and camped in Tzalmonoh.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:42)


וּנְטָלוּ מִצַּלְמוֹנָה וּשְׁרוֹ בְּפוּנֹן:


They journeyed from Tzalmonoh and camped in Punon.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:43)


וּנְטָלוּ מִפּוּנֹן וּשְׁרוֹ בְּאֹבוֹת:


They journeyed from Punon and camped in Ovos.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:44)


וּנְטָלוּ מֵאֹבוֹת וּשְׁרוֹ בִּמְגִזַּת עֲבָרָאֵי בִּתְחוּם מוֹאָב:


They journeyed from Ovos and camped at the ruins of Avorim on Moav’s border.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:45)


וּנְטָלוּ מִמְּגִזָּתָא וּשְׁרוֹ בְּדִיבוֹן גָד:


They journeyed from the ruins and camped in Divon Gad.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:46

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:46](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:46)


וּנְטָלוּ מִדִּיבוֹן גָּד וּשְׁרוֹ בְּעַלְמוֹן דִּבְלָתָיְמָה:


They journeyed from Divon Gad and camped in Almon Divlasayim.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:47

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:47](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:47)


וּנְטָלוּ מֵעַלְמוֹן דִבְלָתָימָה וּשְׁרוֹ בְּטוּרֵי דַעֲבָרָאֵי דִּקֳדָם נְבוֹ:


They journeyed from Almon Divlasayim and camped in the Avorim Mountains before Nevo.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:48

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:48](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:48)


וּנְטָלוּ מִטּוּרֵי דַעֲבָרָאֵי וּשְׁרוֹ בְּמֵישְׁרַיָּא דְמוֹאָב עַל יַרְדְּנָא דִירֵחוֹ:


They journeyed from the Avorim Mountains and camped in the plains of Moav along the Yardein near Yereicho.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:49

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:49](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:49)


וּשְׁרוֹ עַל יַרְדְּנָא מִבֵּית יְשִׁימוֹת עַד מֵישַׁר שִׁטִּין בְּמֵישְׁרַיָּא דְמוֹאָב:


They camped along the Yardein, from Beis Yeshimos until Aveil Hasheetim, in the plains of Moav.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:50

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:50](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:50)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה בְּמֵישְׁרַיָּא דְמוֹאָב עַל יַרְדְּנָא דִירֵחוֹ לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe in the plains of Moav along the Yardein [near] Yereicho, saying:


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:51

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:51](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:51)


מַלֵּל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן אֲרֵי אַתּוּן עָבְרִין יָת יַרְדְּנָא לְאַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן:


“Speak to Bnei Yisroel and say to them: ‘When you cross the Yardein to the land of Canaan,


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:52

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:52](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:52)


וּתְתָרְכוּן יָת כָּל יָתְבֵי אַרְעָא מִן קֳדָמֵיכוֹן וְתֵבְדוּן יָת כָּל בֵּית סִגְדַּתְהוֹן וְיָת כָּל צַלְמֵי מַתְּכַתְהוֹן תֵּבְדוּן וְיָת כָּל בָּמַתְהוֹן תְּשֵׁצוּן:


you must drive out all of the inhabitants of the land before you. You must destroy all their carved stones, and demolish all their cast metal idols and tear down all their altars.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:53

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:53](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:53)


וּתְתָרְכוּן יָת יָתְבֵי אַרְעָא וְתֵיתְבוּן בַּהּ אֲרֵי לְכוֹן יְהָבִית יָת אַרְעָא לְמֵירַת יָתָהּ:


Drive out [the inhabitants of] the land and settle in it, because I have given this land to you to possess.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:54

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:54](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:54)


וְתַחְסְנוּן יָת אַרְעָא בְעַדְבָא לְזַרְעֲיַתְכוֹן לְסַגִּיאֵי תַּסְגּוּן יָת אַחֲסַנְתְּהוֹן וְלִזְעִירֵי תַּזְעִירוּן יָת אַחֲסַנְתְּהוֹן לִדְיִפּוֹק לֵיהּ תַּמָּן עַדְבָא דִּי לֵיהּ יְהֵי לְשִׁבְטֵי אֲבָהַתְכוֹן תַּחְסְנוּן:


You shall give the land as an inheritance to your families by means of a lottery; to the large [family] you shall increase its portion, to the small [family] you shall decrease its portion. To whomever the lottery [system] ordains, it shall be his; according to your paternal tribes shall you inherit.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:55

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:55](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:55)


וְאִם לָא תְתָרְכוּן יָת יָתְבֵי אַרְעָא מִן קֳדָמֵיכוֹן וִיהֵי דִּי תַשְׁאֲרוּן מִנְּהוֹן **לְסִיעַן נָטְלַן זְיַן לָקִבְלֵיכוֹן וּלְמַשִּׁרְיַן מַקְפַּנְכוֹן** וִיעִיקוּן לְכוֹן עַל אַרְעָא דִּי אַתּוּן יָתְבִין בַּהּ:


But if you do not drive out the inhabitants of the land before you, those whom you will leave over will be spikes in your eyes and thorns in your sides [**armed groups coming against you and battalions surrounding you**], and they will persecute you upon the land in which you settle.


###### Onkelos Numbers 33:56

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:56](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/33:56)


וִיהֵי כְּמָא דִי חֲשָׁבִית לְמֶעְבַּד לְהוֹן אַעֲבֵּד לְכוֹן:


And it shall be that what I had intended to do to them I will do to you.’”



## Chapter 34

###### Onkelos Numbers 34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/34:1)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


###### Onkelos Numbers 34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/34:2)


פַּקֵּד יָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן אֲרֵי אַתּוּן עָלִין לְאַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן דָּא אַרְעָא דִּי **תִתְפְּלֵג** לְכוֹן בְּאַחֲסָנָא אַרְעָא דִכְנַעַן לִתְחוּמָהָא:


“Instruct Bnei Yisroel and say to them: ‘When you come to the land of Canaan, this is the land which will fall to [**divided for**] you as hereditary property, the land of Canaan according to its borders.


###### Onkelos Numbers 34:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/34:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/34:3)


וִיהֵי לְכוֹן רוּחַ דָּרוֹמָא מִמַּדְבְּרָא דְצִן עַל **תְּחוּמֵי** אֱדוֹם וִיהֵי לְכוֹן תְּחוּם דָּרוֹמָא מִסְּיָפֵי יַמָּא דְמִלְחָא קִדּוּמָא:


Your southern flank shall be from the Tzin wilderness along [the land of] [**the borders of**] Edom; the southern border shall be from the edge of the Salt Sea to the east.


###### Onkelos Numbers 34:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/34:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/34:4)


וְיַסְחַר לְכוֹן תְּחוּמָא מִדְּרוֹמָא **לְמַסְקָנָא** דְעַקְרַבִּים וִיעִבַר לְצִן וִיהוֹן מַפְּקָנוֹהִי מִדְּרוֹמָא **לִרְקַם גֵּיאָה** וְיִפּוֹק לַחֲצַר אַדָּר וִיעִבַר לְעַצְמוֹן:


The border then turns south of Maaleh [**the heights of**] Akrabim passing toward Tzin; and its outskirts shall be south of Kadeish Barne’a [**Rekam Geyoh**], and then it shall extend to Chatzar Adar and continue toward Atzmon.


###### Onkelos Numbers 34:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/34:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/34:5)


וְיַסְחַר תְּחוּמָא מֵעַצְמוֹן לְנַחֲלָא דְמִצְרָיִם וִיהוֹן מַפְּקָנוֹהִי לְיַמָּא:


The border turns from Atzmon toward the Egyptian Estuary and its outskirts shall reach the sea.

As for the western border; it shall be the border of the Great Sea, this shall be your western border.

This shall be your northern border; from the Great Sea turn yourselves to Hor Hahar.

From Hor Hahar turn to [**direct yourselves toward**] Levo Chamas and the outskirts of the border shall be toward Tzedad.

The leader for the tribe of Asher, Achihud, son of Shlomi.

The leader for the tribe of Naftali, Pedah’eil, son of Amihud.”

These are the ones whom Adonoy commanded to apportion the inheritance to Bnei Yisroel, in the land of Canaan.



## Chapter 35

###### Onkelos Numbers 35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:1)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה בְּמֵישְׁרַיָּא דְמוֹאָב עַל יַרְדְּנָא דִירֵחוֹ לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe in the plains of Moav by the Yardein near Yereicho, saying:


###### Onkelos Numbers 35:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:2)


פַּקֵּד יָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְיִתְּנוּן לְלֵוָאֵי מֵאַחֲסָנַת אַחוּדַתְהוֹן קִרְוִין לְמִתָּב וְרֶוַח לְקִרְוַיָּא סַחֲרָנֵיהוֹן תִּתְּנוּן לְלֵוָאֵי:


“Instruct Bnei Yisroel and have them give to the Levites from their hereditary possession, cities in which to settle; and open space for the cities all around you shall give to the Levites.


###### Onkelos Numbers 35:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:3)


וִיהוֹן קִרְוַיָּא לְהוֹן לְמִתָּב וְרַוְחֵיהוֹן יְהוֹן לִבְעִירְהוֹן וּלְקִנְיָנֵיהוֹן וּלְכָל חֵיוַתְהוֹן:


These cities shall be theirs for dwelling, and their open space shall be for their animals, their property and all of their necessities to live.


###### Onkelos Numbers 35:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:4)


וּרְוָחֵי קִרְוַיָּא דִּי תִתְּנוּן לְלֵוָאֵי מִכּוֹתֵל קַרְתָּא וּלְבָרָא אֲלַף אַמִּין סְחוֹר סְחוֹר:


The open space for the cities that you shall give the Levites [shall extend] from the walls of the city and outward a thousand *amo¯s* all around.


###### Onkelos Numbers 35:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:5)


וְתִמְשְׁחוּן מִבָּרָא לְקַרְתָּא יָת רוּחַ קִדּוּמָא תְּרֵין אַלְפִין בְּאַמִּין וְיָת רוּחַ דְּרוֹמָא תְּרֵין אַלְפִין בְּאַמִּין וְיָת רוּחַ מַעַרְבָא תְּרֵין אַלְפִין בְּאַמִּין וְיָת רוּחַ צִפּוּנָא תְּרֵין אַלְפִין בְּאַמִּין וְקַרְתָּא בִמְצִיעָא דֵּין יְהֵי לְהוֹן רְוָחֵי קִרְוַיָּא:


You shall measure from outside the city two thousand *amo¯s* on the eastern side, two thousand *amo¯s* on the southern side, two thousand *amo¯s* on the western side, and two thousand *amo¯s* on the northern side; with the city in the center. This shall be the open spaces for the cities.


###### Onkelos Numbers 35:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:6)


וְיָת קִרְוַיָּא דִּי תִתְּנוּן לְלֵוָאֵי יָת שִׁית קִרְוֵי שֵׁזָבוּתָא דִּי תִתְּנוּן לְמֵעֲרַק תַּמָּן קָטוֹלָא וַעֲלֵיהוֹן תִּתְּנוּן אַרְבְּעִין וְתַרְתֵּין קִרְוִין:


Along with the cities that you shall give the Levites shall be the six refuge cities which you shall provide [as places] for a murderer to flee; and in addition to them, you shall provide forty-two cities.


###### Onkelos Numbers 35:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:7)


כָּל קִרְוַיָּא דִּי תִתְּנוּן לְלֵוָאֵי אַרְבְּעִין וְתַמְנֵי קִרְוִין יָתְהֶן וְיָת רְוָחֵיהֶן:


The total number of cities that you shall give to the Levites is forty eight cities, with their open spaces.


###### Onkelos Numbers 35:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:8)


וְקִרְוַיָּא דִּי תִתְּנוּן מֵאֲחוּדַת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מִן סַגִּיאֵי תַּסְגּוּן וּמִן זְעִירֵי תַּזְעִירוּן גְּבַר כְּפוּם אַחֲסַנְתֵּיהּ דִּי יַחְסְנוּן יִתֵּן מִקִּרְווֹהִי לְלֵוָאֵי:


The cities that you shall give, [shall be] from the possession of Bnei Yisroel; take more from a larger [holding], and less from a smaller [holding], each person, according to his inheritance, shall give of his cities to the Levites.”

“Speak to Bnei Yisroel and say to them: ‘When you cross the Yardein into the land of Canaan,


###### Onkelos Numbers 35:11

you shall provide cities for yourselves, which shall serve as refuge cities for you, so that a murderer can flee there, one who accidentally killed a person.

But if suddenly, without enmity, he pushed him or threw any object at him not deliberately,

Then the congregation shall judge between the assailant and the blood avenger, abiding by these laws.

The congregation shall protect the murderer from the blood avenger, and return him to his refuge city where he had fled, and he must remain there until the death of the *Kohein Gadol* who was anointed with the sacred oil.

But if the murderer goes outside of the border of his refuge city where he had fled,

וְיַשְׁכַּח יָתֵיהּ גָּאֵל דְּמָא מִבָּרָא לִתְחוּם קִרְיַת שֵׁזָבוּתֵיהּ וְיִקְטוֹל גָּאֵל דְּמָא יָת קָטוֹלָא לֵית לֵיהּ דְּמָא:


and the blood avenger finds him outside the border of his refuge city, and the blood avenger kills the murderer, he has no blood.

אֲרֵי בְקִרְיַת שֵׁזָבוּתֵיהּ יִתֵּב עַד דִּימוּת כַּהֲנָא רַבָּא וּבָתַר דִּימוּת כַּהֲנָא רַבָּא יְתוּב קָטוֹלָא לַאֲרַע אַחֲסַנְתֵּיהּ:


For he (the killer) must remain in his refuge city until the death of the *Kohein Gadol.* After the death of the *Kohein Gadol* the murderer may return to the land of his possession.


###### Onkelos Numbers 35:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:29)


וִיהוֹן אִלֵּין לְכוֹן לִגְזֵרַת דִּין לְדָרֵיכוֹן בְּכָל מוֹתְבָנֵיכוֹן:


These [rules] shall be for you the law of justice for all your descendants in all your dwelling places.


###### Onkelos Numbers 35:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:30)


כָּל דְּיִקְטוֹל נַפְשָׁא לְפוּם סַהֲדִין יִקְטוֹל יָת קָטוֹלָא וְסָהִיד חַד לָא יַסְהֵד בֶּאֱנָשׁ לְמִקְטָל:


If anyone kills a person, only upon the testimony of witnesses shall the murderer be killed. But one witness may not testify against a person, in a case of capital punishment.


###### Onkelos Numbers 35:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:31)


וְלָא תְקַבְּלוּן **מָמוֹן** עַל אֱנַשׁ קָטוֹל דִּי הוּא חַיָּב לְמִקְטָל אֲרֵי אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵל:


And do not accept atonement [**monetary**] compensation for the life of a murderer who is wicked to incur the death penalty, for he must be executed.


###### Onkelos Numbers 35:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:32)


וְלָא תְקַבְּלוּן מָמוֹן לְמֵעֲרוֹק לְקִרְיַת שֵׁזָבוּתֵיהּ לְמִתּוּב לְמִתַּב בְּאַרְעָא עַד דִּימוּת כַּהֲנָא:


And do not accept atonement compensation for one who has fled to his refuge city, to allow him to return to live in the land, before the death of the *Kohein* (*Gadol*).


###### Onkelos Numbers 35:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:33)


וְלָא **תְחַיְּבוּן** יָת אַרְעָא דִּי אַתּוּן בַּהּ אֲרֵי דְמָא הוּא מְחַיַּב יָת אַרְעָא וּלְאַרְעָא לָא מִתְכַּפֵּר עַל דַּם **זַכַּאי** דְּאִתָּשַׁד בַּהּ אֶלָּהֵן בְּדַם אַשְׁדֵּיהּ:


Do not pollute [**incur guilt on**] the land in which you live, for, the blood pollutes the land, and for the land there can be no atonement for the [**innocent**] blood that was spilled in it, except through the blood of the one who spilled it.


###### Onkelos Numbers 35:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/35:34)


וְלָא תְסָאֲבוּן יָת אַרְעָא דִּי אַתּוּן יָתְבִין בַּהּ דִּי **שְׁכִינְתִּי** שָׁרְיָא בְּגַוָּהּ אֲרֵי אֲנָא יְיָ **דִּשְׁכִנְתִּי** שָׁרְיָא בְּגוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


You must not defile the land in which you live, wherein I [**My Shechinah**] reside[**s**], for, I, Adonoy, [**whose Shechinah**] dwell[**s**] among Bnei Yisroel.’”



## Chapter 36

###### Onkelos Numbers 36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:1)


וְקָרִיבוּ רֵישֵׁי אֲבָהָתָא לְזַרְעִית בְּנֵי גִּלְעָד בַּר מָכִיר בַּר מְנַשֶּׁה מִזַּרְעֲיַת בְּנֵי יוֹסֵף וּמַלִּילוּ קֳדָם משֶׁה וְקֳדָם רַבְרְבַיָּא רֵישֵׁי אֲבָהָתָא לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


The paternal leaders approached—for the children of Gilad, son of Machir, son of Menashe, of the families of Yoseif’s sons—and they spoke before Moshe and the leaders who were the paternal heads of Bnei Yisroel.


###### Onkelos Numbers 36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:2)


וַאֲמָרוּ יָת רִבּוֹנִי פַּקִּיד יְיָ לְמִתַּן יָת אַרְעָא בְּאַחֲסָנָא בְּעַדְבָא לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְרִבּוֹנִי אִתְפַּקַּד **בְּמֵמְרָא** דַיְיָ לְמִתַּן יָת אַחֲסָנַת צְלָפְחָד אָחוּנָא לִבְנָתֵיהּ:


They said: “Adonoy commanded our master to give the land as an inheritance, through the casting of lots, to Bnei Yisroel, and our master was commanded by [**the word of**] Adonoy to give the inheritance of our brother Tzelofchad to his daughters.


###### Onkelos Numbers 36:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:3)


וִיהֶוְיַן לְחַד מִבְּנֵי שִׁבְטַיָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לִנְשִׁין **וְתִתְמְנַע** אַחֲסַנְתְּהוֹן מֵאַחֲסָנַת אֲבְהָתָנָא וְתִתּוֹסַף עַל אַחֲסָנַת שִׁבְטַיָּא דִּי יֶהֶוְיָן לְהוֹן וּמֵעֲדַב אַחֲסַנְתָּא יִתִּמְנָע:


But if they marry a member of another tribe of Bnei Yisroel, their inheritance will be diminished [**subtracted**] from our father’s inheritance, and it will be added to the inheritance of the tribe into which they will marry, and from the lot of our inheritance it will be subtracted.


###### Onkelos Numbers 36:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:4)


וְאִם יְהֵי יוֹבְלָא לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְתִתּוֹסַף אַחֲסַנְתְּהוֹן עַל אַחֲסָנַת שִׁבְטָא דִּי תְהֶוְיַן לְהוֹן וּמֵאַחֲסָנַת שִׁבְטָא דַאֲבְהָתָנָא יִתִּמְנַע אַחֲסַנְתְּהֶן:


And even when the *yoveil* occurs for Bnei Yisroel, their inheritance will be added to the inheritance of the tribe into which they will marry, and from the inheritance of our fathers’ tribe, their inheritance will be subtracted.


###### Onkelos Numbers 36:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:5)


וּפַקִּיד משֶׁה יָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל עַל מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ לְמֵימָר יָאוּת שִׁבְטָא דִבְנֵי יוֹסֵף מְמַלְּלִין:


Moshe instructed Bnei Yisroel according to command of Adony, saying: “Correctly are the descendants of Yoseif’s tribe speaking.


###### Onkelos Numbers 36:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:6)


דֵּין פִּתְגָּמָא דִּי פַקִּיד יְיָ לִבְנַת צְלָפְחָד לְמֵימַר לִדְתַקִּין בְּעֵינֵיהוֹן תְּהֶוְיַן לִנְשִׁין בְּרַם לְזַרְעִית שִׁבְטָא דַאֲבוּהֶן תְּהֶוְיַן לִנְשִׁין:


This is the word that Adonoy has commanded regarding Tzelofchad’s daughters: ‘Let them marry whom they think best, as long as they marry within their tribe of their father.


###### Onkelos Numbers 36:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:7)


וְלָא תַסְחַר אַחֲסָנָא לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מִשִּׁבְטָא לְשִׁבְטָא אֲרֵי גְבַר בְּאַחֲסָנַת שִׁבְטָא דַאֲבָהָתוֹהִי יִדַּבְּקוּן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


Thereby, there will be no transfer of inheritance of Bnei Yisroel from tribe to tribe but each person to the inheritance of his father’s tribe, shall Bnei Yisroel remain attached.


###### Onkelos Numbers 36:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:8)


וְכָל בְּרַתָּא יָרְתַת אַחֲסָנָא מִשִּׁבְטַיָּא דִּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְחַד מִזַּרְעִית שִׁבְטָא דַאֲבוּהָא תְּהֵי לְאִנְתּוּ בְּדִיל דִּי יֵרְתוּן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל גְּבַר אַחֲסָנַת אֲבָהָתוֹהִי:


Every daughter who inherits property among the tribes of Bnei Yisroel to a member of her father’s tribe shall she marry, so that each one of Bnei Yisroel will inherit the property of his fathers.


###### Onkelos Numbers 36:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:9)


וְלָא תַסְחַר אַחֲסָנָא מִשִּׁבְטָא לְשִׁבְטָא אוֹחֲרָנָא אֲרֵי גְבַר בְּאַחֲסַנְתֵּיהּ יִדְּבְּקוּן שִׁבְטַיָּא דִּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


And no inheritance will be transferred from one tribe to another tribe; for, through every man with his own inheritance, will the tribes of Bnei Yisroel be attached.


###### Onkelos Numbers 36:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:10)


כְּמָא דִי פַּקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה כֵּן עֲבָדוּ בְּנַת צְלָפְחָד:


Exactly as Adonoy commanded Moshe, so did Tzelofchad’s daughters.


###### Onkelos Numbers 36:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:11)


וַהֲוָאָה מַחְלָה תִרְצָה וְחָגְלָה וּמִלְכָּה וְנֹעָה בְּנַת צְלָפְחָד **לִבְנֵי אֲחֵי אֲבוּהֶן** לִנְשִׁין:


And they were—Machlah, Sirtzah, Choglah, Milkah and No’ah, the daughters of Tzelofchad—wives to their cousins [**the sons of their father’s brothers**].


###### Onkelos Numbers 36:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:12)


מִזַרְעֲיַת בְּנֵי מְנַשֶּׁה בַר יוֹסֵף הֲוָאָה לִנְשִׁין וַהֲוַת אַחֲסַנְתְּהֶן עַל שִׁבְטָא זַרְעִית אַבוּהֶן:


From the families of Menashe, son of Yoseif, they married, and their inheritance remained with the tribe of their father’s family.


###### Onkelos Numbers 36:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Numbers/r/36:13)


אִלֵּין פִּקּוֹדַיָּא וְדִינַיָּא דִּי פַקִּיד יְיָ בִּידָא דְמשֶׁה לְוָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּמֵישְׁרַיָּא דְמוֹאָב עַל יַרְדְּנָא דִירֵחוֹ:


These are the commandments and the laws that Adonoy commanded Bnei Yisroel through Moshe, in the plains of Moav at the Yardein near Yereicho. **Chazak**
